144题分块背诵版

完整参考答案按原有逻辑分块。先理解中文，再记英文开头提示，最后遮住答案写出整块。提示句不能替代完整答案；条件、例子与题干要求的数量都要保留。

26天逐日路线 · 详细教学题库

T001 · Top-down 与 bottom-up · 128词

题目：Compare top-down and bottom-up approaches to network design.

比较网络设计中的自顶向下与自底向上方法。

先记回答顺序

  1. 先对比起点：业务需求或技术产品
  2. 说明需求怎样引导架构与设备选择
  3. 比较需求匹配的好处与先买设备的风险
  4. 用校园例子说明，并保留现有设备约束与反馈

第1块

Top-down先看组织目标、用户和应用需要，再逐步确定服务、流量、架构，最后选具体技术设备。

起句提示：A top-down approach starts with the organization’s goals and the needs of users and applications.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A top-down approach starts with the organization’s goals and the needs of users and applications. The designer develops service requirements, traffic flows and architecture before selecting detailed technologies and equipment. This helps the network support the business.

第2块

Bottom-up先从技术、连接结构或产品出发；熟悉设备可使开始较方便，但可能不符合真实需求。

起句提示：A bottom-up approach begins with technologies, topologies or products.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A bottom-up approach begins with technologies, topologies or products. It can be convenient when a designer already knows particular equipment, but it may produce a network that fails to meet the real requirements.

第3块

先买更快交换机未必解决外部链路或服务器慢的问题。应先查需求和瓶颈，同时考虑现有设备及技术限制，允许回头修改。

起句提示：For example, buying faster switches first will not solve every application problem.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

For example, buying faster switches first will not solve every application problem. The actual problem may be delay on an external link or poor server performance. A top-down process investigates that need first. Existing equipment and technical limits still matter, so the process allows feedback. The key is to justify technology choices through requirements rather than personal preference.

不能丢的条件

top-down 不表示完全忽略现有设备。

闭卷验收

先口头用中文解释，再手写或打出英文；对照上面的回答顺序，逐点检查。再做题库中的变式，确认能按新问法重组。

T132 · Access、distribution、core 的职责 · 142词

题目：Explain the roles of the access, distribution and core layers in a hierarchical network. How does this structure help design?

解释三层职责，并说明层次结构的设计价值。

先记回答顺序

  1. 列access、distribution、core并分别说明连接、汇聚、骨干传输
  2. 沿同一校园路径说明层之间如何承接
  3. 解释复杂度、增长、故障隔离等设计收益
  4. 保留功能层不等于固定设备数的条件

第1块

Access把用户和终端接入网络，可负责本地转发、端口控制和流量初步分类。

起句提示：The access layer connects users and end devices to the network.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The access layer connects users and end devices to the network. It can provide local switching, port access controls and initial traffic classification. Its design should support the availability and service needs of attached users.

第2块

Distribution汇聚接入流量并应用跨区域政策；core负责主要网络区域之间的运输，重视可靠、可用、低延迟与可管理。

起句提示：The distribution layer brings together traffic from access networks.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The distribution layer brings together traffic from access networks. It provides a place to organize connectivity and apply policies between access areas and the backbone. The core provides transport between major parts of the network. Important goals include reliability, availability, low delay and manageable operation.

第3块

分工便于扩容和定位故障。校园可按教室、楼宇、楼间连接来理解；功能层不等于必须买三套独立设备。

起句提示：Separating these roles can reduce complexity and make growth and fault isolation easier.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Separating these roles can reduce complexity and make growth and fault isolation easier. For example, a university can connect teaching rooms at access, combine building traffic at distribution and carry inter-building traffic through the core. The layers describe functions; they do not require exactly three separate devices in every small network. Capacity, resilience, cost and required flows should justify the actual arrangement.

不能丢的条件

用同一包路径依次说明，才能把名称变成因果。小网可合并设备，但角色仍需完成；高可用核心不能弥补接入单点或过载。

闭卷验收

先口头用中文解释，再手写或打出英文；对照上面的回答顺序，逐点检查。再做题库中的变式，确认能按新问法重组。

T002 · PPDIOO 生命周期 · 134词

题目：Describe the six phases of PPDIOO and explain why network design is a life-cycle process.

说明六阶段及持续循环。

先记回答顺序

  1. 完整列出Prepare→Plan→Design→Implement→Operate→Optimize
  2. 每阶段写一个主要任务
  3. 解释运行证据如何反馈到规划和设计
  4. 用新应用带来容量问题说明循环

第1块

六阶段依次准备目标、规划信息与方案、依据需求和现网完成设计。

起句提示：PPDIOO describes six phases: Prepare, Plan, Design, Implement, Operate and Optimize.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

PPDIOO describes six phases: Prepare, Plan, Design, Implement, Operate and Optimize. Prepare establishes business goals and requirements. Plan gathers information, analyses needs and identifies an approach. Design develops the network topology and solution using requirements and knowledge of the existing network.

第2块

实施阶段安装配置，运行阶段管理监测，优化阶段用实际证据解决问题或适应新需求。

起句提示：Implement puts the design into practice by installing and configuring the required solution.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Implement puts the design into practice by installing and configuring the required solution. Operate manages and monitors the deployed network. Optimize uses operational evidence to improve the design or respond to problems and new requirements.

第3块

所以安装不是终点：新教学应用可能暴露容量不足，需回到规划或设计，再实施并验证。

起句提示：The phases form a life cycle rather than a process that ends after installation.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The phases form a life cycle rather than a process that ends after installation. For example, a new teaching application may expose a capacity problem during operation. Optimization may lead back to planning or design before further changes are implemented. This connects ongoing service quality with the organization’s changing goals and preserves the need for verification after deployment.

不能丢的条件

PPDIOO有两个O：Operate和Optimize；不要漏Prepare或把Plan/Design混同。

闭卷验收

先口头用中文解释，再手写或打出英文；对照上面的回答顺序，逐点检查。再做题库中的变式，确认能按新问法重组。

T003 · Eight-step design methodology · 134词

题目：Describe the eight-step network design methodology and its documentation.

说明八步方法与文档用途。

先记回答顺序

  1. 按课件顺序列出八步
  2. 说明需求、现网、方案、实施计划与原型的关系
  3. 列出设计文档主要内容
  4. 最后说明实施后的验证、监测与修改

第1块

八步依次是识别需求、描述现网、设计拓扑和方案、规划实施、建立原型、完整记录、实施、验证监测并修改。

起句提示：The eight steps are: identify customer requirements, describe the existing network, design the topology and solution, plan implementation, construct a prototype, fully document the design, implement it, and verify, monitor and modify it as needed.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The eight steps are: identify customer requirements, describe the existing network, design the topology and solution, plan implementation, construct a prototype, fully document the design, implement it, and verify, monitor and modify it as needed.

第2块

需求含目标与限制；现网记录地点、设备、线缆、地址和协议。原型在正式部署前提供证据。

起句提示：Requirements include organizational and technical goals and constraints.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Requirements include organizational and technical goals and constraints. The existing-network description records sites, devices, cabling, addressing, protocols and other relevant information. The solution should meet those needs, while the implementation plan explains procedures and timing. A prototype provides evidence before full deployment.

第3块

文档保存为什么这样做，供后续运行改进。实施后还须用测量核对目标，不以“装好了”代替成功。

起句提示：Documentation preserves decisions and supports future operation and improvement.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Documentation preserves decisions and supports future operation and improvement. The course suggests sections such as introduction, requirements, existing infrastructure, design, proof of concept, implementation plan and appendices. After implementation, measurements check the goals and guide changes. The method therefore connects planning and evidence with practical delivery rather than treating the final installation as sufficient proof of success.

不能丢的条件

原型是验证方案的一环；不把课件文字中的prototype与全面安装混成一步。

闭卷验收

先口头用中文解释，再手写或打出英文；对照上面的回答顺序，逐点检查。再做题库中的变式，确认能按新问法重组。

T004 · 分析、架构、设计的联系 · 127词

题目：Explain how network analysis, architecture and design are connected.

解释三阶段怎样相互连接。

先记回答顺序

  1. analysis找出需求与约束
  2. architecture据此组织功能和机制
  3. design落实设备、服务与位置
  4. 用同一案例串联三者，并说明不满足条件时要反馈

第1块

Analysis收集用户、应用、设备和现网需要，研究流量、服务与限制，为后续选择提供依据。

起句提示：Network analysis identifies what users, applications and devices need.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Network analysis identifies what users, applications and devices need. It studies the current network, traffic, service levels and constraints. Its results give reasons for later decisions.

第2块

Architecture安排路由、性能、安全、管理等主要功能及关系；design把它落实成设备、服务、供应商和位置。

起句提示：Network architecture uses these results to organize major functions, such as routing, performance, security and management.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Network architecture uses these results to organize major functions, such as routing, performance, security and management. It describes their mechanisms and relationships. Network design then turns the architecture into practical choices of equipment, suppliers, services and physical locations.

第3块

例如教学需稳定，架构可安排冗余和优先级，设计才选具体链路设备。太贵或达不到要求时回头修订。

起句提示：For example, analysis may identify a need for reliable online teaching.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

For example, analysis may identify a need for reliable online teaching. Architecture may introduce redundant paths and traffic priorities. Design chooses suitable devices, links and their placement. The three activities depend on each other. If a design option is too expensive or cannot meet a requirement, earlier decisions must be reviewed. Keeping these links makes the final design easier to explain and defend.

不能丢的条件

不要把 architecture 等同于设备型号清单。

闭卷验收

先口头用中文解释，再手写或打出英文；对照上面的回答顺序，逐点检查。再做题库中的变式，确认能按新问法重组。

T133 · System view 与可辩护的设计 · 147词

题目：Why should network analysis use a system view and maintain an audit trail? Explain technical, budgetary, schedule and resource defensibility.

解释整体系统视角、决策记录和四种设计理由。

先记回答顺序

  1. 说明用户、应用、设备和网络共同决定服务
  2. 解释audit trail记录什么以及为什么要留记录
  3. 完整列技术、预算、进度、资源四种defensibility
  4. 用技术达标但培训来不及的例子作有条件判断

第1块

System view把网络和用户、应用、终端一起看；一条链路加速不一定解决服务器慢。

起句提示：A system view considers the network together with its users, applications and devices.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A system view considers the network together with its users, applications and devices. The delivered service depends on their combined behaviour. Improving one link may have little value if an application or end device remains the limiting part.

第2块

记录需求、测量、假设与理由形成audit trail。技术、预算、进度和人员设备资源四方面都要能解释。

起句提示：Network analysis should therefore record requirements, measurements, assumptions and the reasons for decisions.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Network analysis should therefore record requirements, measurements, assumptions and the reasons for decisions. This audit trail makes the design easier to review and revise. Technical defensibility explains how a choice meets service needs. Budgetary defensibility explains whether its costs fit the available funds. Schedule defensibility addresses whether the work can be completed in the required time. Resource defensibility considers people, skills, equipment and other resources.

第3块

例如设备性能够，但团队来不及培训，方案仍可能不可实施。可辩护是有证据地说明取舍，不是宣称产品永远最好。

起句提示：For example, a faster platform may meet a capacity target but require training that cannot be completed before deployment.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

For example, a faster platform may meet a capacity target but require training that cannot be completed before deployment. A defensible choice acknowledges this constraint and compares alternatives. Defensibility means providing reasons and evidence, rather than claiming that any chosen product is always best.

不能丢的条件

defensibility分四种理由：技术能达标、钱够、时间赶得上、人员设备资源足。audit trail保存依据与假设，方便说明为什么舍弃一个看似更强的选项。

闭卷验收

先口头用中文解释，再手写或打出英文；对照上面的回答顺序，逐点检查。再做题库中的变式，确认能按新问法重组。

T005 · 网络要素与设备 · 129词

题目：Describe the main elements of a computer network and their roles.

说明终端、中间设备和介质的作用。

先记回答顺序

  1. 先列end devices、intermediate devices、media
  2. 分别写产生/接收、转发/连接、传信号的作用
  3. 给switch、router、AP等对应例子
  4. 说明端到端服务仍可能受某一环节限制

第1块

网络由终端、中间设备和传输介质组成；终端发出或接收消息。

起句提示：A computer network connects end devices through communication media and intermediate devices.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A computer network connects end devices through communication media and intermediate devices. End devices, also called hosts, create or receive messages. Examples include computers, servers and printers.

第2块

Switch通常在本地转发frame，router跨网络转发IP packet，AP接入无线设备，其他设备承担信号或连接工作。

起句提示：Intermediate devices help messages reach their destinations.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Intermediate devices help messages reach their destinations. A switch usually forwards Ethernet frames within a local network. A router forwards IP packets between networks. An access point allows wireless devices to join a network. Other devices, such as repeaters and modems, have different connection or signal functions.

第3块

铜线、光纤、无线承载信号，协议和地址让通信可理解。整条路径中慢的无线接入或服务器仍可限制效果。

起句提示：The media carry signals and may include copper cables, optical fibre or wireless links.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The media carry signals and may include copper cables, optical fibre or wireless links. Protocols and addresses make communication possible across these elements. A design must consider how the elements work together. For example, a fast backbone does not remove a slow wireless access link or a server bottleneck at the end of a path.

不能丢的条件

不要把 switch 与 router 的典型作用写反。

闭卷验收

先口头用中文解释，再手写或打出英文；对照上面的回答顺序，逐点检查。再做题库中的变式，确认能按新问法重组。

T006 · 网络类别 · 134词

题目：Explain LAN, WAN, WLAN, CAN, MAN, SAN and VPN.

解释常见网络类别，并区分分类维度。

先记回答顺序

  1. 按范围解释LAN、CAN、MAN、WAN
  2. 解释WLAN的无线接入属性
  3. 解释SAN的存储目的和VPN的逻辑私有连接
  4. 指出分类维度不同，同一网络可同时属于多类

第1块

LAN、WAN、MAN、CAN、PAN主要按地理范围区分，从个人附近到跨广域。

起句提示：A LAN connects devices within a limited local area, such as an office.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A LAN connects devices within a limited local area, such as an office. A WAN connects networks across larger distances. A MAN covers a metropolitan area, while a CAN links networks across a campus. A PAN serves a person’s nearby devices.

第2块

WLAN说无线局域接入，SAN说存储用途，VPN说在其他网络上建立受控的逻辑私有连接。

起句提示：A WLAN is a wireless local area network.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A WLAN is a wireless local area network. Wireless describes how devices connect, so it does not mean the network covers a larger area than a LAN. A SAN is a storage area network designed for communication with storage systems. A VPN creates a logical private connection over another network, often using encryption and controlled access.

第3块

这些词按范围、技术、用途或访问方式分类，能重叠；大学可同时有校园LAN、无线和远程VPN。

起句提示：These labels use different ways of classifying networks: geographical scope, connection technology, purpose and logical access.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

These labels use different ways of classifying networks: geographical scope, connection technology, purpose and logical access. Therefore, one network can fit several labels. A university can have campus LANs, wireless access and VPN access through a WAN.

不能丢的条件

CAN 在这里是 Campus Area Network；不要混用地理范围与用途。

闭卷验收

先口头用中文解释，再手写或打出英文；对照上面的回答顺序，逐点检查。再做题库中的变式，确认能按新问法重组。

T134 · 传输介质与无线范围 · 150词

题目：Compare copper, optical fibre and wireless media. What should a designer check when selecting a medium and describing wireless coverage?

按容量、距离、环境和部署条件比较介质。

先记回答顺序

  1. 分别说明copper、fibre、wireless承载信号与部署特点
  2. 按距离、干扰、移动性、容量、安装与支持比较
  3. 解释无线覆盖类别不能直接推出实际速率
  4. 用骨干＋无线接入例子说明组合与验证

第1块

Copper传电信号，适合一定距离内连接但受距离与干扰限制；fibre传光，适合合适条件下的高容量远距离连接。

起句提示：Copper carries electrical signals and can be practical for local connections, subject to its distance and interference limits.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Copper carries electrical signals and can be practical for local connections, subject to its distance and interference limits. Optical fibre carries light and can support high-capacity links over suitable distances. It requires appropriate interfaces, installation and maintenance.

第2块

Wireless便于移动终端，但覆盖、障碍、干扰和共享用户会影响实得性能；不同无线技术的范围和对准要求也不同。

起句提示：Wireless communication avoids a cable to each mobile endpoint, but its performance depends on coverage, shared demand, obstacles, interference and the radio technology.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Wireless communication avoids a cable to each mobile endpoint, but its performance depends on coverage, shared demand, obstacles, interference and the radio technology. The lecture distinguishes nearby, extended local and mobile communication contexts. Infrared, radio, microwave and laser links also have different propagation and alignment requirements. These names alone do not specify an application’s delivered rate.

第3块

选介质应核对容量、时延、距离、环境、移动、成本与维护技能。整条路径可混用介质，不能只看标称速率。

起句提示：A designer should compare the required capacity, delay, reach, environment, mobility, installation cost and support skills.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A designer should compare the required capacity, delay, reach, environment, mobility, installation cost and support skills. For example, fibre may suit a building backbone while wireless access supports moving users. A complete path can combine media. The choice must be verified under expected conditions rather than based only on a nominal link speed or a medium’s name.

不能丢的条件

课件列出的红外、射频、微波、激光有不同传播/对准条件；不要把某个示意链路写成整个技术永远单向。

闭卷验收

先口头用中文解释，再手写或打出英文；对照上面的回答顺序，逐点检查。再做题库中的变式，确认能按新问法重组。

T007 · 网络组织模式 · 129词

题目：Compare centralized, client-server, distributed, peer-to-peer, cloud-based and hybrid networks.

比较六种组织模式。

先记回答顺序

  1. 完整识别六种组织模式
  2. 按控制/处理位置和资源角色比较
  3. 补管理、依赖、故障与成本的取舍
  4. 说明模式可以组合，用需求选型

第1块

Centralized把重要处理集中；client-server把请求和提供服务分给不同角色，便于管理也可能形成关键故障点。

起句提示：A centralized network places major control or processing at a central computer.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A centralized network places major control or processing at a central computer. A client-server network gives clients and servers different roles: clients request services and servers provide them. Central services can simplify management but may create important points of failure.

第2块

Distributed分散资源与工作；peer-to-peer的节点可同时请求与提供资源，控制一致性可能较难。

起句提示：In a distributed network, resources and work are spread across computers.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

In a distributed network, resources and work are spread across computers. In a peer-to-peer network, peers can both request and provide resources. This can suit small sharing systems, although consistent control may be harder.

第3块

Cloud使用远端托管资源，hybrid组合模式。选择跟随flow、管理、可靠性和成本；cloud也可能是client-server。

起句提示：A cloud-based network uses resources hosted on remote cloud servers and depends on access to those services.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A cloud-based network uses resources hosted on remote cloud servers and depends on access to those services. A hybrid network combines models, such as central authentication with direct sharing between peers. The choice should follow application flows, management needs, reliability and cost. These models can overlap; a cloud service may also use a client-server model.

不能丢的条件

不要声称 distributed 绝对没有中心；模式可以组合。

闭卷验收

T008 · 拓扑与故障 · 131词

题目：Compare mesh, tree, bus, star and ring topologies.

比较五种拓扑及成本、故障影响。

先记回答顺序

  1. 先说明五种连接结构
  2. 逐类解释链路/中心设备故障影响
  3. 比较冗余、成本与扩展性
  4. 补physical与logical的区别和保护条件

第1块

Star连接中心，易扩展；中心故障影响多人，单条边缘链路坏通常只影响所接设备。

起句提示：A star connects devices to a central device.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A star connects devices to a central device. It is easy to extend, but failure of the central device may affect many users. Failure of one edge link usually affects only its attached device.

第2块

Mesh提供备用路径但增加线缆端口和管理；tree分层但根附近故障影响大；bus共享介质；ring故障影响取决于保护机制。

起句提示：A mesh provides multiple connections and alternative paths.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A mesh provides multiple connections and alternative paths. It can improve resilience, but it needs more links, ports and management. A tree organizes connections in a hierarchy. It supports growth, although a failure near the root can affect a large branch. A bus uses a shared backbone, so faults or contention on that shared medium can affect many devices. A ring connects devices in a loop; failure effects depend on whether protection or an alternative path exists.

第3块

Physical topology说实际连接，logical topology说通信关系。选择要平衡成本、规模、流量和故障要求。

起句提示：Physical topology describes actual connections.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Physical topology describes actual connections. Logical topology describes communication relationships. A suitable choice balances cost, scale, traffic and failure requirements.

不能丢的条件

不能说任何 ring 断一条链路都必然全网停机。

T009 · Protocol 的规则 · 132词

题目：What is a network protocol, and why must communicating devices follow common rules?

什么是协议，为什么必须遵循共同规则？

先记回答顺序

  1. 定义protocol是通信规则
  2. 说明格式、含义、顺序和事件动作
  3. 解释双方一致才能识别、响应与处理消息
  4. 用超时或共享介质说明规则的作用

第1块

Protocol规定消息格式、字段含义、交换顺序及事件发生时的动作。

起句提示：A network protocol defines rules for exchanging messages between communicating entities.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A network protocol defines rules for exchanging messages between communicating entities. It specifies message formats, the meaning of fields, the order of exchanges and the actions taken when a message or event occurs.

第2块

双方还须约定编码、消息大小、封装、时序和交付；例如何时回应、超时怎么办。

起句提示：Common rules are needed for encoding, message size, encapsulation, timing and delivery.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Common rules are needed for encoding, message size, encapsulation, timing and delivery. For example, devices must know how to recognize a message, when to respond and what to do if a response does not arrive. Timing rules may include flow control, response timeouts and access to a shared medium.

第3块

协议可由软件或硬件实现，应用、路由、安全各有任务。物理上连通仍不代表双方能正确理解消息。

起句提示：Protocols can be implemented in software, hardware or both.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Protocols can be implemented in software, hardware or both. Different protocols perform different tasks, such as application communication, routing and security. They must work together along a communication path. Without agreed rules, two devices might be physically connected but still be unable to understand or correctly process each other’s messages.

不能丢的条件

protocol 不只是设备之间的一根线或一种语言名称。

T010 · OSI、TCP/IP 与封装 · 129词

题目：Describe the OSI and TCP/IP models and explain encapsulation.

描述分层模型与封装过程。

先记回答顺序

  1. 按顺序列OSI七层与本课TCP/IP四层
  2. 说明上层和network access的对应
  3. 沿发送方向说明封装
  4. 沿接收方向说明解封装及分层用途

第1块

OSI七层各有职责并使用下层服务，顺序从application到physical。

起句提示：The OSI model has seven layers: application, presentation, session, transport, network, data link and physical.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The OSI model has seven layers: application, presentation, session, transport, network, data link and physical. Each layer has a particular communication role and uses services from the layer below.

第2块

课程TCP/IP四层为application、transport、Internet、network access；上下层功能分组与OSI不完全一对一。

起句提示：The TCP/IP model in this course has four layers: application, transport, Internet and network access.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The TCP/IP model in this course has four layers: application, transport, Internet and network access. Its application layer covers several upper OSI functions. Its network access layer covers data-link and physical communication.

第3块

发送时逐层加传输与地址等控制信息、形成frame并发送信号；接收时反向处理。分层便于排错，不代表每层一台设备。

起句提示：During encapsulation, application data passes down the stack.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

During encapsulation, application data passes down the stack. Transport and Internet protocols add information needed for process delivery and routing. The link layer places the packet in a frame, and the physical layer sends signals. At the receiver, corresponding layers interpret and remove the relevant information. Layering separates responsibilities and helps troubleshooting. It does not mean that each model layer always corresponds to one separate physical device.

不能丢的条件

区分 packet、frame 和 signal；TCP/IP 本课用四层。

闭卷验收

T011 · 应用协议、端口与 socket · 135词

题目：Explain the roles of application protocols, ports and sockets.

解释应用层协议与进程定位。

先记回答顺序

  1. 说明应用协议规定消息交互
  2. 区分IP地址、port和socket的定位作用
  3. 解释multiplexing与demultiplexing
  4. 用浏览器和HTTP说明应用不是协议

第1块

HTTP、DNS、SMTP等application protocol规定应用怎样交换有意义的消息；不同协议完成不同任务。

起句提示：Application protocols define how applications exchange meaningful messages.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Application protocols define how applications exchange meaningful messages. HTTP supports web requests and responses, DNS translates names into network information, and SMTP supports sending email. FTP supports file transfer, while POP and IMAP support email access.

第2块

IP帮助把packet送到主机接口，port帮助识别主机内应用端点；socket是程序使用的通信端点。

起句提示：An IP address identifies a network interface for packet delivery.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

An IP address identifies a network interface for packet delivery. A transport port helps identify the application endpoint on a host. For example, HTTP commonly uses TCP port 80 and HTTPS commonly uses TCP port 443. A socket is an application’s communication endpoint.

第3块

Multiplexing把不同socket的数据送上网，demultiplexing把收到的数据分回正确socket。浏览器是应用，HTTP是协议。

起句提示：Multiplexing collects data from different sockets for transmission.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Multiplexing collects data from different sockets for transmission. Demultiplexing delivers received data to the correct socket using transport information. This allows several applications to share the network at the same time. A web browser is an application; HTTP is a protocol that the browser and server use. They are related but are not the same thing.

不能丢的条件

DNS 不等于 DHCP；应用与应用层协议不同。

T135 · PDU、process 与 socket 的完整路径 · 154词

题目：Trace a message from an application process through encapsulation to the medium. Name the PDUs and explain the role of a socket.

把进程、socket、各层PDU及封装连成一条完整链。

先记回答顺序

  1. 定义process和socket，区分application与protocol
  2. 按传输、网络、链路、物理顺序列PDU
  3. 解释封装增加本层控制信息、接收端对应处理
  4. 用web说明完整路径，保留消息和包并非一对一

第1块

运行中的程序是process，借socket使用协议栈；应用协议规定消息类型、形式、含义和交换规则。

起句提示：An application process is a running program.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

An application process is a running program. It uses a socket, an application communication endpoint, to exchange messages through the network stack. An application protocol defines message types, syntax, meaning and exchange rules. The application itself is broader than that protocol.

第2块

Transport的TCP segment或UDP datagram、network packet、link frame、physical bits依次处理；封装会加入header，link也可加trailer。

起句提示：In the course model, the upper OSI layers handle data.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

In the course model, the upper OSI layers handle data. At the transport layer, TCP carries segments and UDP carries datagrams. The network layer carries packets, the data-link layer carries frames, and the physical layer sends bits through signals or symbols. During encapsulation, layers add the control information needed for their roles; the link layer may add a trailer as well as a header.

第3块

目的端逐层读信息并交给正确socket。一个网页消息不必刚好对应一个packet，socket也不是实体网线。

起句提示：At the destination, the stack processes the received information and delivers application data through the appropriate socket.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

At the destination, the stack processes the received information and delivers application data through the appropriate socket. For example, a web browser uses HTTP messages, transport delivery and IP packet delivery together. A socket is not a physical cable, and one application message need not equal one network packet.

不能丢的条件

封装增加本层控制信息，解封装解释并移除。一个message可分成多个segment/packet，所以不要强行一对一；frame也可有trailer。

闭卷验收

T012 · TCP 与 UDP · 129词

题目：Compare TCP and UDP and explain when each may be useful.

比较传输层两种协议。

先记回答顺序

  1. 按连接、可靠性、顺序、控制机制对照TCP与UDP
  2. 说明机制产生的开销和服务差别
  3. 各给合适应用
  4. 补UDP不自动低延迟、应用可自行实现机制

第1块

TCP建立连接，提供可靠有序字节流，用编号、确认和重传处理缺失，还管理流量和拥塞。

起句提示：TCP provides connection-oriented communication and a reliable, ordered byte stream.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

TCP provides connection-oriented communication and a reliable, ordered byte stream. It uses sequence numbers, acknowledgements and retransmission to handle missing data. It also includes flow control and congestion control.

第2块

UDP发送datagram，没有TCP式连接，也不内建送达、顺序或重传保证；应用可能要自行处理。

起句提示：UDP sends datagrams without setting up a TCP-style connection.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

UDP sends datagrams without setting up a TCP-style connection. It does not itself guarantee delivery, ordering or retransmission. This makes its service simpler, but an application may need to provide its own protection against loss or congestion.

第3块

文件传输通常重视完整有序；某些实时应用重视及时到达。但UDP不自动低延迟，TCP也不保证低延迟，仍需看整条路径。

起句提示：TCP is useful when complete and ordered delivery matters, such as transferring a file.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

TCP is useful when complete and ordered delivery matters, such as transferring a file. UDP can suit applications that prefer timely delivery or use their own communication mechanisms. However, UDP is not automatically fast enough for every real-time application, and TCP does not guarantee low delay. The designer must consider application tolerance for loss, delay, overhead and the behaviour of the complete path.

不能丢的条件

不要写 UDP 保证实时、TCP保证固定延迟。

T013 · Forwarding、routing 与各层地址 · 130词

题目：Distinguish forwarding from routing and explain link-layer and network-layer delivery.

区分转发与路由，以及链路层和网络层。

先记回答顺序

  1. routing学习可达性并选路径
  2. forwarding按已有信息进行本地转发
  3. 区分IP跨网络与MAC逐链路交付
  4. 沿下一跳说明frame重建与IP目的地址通常保持

第1块

Routing学到哪些网络可达并选择路径，生成供forwarding使用的信息；forwarding是当前路由器把包交给出口/下一跳的动作。

起句提示：Routing learns network reachability and selects paths.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Routing learns network reachability and selects paths. Routing information is used to build forwarding information. Forwarding is the local action of moving an arriving packet to the correct outgoing interface or next hop.

第2块

IP地址用于跨网络目的接口；frame和MAC用于当前本地链路；physical层把bit变成信号。

起句提示：At the network layer, IP addresses identify source and destination interfaces across networks.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

At the network layer, IP addresses identify source and destination interfaces across networks. At the data-link layer, a frame carries data across a particular local link. Ethernet uses MAC addresses for this local delivery. The physical layer carries the bits as signals.

第3块

跨下一条链路时router通常换新的frame；除NAT等特殊机制外最终IP目的地不变。两层信息都要有。

起句提示：When a router forwards a packet onto another link, it usually creates a new link-layer frame for that next link.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

When a router forwards a packet onto another link, it usually creates a new link-layer frame for that next link. The final IP destination normally remains the same unless a mechanism such as NAT changes it. This distinction helps explain why routers need both network-layer forwarding information and suitable link-layer information for their next-hop communication.

不能丢的条件

不要把 forwarding 的逐包动作与 routing 的路径计算互换。

闭卷验收

先口头用中文解释，再手写或打出英文；对照上面的回答顺序，逐点检查。再做题库中的变式，确认能按新问法重组。

T014 · Router 四部件 · 137词

题目：Describe the four main components of a router and their roles.

解释路由器内部四个主要组成。

先记回答顺序

  1. 列input ports、switching fabric、output ports、routing processor
  2. 沿包的接收、内部交换、排队、发送说明前三项
  3. 解释routing processor的control-plane工作
  4. 区分决策与执行，补性能限制

第1块

Input ports收包并处理接入链路功能，也参与查转发表。

起句提示：A router has input ports, output ports, a switching fabric and a routing processor.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A router has input ports, output ports, a switching fabric and a routing processor. Input ports receive traffic and perform the relevant physical- and link-layer functions. They also participate in forwarding operations.

第2块

Switching fabric在机内把包搬到出口；output ports必要时排队并发送；到达多于服务能力就排队。

起句提示：The switching fabric connects input ports to the appropriate output ports inside the router.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The switching fabric connects input ports to the appropriate output ports inside the router. Output ports store packets when needed and transmit them onto outgoing links using the required link and physical functions. Queuing can occur when arriving traffic exceeds the available transmission service.

第3块

Routing processor负责控制平面与路由信息；SDN下规则也可来自controller。决定规则和逐包执行不同，任一内部部件都可能成瓶颈。

起句提示：The routing processor performs control-plane work.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The routing processor performs control-plane work. In a traditional router, it runs routing protocols, maintains routing information and helps compute forwarding tables. In an SDN design, the device can receive forwarding information through communication with a controller. These roles explain the distinction between deciding forwarding behaviour and carrying out local packet forwarding. Processing, fabric and interface limits can each affect performance.

不能丢的条件

switching fabric是路由器内部连接结构，不是独立外置交换机的必然名称。

T015 · Routing algorithm 三组分类 · 136词

题目：Compare centralized/decentralized, static/dynamic and load-sensitive/load-insensitive routing algorithms.

解释三种独立分类维度。

先记回答顺序

  1. 明确这是三条独立分类轴
  2. 比较centralized与decentralized的信息来源
  3. 比较static与dynamic的变化方式
  4. 比较load-sensitive与load-insensitive的成本输入，并说明类别可交叉

第1块

第一维看算法用全网信息还是邻居逐步交换；link-state可在各router各自用全图计算。

起句提示：The course classifies routing algorithms along three separate dimensions.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The course classifies routing algorithms along three separate dimensions. A centralized algorithm uses complete network information to calculate paths. Link-state routing uses this global-information approach, even though calculations can be performed at individual routers. A decentralized algorithm develops routes through interactions between nodes; distance-vector routing is an example.

第2块

第二维看路线静态还是会随变化更新；第三维看成本是否反映当前负载。

起句提示：A static approach changes routes slowly or through manual intervention.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A static approach changes routes slowly or through manual intervention. A dynamic approach can respond to changes in topology or other routing information.

第3块

三维可以组合，不能把“全局信息”误认为必须只有一台中心计算机；选择还看规模、变化与开销。

起句提示：A load-sensitive algorithm changes link costs to reflect current congestion.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A load-sensitive algorithm changes link costs to reflect current congestion. A load-insensitive algorithm does not explicitly use current congestion as its link-cost input. These categories should not be treated as identical. For example, a dynamic protocol can react to a failed link without being load-sensitive. Explain which property is being compared before claiming that one algorithm is more responsive or suitable for a particular network.

不能丢的条件

centralized knowledge不表示一定只有一台中心机器；dynamic不等于load-sensitive。

T016 · Link、physical 与 service model · 131词

题目：Describe link-layer and physical-layer services and distinguish them from an end-to-end network service model.

区分逐链路、物理传输与端到端服务承诺。

先记回答顺序

  1. 说明data-link逐链路frame服务
  2. 说明physical传送bits和信号
  3. 说明end-to-end service model承诺哪些交付特征
  4. 强调单链路可靠不等于全路径保证

第1块

Data-link在相邻节点间处理frame，可包括成帧、接入介质和某些差错处理。

起句提示：The data-link layer transfers frames between adjacent nodes on a link.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The data-link layer transfers frames between adjacent nodes on a link. Its functions can include framing, access to the medium, error detection and correction, and, for some link protocols, reliable local delivery. A device uses a network interface to connect to a link.

第2块

Physical负责bit对应的电、光或无线信号及传输条件；某一跳的服务能力有边界。

起句提示：The physical layer transmits bits through a medium, such as copper, optical fibre or wireless communication.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The physical layer transmits bits through a medium, such as copper, optical fibre or wireless communication. Its operation depends on that medium and the associated signalling rules.

第3块

端到端服务还涉及所有链路、router和端点。单条链路可靠不保证整个应用任务可靠或准时。

起句提示：An end-to-end network service model describes the delivery characteristics offered between sender and receiver.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

An end-to-end network service model describes the delivery characteristics offered between sender and receiver. Possible characteristics include delivery, bounded delay, ordering, minimum bandwidth or security. Best effort does not promise all these properties. A reliable local link is therefore different from guaranteed end-to-end delivery. Every relevant part of the path, and possibly the endpoints, must support the application’s actual service needs.

不能丢的条件

不是所有link protocol都提供可靠交付；某一跳可靠不代表端到端保证。

闭卷验收

先口头用中文解释，再手写或打出英文；对照上面的回答顺序，逐点检查。再做题库中的变式，确认能按新问法重组。

T017 · Strategic planning 与 Crouch · 136词

题目：Explain the purpose of a strategic network plan using the Crouch questions.

用 Crouch 的问题说明战略规划。

先记回答顺序

  1. 用Crouch问题串起使命、现状、目标、路径与验证
  2. 解释gap analysis
  3. 区分strategy与tactics
  4. 用多校园目标连接预算、人员和里程碑

第1块

战略网络计划把组织长期目标与行动和资源连接起来，先回答使命和当前状态。

起句提示：A strategic network plan connects long-term organizational goals with actions and resources.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A strategic network plan connects long-term organizational goals with actions and resources. It asks why the organization exists, how it operates, where it is now, where it wants to be, how it will get there and how it will know that it has arrived.

第2块

Crouch的问题帮助问目标、差距、路径、策略和怎样判断成功。

起句提示：These questions connect mission and values with the current situation, future direction and measures of success.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

These questions connect mission and values with the current situation, future direction and measures of success. Gap analysis identifies the difference between the present and desired states. Strategy sets the broad direction and deployment of resources. Tactics describe more specific actions used to carry out that direction.

第3块

例如扩校区须先说明教学业务目标，再分解网络措施；策略方向和实际行动都需能被检查。

起句提示：For example, a university may aim to support teaching across several campuses.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

For example, a university may aim to support teaching across several campuses. Its plan must connect that aim to network reach, service quality, budgets, staff and milestones. A plan is useful only if people can implement it, monitor progress and revise it when conditions change.

不能丢的条件

不要把 strategy 写成某条具体设备配置命令。

T018 · 规划建议与陷阱 · 136词

题目：Describe good strategic planning practices and common pitfalls.

说明有效规划方法及常见陷阱。

先记回答顺序

  1. 先说明规划是持续过程
  2. 列实用做法：简洁、领导参与、创新与实施可行
  3. 列常见陷阱并说明后果
  4. 用能否筹资、运行和维护检验计划

第1块

战略规划不是一次写完的文件，而应持续、简单、让领导和相关人员参与。

起句提示：Strategic planning should be an ongoing way of thinking.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Strategic planning should be an ongoing way of thinking. The plan is never completely finished. Keep the process simple and manageable, involve leaders, encourage creative thinking and consider implementation before accepting a strategy. Planning should help the organization achieve its mission.

第2块

计划应与使命相连，也要考虑创新、资源与实际可执行性；常见陷阱是照抄或脱离现实。

起句提示：Common pitfalls include relying only on financial forecasts, collecting forms without real discussion and claiming to support planning without providing time or resources.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Common pitfalls include relying only on financial forecasts, collecting forms without real discussion and claiming to support planning without providing time or resources. Long-term plans can also fail when rewards encourage only short-term results. Other mistakes include copying competitors, blaming outsiders, weakening staff commitment and keeping outdated assumptions about the future.

第3块

网络需求变了就复查计划；只写雄心口号却没负责人、资源和检验，会使计划失效。

起句提示：For a network project, a technically attractive plan may still fail if no one can fund, operate or maintain it.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

For a network project, a technically attractive plan may still fail if no one can fund, operate or maintain it. A useful plan therefore links goals to resources, responsibilities and review. Success means improved organizational outcomes rather than simply producing a large planning document.

不能丢的条件

六条建议、十条陷阱的完整清单见同题变式清单。

闭卷验收

T019 · 四种变革策略 · 128词

题目：Describe the four change strategies and the factors used to select them.

解释四种变革策略与选择因素。

先记回答顺序

  1. 列四种change strategy及各自作用机制
  2. 按阻力、人数、利害、时间、能力、依赖选择
  3. 说明可能混合
  4. 给迁移例子并保留沟通和支持条件

第1块

Rational-empirical靠信息与利益；normative-re-educative改变共同价值；power-coercive用权力；environmental-adaptive先建新环境再迁移。

起句提示：Rational-empirical change uses information and incentives, assuming people respond to their interests.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Rational-empirical change uses information and incentives, assuming people respond to their interests. Normative-re-educative change builds commitment by changing shared values and social norms. Power-coercive change uses authority and sanctions. Environmental-adaptive change creates a new environment and gradually moves people into it.

第2块

没有一法通用；考虑阻力、受影响人数、风险、时间、团队能力及人员对组织的依赖。

起句提示：No single strategy fits every situation.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

No single strategy fits every situation. Selection depends on resistance, the number of people affected, the stakes, the time available, the change team’s expertise and the dependency between staff and the organization. A large or high-stakes change may need a mixture.

第3块

网络迁移可结合解释培训、期限和逐步切换。时间紧可能更多用权力，但仍需沟通支持。

起句提示：For example, a network migration may combine explanations and training with clear deadlines and a gradual move to the new service.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

For example, a network migration may combine explanations and training with clear deadlines and a gradual move to the new service. The course associates short time frames with greater use of authority, but this does not mean authority removes the need for communication or implementation support.

不能丢的条件

四种是组织变革策略，不是四种 routing protocol。

闭卷验收

T020 · 规划团队与利益相关者 · 134词

题目：Who should be involved in strategic planning, and why?

谁应参与规划，为什么？

先记回答顺序

  1. 先说明规划同时需要权力与实务知识
  2. 列领导、开发实施、供信息、审核授权角色
  3. 解释跨组织参与和coordinator的作用
  4. 说明团队素质与阶段变化

第1块

战略规划同时需要有批准预算和方向权力的人，也需要了解真实网络使用情况的人。

起句提示：Strategic planning needs both authority and practical knowledge.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Strategic planning needs both authority and practical knowledge. Leaders, including the chief executive and board representatives, should guide strategic direction and support implementation. At least one participant should have authority to make strategic decisions.

第2块

领导提供授权与方向；使用者、工程师和管理者提供真实需求、现网知识和实施限制。

起句提示：The team should include people who develop and implement the plan, people who provide key information and people who review or authorize the result.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The team should include people who develop and implement the plan, people who provide key information and people who review or authorize the result. Input should come from across the organization so that users and staff have a stake in the outcome. A coordinator should arrange meetings, record decisions and track preparation.

第3块

参与范围要足以发现冲突并形成责任；只让某一方写计划，可能漏掉预算、体验或运维问题。

起句提示：Team members should work well together, be committed and be respected by others.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Team members should work well together, be committed and be respected by others. Membership can change as the work moves from mission and direction to analysis and detailed actions. For a network plan, this helps connect business goals with users’ needs and the realities of operating the network.

不能丢的条件

让顾问独自决定并不能替代组织领导的参与。

闭卷验收

T021 · Core、feature、future、rejected、informational · 138词

题目：Explain the five requirement categories used during analysis.

解释需求分析中的五类归属。

先记回答顺序

  1. 列core、feature、future、rejected、informational
  2. 每类给判定依据
  3. 给一个核心、可选和未来例子
  4. 区分类别与来源Type，可同时记录

第1块

Core是达成约定目的所必需；feature是期望但可权衡的能力；future属于后续增长。

起句提示：Core requirements are necessary for the network to achieve its agreed purpose.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Core requirements are necessary for the network to achieve its agreed purpose. Features are desirable capabilities that may be delivered later if they are not essential now. Future requirements describe needs planned for later revisions or upgrades.

第2块

Rejected是分析后明确不纳入，informational只是背景事实，可用来推导真正需求。

起句提示：Rejected requirements are considered unnecessary or undesirable for the agreed solution.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Rejected requirements are considered unnecessary or undesirable for the agreed solution. The reason for rejection should be recorded. Informational items describe useful background, such as user numbers or existing conditions, without necessarily requiring a new capability.

第3块

类别帮助决定现在做什么以及为什么；不要把“用户提出”误当“必须做”，也不要把尚不清楚的项直接拒绝。

起句提示：These categories help control scope, resources and expectations.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

These categories help control scope, resources and expectations. For example, access to a critical service may be core, an optional report may be a feature, and a planned new campus may create a future requirement. Classification should follow analysis and stakeholder agreement. It differs from classifying a requirement by its source, such as user, application, device or network. A useful specification can record both dimensions.

不能丢的条件

不要把future当rejected；informational背景也可能用于推导真正需求。

闭卷验收

先口头用中文解释，再手写或打出英文；对照上面的回答顺序，逐点检查。再做题库中的变式，确认能按新问法重组。

T022 · MUST、SHOULD、MAY 与禁止性要求 · 142词

题目：Explain MUST, MUST NOT, SHOULD, SHOULD NOT and MAY. How does the course relate these words to requirement categories?

解释要求强度，并区别RFC词义与课件分类提示。

先记回答顺序

  1. 区分必须、禁止、强烈建议与可选
  2. 保留SHOULD允许有理由的例外
  3. 说明课件与项目类别的联系
  4. 用正反要求举例，不把MAY解释为必须拒绝

第1块

MUST/SHALL/REQUIRED表示强制，MUST NOT表示强制禁止；SHOULD系列通常要遵守，偏离需说明理由；MAY可选。

起句提示：MUST, SHALL and REQUIRED express a mandatory requirement.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

MUST, SHALL and REQUIRED express a mandatory requirement. MUST NOT and SHALL NOT express an absolute prohibition. SHOULD and RECOMMENDED express a strong recommendation: an exception may be reasonable, but its consequences must be understood. SHOULD NOT and NOT RECOMMENDED similarly allow justified exceptions. MAY and OPTIONAL mean that a feature is genuinely optional.

第2块

课件把强制词与core、建议词与feature或future等联系，是分析提示，不是任何可选项都要删除。

起句提示：The lecture links mandatory wording with core requirements, recommended wording with features or future needs, and optional wording with several possible project categories.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The lecture links mandatory wording with core requirements, recommended wording with features or future needs, and optional wording with several possible project categories. This is guidance for analysing a project, rather than a rule that every optional item must be rejected.

第3块

例如保护核心记录可写MUST，报表可写SHOULD，附加显示可写MAY；禁止未授权访问仍是核心要求。

起句提示：For example, a system MUST protect an essential record, SHOULD provide a useful report and MAY offer an optional display.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

For example, a system MUST protect an essential record, SHOULD provide a useful report and MAY offer an optional display. State the required behaviour clearly. A mandatory prohibition, such as preventing unauthorized access, is still a core requirement even though it describes something that must not happen.

不能丢的条件

普通英文may不是自动在使用RFC关键词；MAY不等于rejected。RFC2119解释与课程管理分类要分清。

闭卷验收

先口头用中文解释，再手写或打出英文；对照上面的回答顺序，逐点检查。再做题库中的变式，确认能按新问法重组。

T023 · 分类、Type、Status 与 Priority 的案例 · 139词

题目：Classify a mandatory access-control requirement, an optional dashboard, a next-year campus expansion, a request formally rejected after analysis and a user-count statement. Distinguish type, status and priority.

给五项陈述分类，再解释规格表三个字段。

先记回答顺序

  1. 对五个案例逐一给类别和理由
  2. 区分Type、Status、Priority各描述什么
  3. 说明同一要求可同时具备多个字段
  4. 资料不足时记录待确认

第1块

必需访问控制属core，可选仪表板是feature，明年扩校区是future，分析后拒绝的要求属rejected，现有人数属informational。

起句提示：A mandatory access-control capability is a core requirement.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A mandatory access-control capability is a core requirement. An optional dashboard can be a feature. A campus expansion planned for next year creates a future requirement. A request rejected after analysis belongs in the rejected category, with its reason recorded. A statement of current user numbers is informational, although it can support derived capacity requirements.

第2块

Type写谁或什么提出需求，status写当前处理状态，priority写重要程度与处理顺序。

起句提示：The specification also records other dimensions.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The specification also records other dimensions. Type identifies the source or entity involved, such as user, application, device or network. Status records the item’s current handling state, including information or an unresolved decision. Priority expresses its relative importance or order of attention.

第3块

一条application需求也可同时是core和high priority；尚未明确时记不确定，不暗中批准或否决。

起句提示：These fields should not be confused.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

These fields should not be confused. An application requirement may be core and high priority, while a network requirement may be optional and deferred. If an item is still unclear, record the uncertainty rather than silently treating it as approved or rejected.

不能丢的条件

Unsupported/unclear并不自动rejected，题目明确“分析后决定不做”才归rejected；TBD就是待确定。

T024 · 三种服务等级 · 134词

题目：Compare best-effort, predictable and guaranteed network services.

比较 best-effort、predictable、guaranteed。

先记回答顺序

  1. 按服务承诺强度比较三等级
  2. 说明best-effort、predictable、guaranteed各提供什么
  3. 按应用需要选服务
  4. 补端到端资源、测量与验证条件

第1块

Best-effort尽力传送，但不承诺特定容量或时延，适合能容忍波动的任务。

起句提示：Best-effort service attempts to deliver traffic without promising a particular level of performance.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Best-effort service attempts to deliver traffic without promising a particular level of performance. It may be suitable for traffic that can tolerate variable delay or completion time.

第2块

Predictable追求较可控稳定；guaranteed在约定条件下对个别流承诺指标，需要资源、控制和端到端验证。

起句提示：Predictable service aims for a more controlled and consistent level of performance.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Predictable service aims for a more controlled and consistent level of performance. Guaranteed service commits to specified requirements for an individual flow or service under stated conditions. Such requirements may involve capacity, delay or RMA. They need suitable resource allocation, control and verification along the complete path.

第3块

下载可用尽力服务，关键互动可能需更强支持；不是所有flow都用最贵级别，单台router配置也不等于全程保证。

起句提示：For example, a flexible download may accept best effort, while a critical interactive service may need stronger support.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

For example, a flexible download may accept best effort, while a critical interactive service may need stronger support. The choice must follow application needs rather than giving every flow the most expensive service. Services should be configurable, measurable and verifiable. A guarantee at one router alone cannot establish an end-to-end guarantee if another part of the path cannot support it.

不能丢的条件

guaranteed 不等于无条件永远不会出故障。

闭卷验收

先口头用中文解释，再手写或打出英文；对照上面的回答顺序，逐点检查。再做题库中的变式，确认能按新问法重组。

T136 · Service hierarchy 与端到端瓶颈 · 153词

题目：Explain service hierarchy and why service requirements should be configurable, measurable and verifiable across an end-to-end path.

说明通用与专用服务如何组织，为什么必须配置、测量和验证。

先记回答顺序

  1. 分别解释configurable、measurable、verifiable
  2. 说明通用骨干服务与靠近用户的特定支持
  3. 解释需求沿端到端路径累积以及瓶颈影响
  4. 用教学服务说明配置存在不等于已验证达标

第1块

网络服务要支撑用户、应用、设备需求；可配置是能落实要求，可测量是能观察表现，可验证是能和目标比较。

起句提示：Network services support user, application and device requirements.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Network services support user, application and device requirements. A useful service must be configurable so that the required behaviour can be applied, measurable so that its performance can be observed, and verifiable so that evidence can be compared with the requirement.

第2块

服务层次可让骨干提供通用支撑、靠近用户处提供专用支撑；这是设计思路，不是固定设备位置规则。

起句提示：A service hierarchy can place more general support in the backbone and more specific support near particular users or applications.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A service hierarchy can place more general support in the backbone and more specific support near particular users or applications. This is a design approach, not a rule that every function belongs at exactly one layer. Requirements accumulate as services depend on devices and network resources along their paths.

第3块

互动课堂依赖接入、骨干、端点完整路径。核心再好也补不了忙碌服务器，须查瓶颈与实际体验。

起句提示：For example, an interactive lesson needs suitable access, backbone and endpoint performance.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

For example, an interactive lesson needs suitable access, backbone and endpoint performance. A well-configured core cannot compensate for an overloaded server or an access link that cannot carry the traffic. The designer should trace the complete path, identify bottlenecks and verify the application’s service. A local configuration or a successful connectivity test alone does not prove that the end-to-end requirement has been met.

不能丢的条件

service hierarchy帮助把通用功能放骨干、专用支持靠近用户，但路径仍累积设备和服务要求；最弱环节可成瓶颈。

闭卷验收

先口头用中文解释，再手写或打出英文；对照上面的回答顺序，逐点检查。再做题库中的变式，确认能按新问法重组。

T025 · 四类需求 · 133词

题目：Describe user, application, device and network requirements.

描述四类需求并说明联系。

先记回答顺序

  1. 列user、application、device、network四类
  2. 分别说明关注对象和典型要求
  3. 用同一视频教学案例串联四类
  4. 补人数、位置与增长

第1块

User requirement先表达人的期望，往往还需澄清；application requirement把它变成流量和时延等技术需要。

起句提示：User requirements describe what people need, such as timely responses, reliability, security, affordability and future growth.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

User requirements describe what people need, such as timely responses, reliability, security, affordability and future growth. They are often less technical and need clarification.

第2块

Device requirement说服务器终端的处理、存储和接口能力；network requirement含现网、扩容、位置和互通限制。

起句提示：Application requirements translate those needs into communication behaviour and service requirements, such as capacity, delay and reliability.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Application requirements translate those needs into communication behaviour and service requirements, such as capacity, delay and reliability. Device requirements describe the capabilities and limits of end systems, servers and specialized equipment. Processor, memory, storage and interface performance can all affect the service. Network requirements include existing infrastructure, scalability, location, interoperability and support constraints.

第3块

“视频流畅”须同时看应用指标、终端处理和整条网络路径，不能只问链路速度。

起句提示：These requirements build on each other.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

These requirements build on each other. For example, users may want a smooth video lesson. The application needs suitable delay, loss and capacity, devices must process the media, and the network must support the complete path. Gathering only link speed would miss important parts of that requirement. Record user numbers, locations and expected growth as well.

不能丢的条件

host/device 是需求层级之一，不要漏掉终端瓶颈。

闭卷验收

T026 · 应用分类与需求 · 128词

题目：Distinguish mission-critical, rate-critical, real-time and interactive applications.

区分应用类型及主导性能需求。

先记回答顺序

  1. 按关键性、速率和时间区分四类应用
  2. 解释mission-critical与rate-critical
  3. 解释real-time与interactive
  4. 说明可重叠并转成可测量指标

第1块

Mission-critical的失败后果重，关注可靠、可维护与可用；rate-critical关注所需数据速率。

起句提示：A mission-critical application is essential to the organization’s work.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A mission-critical application is essential to the organization’s work. It needs suitable reliability, maintainability and availability because failure has serious consequences. A rate-critical application needs a particular data rate or capacity to operate properly.

第2块

Real-time数据迟到可能失去价值；interactive用户需要可接受的响应时间，两者也可有丢失和可靠性要求。

起句提示：Real-time and interactive applications have important timing requirements.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Real-time and interactive applications have important timing requirements. Real-time traffic may become less useful if it arrives too late. Interactive users need responses within an acceptable time. These applications can also have capacity, loss and reliability requirements.

第3块

类别会重叠；应把“关键”“实时”写成可量的容量、时延或可用性要求。

起句提示：The categories can overlap.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The categories can overlap. A live teaching service may be interactive, rate-sensitive and important to the university’s mission. Categorization helps identify where predictable, guaranteed or high-performance services may be needed. The designer should still define measurable requirements and application locations. Calling an application “critical” or “real-time” alone does not state the required availability, data rate or delay.

不能丢的条件

不要把 mission-critical 直接等同于带宽最大。

T027 · 需求分析五步 · 131词

题目：Describe the requirements analysis process and its outputs.

说明需求分析步骤与产物。

先记回答顺序

  1. 收集列出需求
  2. 建立service metrics并分析用户/应用行为
  3. 把需求发展为capacity、delay、RMA并映射位置
  4. 说明requirements specification持续更新

第1块

先从用户、管理层、现网和项目起点收集并列出需求，再制定可测指标。

起句提示：The process begins by gathering and listing requirements from users, management, the current network and initial project conditions.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The process begins by gathering and listing requirements from users, management, the current network and initial project conditions. The designer then develops service metrics so that needs can be measured and checked.

第2块

研究用户和应用行为，包括人数、频率、持续时间和数据量，然后细化容量、时延与RMA。

起句提示：Next, user and application behaviour is characterized.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Next, user and application behaviour is characterized. This includes user numbers, session frequency, duration, data sizes and traffic patterns. Requirements are then developed into more precise capacity, delay and RMA needs. Finally, the requirements are mapped to locations to show where services and performance are required.

第3块

最后映到具体地点，产出可维护的requirements specification与地图；新高峰数据出现时可回头修订。

起句提示：The main output is a maintained requirements specification, supported by measurements and maps.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The main output is a maintained requirements specification, supported by measurements and maps. The activities may be revisited when new information appears. For example, measured peak use may show that the original capacity estimate was too low. A clear process helps later architecture and design choices remain connected to evidence rather than assumptions.

不能丢的条件

流程允许反复修订，不能把收集到的原话直接当最终可测试需求。

T028 · 初始条件、约束与红旗 · 130词

题目：What initial conditions and warning signs should a designer examine when gathering requirements?

收集需求时应检查哪些条件和警讯？

先记回答顺序

  1. 列项目类型、范围、目标、现网及外部条件
  2. 列预算、人员、设备约束
  3. 识别无量化或互相矛盾的红旗
  4. 通过讨论与测量把模糊表述改成可验证需求

第1块

先了解项目类型、范围、目标、现网和外部组织或财务影响；预算、设备、人员等也会限制方案。

起句提示：Initial conditions describe the starting point of the project.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Initial conditions describe the starting point of the project. They include project type, scope, initial goals, the existing network and outside political, administrative or financial forces. Constraints may include budget, existing equipment, staff and organizational rules.

第2块

含糊的“实时”“高性能”、互相矛盾的要求、没定义观察窗口的可用性百分比都是警讯。

起句提示：The designer should also look for unclear or unrealistic requirements.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The designer should also look for unclear or unrealistic requirements. Warning signs include “real-time” without a timing limit, “high performance” without evidence, inconsistent requirements and availability stated only as a percentage without a measurement period or service definition.

第3块

通过访谈、测量或试验澄清；例如99.9%要写服务、时间窗口和维护计法，才可能检验。

起句提示：These statements should be clarified through discussion and measurements.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

These statements should be clarified through discussion and measurements. For example, “99.9% availability” needs a defined observation period, acceptable outage pattern and rules about maintenance. Existing measurements or a testbed can help validate the problem. Setting realistic expectations early reduces the risk of choosing an expensive solution that does not address what users actually need.

不能丢的条件

说明99.9%不是错误；仅有百分比缺少口径。

T029 · 规格、测量与跟踪 · 130词

题目：How should requirements be measured, recorded and kept up to date?

如何测量、记录、维护需求？

先记回答顺序

  1. 用调查、访谈、观察和测量收集证据
  2. 记录规格字段及来源
  3. 区分gathered与derived要求
  4. 维护变更历史并记录测量时间、负载、方法和单位

第1块

调查、跟进访谈、会议、观察和现网测量帮助核对用户说法，试验台可验证技术与应用能否一起工作。

起句提示：Requirements should be gathered through surveys, follow-up interviews, group meetings and observation.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Requirements should be gathered through surveys, follow-up interviews, group meetings and observation. Measurements on the existing network help verify reported problems and current behaviour. A testbed can show how proposed technologies interact with the organization’s applications.

第2块

规格表写清ID、日期、类别、内容、来源、地点、状态与优先级，区分原话和分析推导要求。

起句提示：A requirements specification should record each requirement clearly.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A requirements specification should record each requirement clearly. Useful fields include an identifier, date, type, description, source, location, status and priority. It should distinguish gathered statements from requirements derived through analysis.

第3块

变更和拒绝理由要留历史；测量要写时间、负载、方法和单位，不能用空闲时数据代表高峰。

起句提示：Keep the specification current and accessible to the people involved.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Keep the specification current and accessible to the people involved. Record additions, changes and rejected proposals rather than silently replacing the history. This creates a useful audit trail. Measurements also need context, including time, load, method and units. A quiet-period measurement alone may not represent peak conditions. Good records make requirements easier to review, test and trace into later architecture and design decisions.

不能丢的条件

不把瞬时测试或安静时段读数当所有时段的性能。

闭卷验收

T030 · Capacity、throughput、delay 与 jitter · 133词

题目：Distinguish capacity, throughput, delay, jitter and task completion time.

区分五项性能概念。

先记回答顺序

  1. 区分capacity潜力与throughput实得
  2. 区分delay、jitter与整任务完成时间
  3. 用数据量/时间说明平均速率
  4. 补峰值、开销、竞争与端系统条件

第1块

Capacity是资源可能承载的速率，throughput是特定条件下实际收到的有用速率，二者不能画等号。

起句提示：Capacity is the data-carrying capability of a network resource.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Capacity is the data-carrying capability of a network resource. Throughput is the rate of useful transfer actually achieved under particular conditions. A high-capacity link does not mean an application always receives that rate.

第2块

Delay是一段数据/响应的时间，单向和往返不同；jitter是delay变化，task completion time是一整个任务所用时间。

起句提示：Delay is the time taken for data or a response to travel through the system.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Delay is the time taken for data or a response to travel through the system. One-way delay and round-trip time describe different paths. Jitter is variation in delay. Task completion time is the time needed to finish an entire application task, such as transferring a file.

第3块

数据量除以允许时间得基础平均速率，还须加开销、峰值、并发和端点限制；应用持续时间也不是packet delay。

起句提示：Data size divided by the allowed transfer time gives a basic average rate requirement after units are converted.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Data size divided by the allowed transfer time gives a basic average rate requirement after units are converted. Actual planning must also consider overhead, peaks, competing flows and end-system limits. Real-time applications may be sensitive to delay and jitter even when average throughput is adequate. Application duration should not be confused with packet delay.

不能丢的条件

8bits=1byte；duration不等于delay；average不等于peak。

闭卷验收

先口头用中文解释，再手写或打出英文；对照上面的回答顺序，逐点检查。再做题库中的变式，确认能按新问法重组。

T137 · Peak、sustained、minimum 与 burst · 155词

题目：Distinguish peak, sustained and minimum rates, burst size and burst duration. Why is an average rate alone insufficient for planning?

区分速率与突发的五个量，并说明平均值的限制。

先记回答顺序

  1. 用同一观察口径区分peak、sustained、minimum rate
  2. 说明burst size是数据量、duration是时间
  3. 解释短时输入超过输出如何形成队列、时延与丢失
  4. 用低长时平均的backup说明为什么平均值不足

第1块

Peak是给定短时间窗口内的高发送速率，sustained是较长窗口维持速率，minimum要说是观测值还是服务最低要求。

起句提示：Peak rate describes a high short-term sending rate under a stated measurement interval.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Peak rate describes a high short-term sending rate under a stated measurement interval. Sustained rate describes the rate maintained over a longer stated period. A minimum-rate requirement states the least service an application needs under specified conditions. The observation period and meaning of each value must be clear.

第2块

Burst size是一次突发的数据量，duration是持续时间；突发可能快于链路服务，缓冲会增加等待，不足则丢包。

起句提示：Burst size is the amount of data in a burst, while burst duration is how long the burst lasts.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Burst size is the amount of data in a burst, while burst duration is how long the burst lasts. A short burst can temporarily arrive faster than a link can transmit. A buffer may absorb some of it, but waiting increases delay and insufficient space can lead to loss.

第3块

一小时平均值可隐藏几秒钟的高峰；规划要看同时发生的flow、缓冲、开销与应用容忍度。

起句提示：For example, a backup may average a modest rate over an hour while sending much faster for several seconds.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

For example, a backup may average a modest rate over an hour while sending much faster for several seconds. Planning only from the hourly average can miss that competition with interactive traffic. Capacity planning should consider timing, concurrent flows, buffers, application tolerance and overhead. State whether a minimum rate is an observed value or a required service commitment.

不能丢的条件

短时到达超过输出时先积累队列，队列有限再丢包。较低长时平均不排除突发损害时延敏感业务；minimum须说是观察值还是保证需求。

闭卷验收

T031 · 应用事务的频率与持续时间 · 147词

题目：Why is it important to consider frequency and time duration for application transactions when characterizing application behavior?

描述应用行为特征时，为什么考虑应用事务的发生频率和持续时间很重要？

先记回答顺序

  1. 先回答两者帮助估计流量发生时间与并发
  2. 解释frequency和duration
  3. 写事务重叠→共享负载→容量和延迟影响
  4. 给高峰备份例子，保留duration不等于速率的条件

第1块

Frequency说事务发生多常，duration说每次活跃多久；合起来帮助估计何时重叠。

起句提示：Frequency describes how often an application transaction occurs.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Frequency describes how often an application transaction occurs. Duration describes how long it remains active. Together, they help the designer estimate when traffic occurs and how many transactions may be active at the same time.

第2块

多个事务同时活跃会竞争链路和服务器，影响容量、时延和用户感受；全天总量可能藏住高峰。

起句提示：For example, frequent transactions that last several minutes may overlap.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

For example, frequent transactions that last several minutes may overlap. Shared links and servers must then support several active transactions. This can affect capacity, delay and the quality of service that users receive. A total daily traffic figure alone may hide these busy periods.

第3块

还须看人数、数据量与速率；持续更久不必然每秒速率更高，弹性备份可避开繁忙教学时段。

起句提示：The designer should therefore consider transaction frequency and duration together with the number of users, data sizes, traffic rates and service requirements.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The designer should therefore consider transaction frequency and duration together with the number of users, data sizes, traffic rates and service requirements. Longer duration does not always mean a higher data rate, especially if the same amount of data is sent more slowly. Understanding these factors supports better capacity estimates, flow specifications and scheduling decisions, such as moving a flexible backup task away from a busy teaching period.

不能丢的条件

只写“能提高性能”；把duration等同于delay；声称持续更久就必然需要更大带宽；完全不解释并发与峰值。

闭卷验收

T032 · RMA 指标与边界 · 126词

题目：Explain reliability, maintainability and availability and their relationship.

解释 RMA 及其关系。

先记回答顺序

  1. 分别定义reliability、maintainability、availability
  2. 对应MTBF/MTBCF与MTTR
  3. 解释可用性如何联系故障间隔和恢复时间
  4. 补观察窗口、计入规则与停机分布

第1块

Reliability看故障频度，可用MTBF或关键业务的MTBCF表示。

起句提示：Reliability describes how often failures occur.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Reliability describes how often failures occur. It can be represented by mean time between failures, or MTBF. For critical services, mean time between mission-critical failures, or MTBCF, may be more useful.

第2块

Maintainability看修复恢复要多久，MTTR包含发现、定位、维修和测试；availability由故障间隔和恢复时间共同影响。

起句提示：Maintainability describes how long restoration takes.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Maintainability describes how long restoration takes. MTTR includes the time needed to detect, isolate, repair and test a fault before full service returns. Availability relates the time between failures to restoration time. The course uses A = MTBF / (MTBF + MTTR), or the corresponding MTBCF form.

第3块

减少故障或缩短恢复都可提高可用性，但相同百分比可对应很不同的中断模式；先定义故障与统计窗口。

起句提示：Availability can improve by reducing failures or shortening restoration.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Availability can improve by reducing failures or shortening restoration. Two systems may have the same availability but very different outage patterns. A frequent short interruption is not always equivalent to one long interruption for users. Define which failures count, the observation period and whether scheduled maintenance is included before comparing figures.

不能丢的条件

MTBF、MTTR单位必须一致；课件公式通常排除计划维护。

闭卷验收

T139 · 串联系统故障率与平均首次故障时间 · 62词

题目：Two critical components are in series: either component’s first failure stops the service. Assume independent, constant failure rates and component mean times to failure (MTTFs) of 1,000 h and 2,000 h. Estimate the system failure rate and mean time to its first failure.

在独立、恒定故障率串联模型下计算首次故障时间；区分MTTF与修复系统的MTBF。

先记回答顺序

  1. 识别series、independent、constant-rate和first failure四个条件
  2. 用λ=1/MTTF将两个平均时间换成h⁻¹
  3. 加故障率后取倒数，写666.67h并检查小于各组件时间
  4. 解释MTTF首次故障、MTBF重复故障间隔和缺少恢复资料的边界

结果与条件

题设独立、恒定故障率且无冗余，故λ=1/1000+1/2000=0.0015每小时，系统MTTF≈666.67小时。加的是故障率，不是平均寿命；没有修复信息不能算可用性。

起句提示：Under the stated independent, constant-rate series model, the system failure rate is 1/1000 + 1/2000 = 0.0015 per hour.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Under the stated independent, constant-rate series model, the system failure rate is 1/1000 + 1/2000 = 0.0015 per hour. Its mean time to first failure is approximately 666.67 hours. Add failure rates, not mean lifetimes. MTTF describes time to failure; MTBF for a repairable service needs a defined operating and restoration model. Availability cannot be inferred from these values alone.

步骤必须会独立重做

1. 题目给定：任一组件首次故障则服务停止；组件独立且恒定故障率，不考虑冗余和修复。 2. λ₁=1/1000=0.001 h⁻¹；λ₂=1/2000=0.0005 h⁻¹。 3. 串联系统 λsys=λ₁+λ₂=0.0015 h⁻¹。 4. 本模型 MTTFsys=1/λsys≈666.67 h，比任一单组件MTTF短，符合直觉。 5. MTTF是mean time to failure（首次故障）；课程MTBF/MTBCF用于故障间隔，涉及修复/重复运行时需定义恢复模型，不能无条件混称。没有MTTR与恢复定义，不能求availability。

不能丢的条件

求和的是failure rates，不是平均时间；首次故障MTTF与修复系统MTBF需区分，冗余/相关故障另建模型。

闭卷验收

先口头用中文解释，再手写或打出英文；对照上面的回答顺序，逐点检查。另换一组数字独立计算并校验。

回本题深入解析与Moodle定位 →

T033 · Availability · 36词

题目：A service has MTBF of 990 hours and MTTR of 10 hours. Find availability and two ways to improve it.

计算可用性并提出两类改进。

先记回答顺序

  1. 统一MTBF与MTTR单位
  2. 写A=MTBF/(MTBF+MTTR)并代入
  3. 转为百分比
  4. 分别解释减少故障和缩短恢复，再检查改进值

结果与条件

A=990/(990+10)=99%。减少故障可提高MTBF，缩短检测与修复可降低MTTR；这两条都能提高availability，前提是统计口径一致。

起句提示：Availability is 990/(990+10) = 99%.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Availability is 990/(990+10) = 99%. It can be improved by increasing time between failures or reducing restoration time. The calculation requires consistent units and a clear definition of the failures and restoration periods being counted.

步骤必须会独立重做

A=990/(990+10)=0.99=99%。两个时间均为小时。 减少故障提高MTBF；缩短检测、备件等待、修复测试降低MTTR。 若MTTR降至5，A=990/995≈99.4975%。这不是把可用性直接“提高5%”。

不能丢的条件

不能用990/10；明确是否含计划维护。

闭卷验收

T034 · 可用性到停机预算 · 39词

题目：If measured availability is 99.9% over a 30-day period, how much downtime is allowed?

30天、99.9%的停机预算。

先记回答顺序

  1. 先求观察窗口总时间
  2. 把availability转成不可用比例
  3. 总时间乘不可用比例
  4. 换算分钟/秒，写计入维护的假设

结果与条件

30天有720小时；不可用0.1%，所以720×0.001=0.72小时=43.2分钟。合同若排除维护时间或另定观察窗口，结果要重算。

起句提示：For a 30-day measurement window, 99.9% availability permits 0.1% downtime.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

For a 30-day measurement window, 99.9% availability permits 0.1% downtime. That is 0.72 hours or 43.2 minutes. The result assumes that the full period is included in the measurement and uses the stated maintenance rules.

步骤必须会独立重做

观察窗口30×24=720小时；不可用比例1−0.999=0.001。 停机720×0.001=0.72小时=43.2分钟=43分12秒。 这个换算以该窗口所有计入时间为分母；若合同排除计划维护，应按合同另算。 同样总停机可以集中一次或分散多次，业务体验不同。

不能丢的条件

99.9%并不自动说明单次中断最大允许时长。

先口头用中文解释，再手写或打出英文；对照上面的回答顺序，逐点检查。另换一组数字独立计算并校验。

T035 · One-way delay 与 RTT 的计算和条件 · 47词

题目：A forward path has 12 ms propagation, 8 ms transmission, 5 ms processing and 15 ms queuing delay. The complete return path takes 30 ms. Endpoint turnaround is 10 ms. Find forward one-way delay and RTT. Can an RTT limit of 100 ms alone guarantee at most 50 ms in each direction?

使用互不重复的已给延迟项求单程和往返；检查“除二”假设。

先记回答顺序

  1. 前向四项求和
  2. 按本题测量定义加turnaround与return得到RTT
  3. 检查单位
  4. 用不对称路径反例判断RTT能否限制每向时延

结果与条件

前向四项之和40ms；加回程30ms和端点10ms得RTT80ms。即使RTT≤100ms，单向仍可能超过50ms，因为两方向未必对称。

起句提示：The forward one-way delay is 40 ms.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The forward one-way delay is 40 ms. Including the stated return path and endpoint turnaround, RTT is 80 ms. An RTT limit of 100 ms alone does not guarantee a 50 ms bound in each direction. The paths may be asymmetric and endpoint processing may also contribute.

步骤必须会独立重做

题中四项已规定为前向路径各项总量，不再按router数重复相乘。 前向one-way=12+8+5+15=40ms。 本题测量定义的RTT=前向40+终端turnaround10+回向30=80ms。 单独知道RTT≤100ms不能推出每向≤50ms。例如前向70、回向20、turnaround10仍RTT100。 仅在题中额外假定两向相等、端点处理可忽略，或明确分配预算时，才可用对称近似；它是额外假设，不是RTT定义。

不能丢的条件

duration是事务持续时间；不是这里的数据包路径delay。RTT与单程的测量端点须定义清楚。

闭卷验收

先口头用中文解释，再手写或打出英文；对照上面的回答顺序，逐点检查。另换一组数字独立计算并校验。

T138 · INTD、HRT 与传播时延的边界 · 155词

题目：Explain INTD, HRT and distance-related propagation delay in the lecture. How should the lecture’s example thresholds be used?

理解课件中的任务与感知时限，避免把示例当统一网络时延标准。

先记回答顺序

  1. 说明INTD针对任务交互容忍、HRT针对感知
  2. 列课件示例量级并明确并非通用标准
  3. 指出该页不等式与数值矛盾，不照背
  4. 区分传播贡献与完整单程、RTT或整任务指标

第1块

INTD谈互动任务多久后变得不合用，课件约10–30秒是某种任务语境；HRT约100ms谈人何时注意到延迟。

起句提示：The lecture uses interactivity, human response and distance to discuss delay requirements.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The lecture uses interactivity, human response and distance to discuss delay requirements. Interaction delay, INTD, concerns how long an interactive task can take before it becomes unsuitable for its purpose. The lecture gives a rough 10–30 second context. Human response time, HRT, concerns the point at which a user notices delay, with about 100 ms used as a guide.

第2块

两个数讨论不同事件与感知，课件不一致不等式不能当普遍法则；传播时延来自信号必须跨越距离。

起句提示：These values refer to different task and perception contexts.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

These values refer to different task and perception contexts. They are not universal limits for every application, and the inconsistent inequality on the slide should not be memorized as a general rule. Propagation delay is different again: a signal needs time to travel through a medium, so distance creates a physical contribution to delay.

第3块

设计时定义事件、端点、容忍时间，并说明单向、往返或整任务；实时媒体可能要求更紧。

起句提示：A designer should specify the application event, endpoints and permitted completion or response time.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A designer should specify the application event, endpoints and permitted completion or response time. Real-time media may need much tighter limits than a longer interactive task. State whether a requirement is one-way, round-trip or whole-task time before choosing mechanisms or comparing measurements.

不能丢的条件

Week4 p53列INTD约10–30秒、HRT约100ms，却有反向不等式；不要背矛盾关系。实际要求需定义事件、端点、单程/往返/整任务。

T036 · 数据速率、并发与单位 · 39词

题目：A 60 MB file must transfer in 20 seconds. Estimate the payload rate and the total for five simultaneous transfers.

数据量/时间与并发计算，MB采用十进制。

先记回答顺序

  1. 说明MB/Mb及十进制口径
  2. 数据量×8÷时间求payload rate
  3. 确认同向、同瓶颈、同时后乘并发数
  4. 补协议开销、余量与端系统约束

结果与条件

60×8/20=24 Mb/s；5个同方向同时进行则至少120 Mb/s payload。协议开销和余量尚未计入；若单位是MiB，要按题意另算。

起句提示：Each transfer needs an average payload rate of 24 Mb/s.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Each transfer needs an average payload rate of 24 Mb/s. Five simultaneous transfers need 120 Mb/s of aggregate payload capacity on the shared direction, before protocol overhead and planning margin. This assumes decimal MB and overlapping transfers.

步骤必须会独立重做

本题规定1MB=10^6 bytes，1Mb/s=10^6 bits/s。 60×10^6×8/20=24×10^6bits/s=24Mb/s。 5个确实同时、均要同样进度且共享同方向瓶颈：5×24=120Mb/s payload。 另需协议overhead与合理余量；不能把100Mb/s链路当够。 若题中写MiB或规定1024制，要按该口径换算；不默默替换单位。

不能丢的条件

不要把60MB/20s=3MB/s误写成3Mb/s。

闭卷验收

先口头用中文解释，再手写或打出英文；对照上面的回答顺序，逐点检查。另换一组数字独立计算并校验。

T037 · 补充性能需求 · 130词

题目：Explain operational suitability, supportability and confidence.

解释三项补充性能需求。

先记回答顺序

  1. operational suitability判断客户能否运行
  2. supportability说明持续维护所需资源
  3. confidence按本课说明按所需速率且无不可接受错丢的交付能力
  4. 给人员培训和备件例子

第1块

Operational suitability问客户能否操作设计好的网络，既看技术也看人员技能。

起句提示：Operational suitability asks whether the customer can operate the planned network.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Operational suitability asks whether the customer can operate the planned network. It depends on both the design and the skills of the people who will use and run it.

第2块

Supportability问交付后能否长期维护，需RMA、培训、程序文档、工具及备件。

起句提示：Supportability asks what is needed to maintain effective operation after delivery.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Supportability asks what is needed to maintain effective operation after delivery. The course identifies RMA, workforce and training, procedures and documentation, tools, and spare or repair parts. A system that performs well on installation day may still be hard to support later.

第3块

Confidence在本课指按需速率、无不可接受错误或丢失地传数据的可信能力。高规格设备若团队不会维护仍可能不合适。

起句提示：Confidence, in this course, concerns the network’s ability to deliver data at the required rate without unacceptable error or loss.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Confidence, in this course, concerns the network’s ability to deliver data at the required rate without unacceptable error or loss. The acceptable level depends on the application. These requirements extend the design beyond nominal link speed. For example, a complex device may offer high performance but require training, maintenance procedures and spare parts that the organization cannot currently provide.

不能丢的条件

confidence 在此不是统计置信区间，也不是用户自信。

T038 · Flow 的定义与建立 · 126词

题目：What is a traffic flow, and how is it developed from requirements?

定义 flow 并说明建立步骤。

先记回答顺序

  1. 定义flow的共同属性和端到端范围
  2. 从应用需求与位置找到source/sink
  3. 应用flow model并形成flowspec
  4. 区分individual、composite、critical与flow map用途

第1块

Flow是具有共同源、目的、方向、信息类型或服务要求的一组网络流量。

起句提示：A traffic flow is a set of network traffic with common attributes, such as source, destination, information type and direction.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A traffic flow is a set of network traffic with common attributes, such as source, destination, information type and direction. It is an end-to-end entity that can be associated with an application, device or user.

第2块

先从应用需求确定谁与谁通信、在哪里、发什么、以什么速率及时限发送，可分个别、组合或关键流。

起句提示：To develop flows, identify application requirements and locations, locate data sources and sinks, apply suitable flow models and combine performance requirements into flow specifications.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

To develop flows, identify application requirements and locations, locate data sources and sinks, apply suitable flow models and combine performance requirements into flow specifications. A source produces data, while a sink receives it. The same device may perform both roles.

第3块

Flow图为后续容量与机制选择提供依据；不能只数总流量而不看方向和位置。

起句提示：An individual flow represents a single application session.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

An individual flow represents a single application session. A composite flow combines flows sharing a link, path or network. A critical flow has particular importance or strict requirements. A flow map connects requirements to places in the network. This helps reveal shared paths, bottlenecks and where service mechanisms must be applied.

不能丢的条件

不要把 flow 当成任意一条物理线；同一设备可同时 source/sink。

闭卷验收
