T088 · Resource control 与 traffic conditioning · 133词

题目：Explain prioritization, admission control and traffic conditioning.

解释资源控制和流量调节。

先记回答顺序

  1. 区分prioritization与admission control
  2. 按classification、metering、shaping、dropping说明动作
  3. 比较延迟、buffer与丢失影响
  4. 依据应用容忍度和policy选择

第1块

Resource control决定有限资源分给谁：prioritization排先后，admission control在资源不足时可拒绝新flow。

起句提示：Resource control determines how limited network resources are used.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Resource control determines how limited network resources are used. Prioritization ranks traffic according to importance or urgency. Admission control may refuse a new flow when accepting it would prevent existing commitments from being met.

第2块

Traffic conditioning先分类、计量速率/突发，再整形等待或丢弃超标流量。

起句提示：Traffic conditioning includes classification, metering, shaping and dropping.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Traffic conditioning includes classification, metering, shaping and dropping. Classification identifies the traffic class. Metering measures rates or bursts and compares them with a profile, such as an SLA. Shaping delays packets to make a flow fit an allowed profile. Dropping discards selected packets.

第3块

Shaping平滑流量但增加排队和缓冲需求；dropping保护其他流却损害被丢流；要按应用容忍度选择。

起句提示：These mechanisms have different effects.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

These mechanisms have different effects. Shaping can smooth traffic but adds waiting time and needs buffers. Dropping may protect other traffic but harms the discarded flow. The designer should choose behaviour according to application tolerance and policy. For example, delaying a flexible backup may be acceptable, while the same delay could make an interactive application unusable.

不能丢的条件

metering只测量，不自动等于限速；shaping与dropping不同。

T089 · Metering、shaping 与逐包等待 · 59词

题目：A meter observes 200 packets/s at 1500 bytes/packet. A traffic profile allows 1.5 Mb/s. A shaper is explicitly configured to release 100 packets/s. Calculate the rates and discuss whether adding a fixed 10 ms delay to every packet necessarily produces the same output rate.

复算流量速率，并区分整体输出调度与每包固定延迟。

先记回答顺序

  1. 输入packet rate×bytes×8求bit rate
  2. 与profile上限比较
  3. 按明确配置输出100packet/s求输出速率
  4. 由profile反求理想包率
  5. 区分固定延时与包释放间隔

结果与条件

先按题设的服务间隔列每包到达与可发送时刻，再求逐包等待和最终离开时间。不能把平均速率直接当每包等待，且要检查队列与单位。

起句提示：The input payload rate is 2.4 Mb/s and exceeds the 1.5 Mb/s profile.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The input payload rate is 2.4 Mb/s and exceeds the 1.5 Mb/s profile. The explicitly configured output rate is 1.2 Mb/s. Delaying every packet by a fixed 10 ms does not necessarily halve throughput; the output rate depends on how release times are scheduled. Shaping controls that schedule and can add queuing delay.

步骤必须会独立重做

输入200×1500×8=2,400,000bit/s=2.4Mb/s，超过1.5Mb/s。 明确配置后的输出100×1500×8=1.2Mb/s，低于1.5Mb/s。 保持1500byte等长包，理想最大平均输出包率1,500,000/(1500×8)=125packet/s；未计额外overhead。 不能仅凭“每个包额外等待10ms”推导成100packet/s：并行/流水处理时，整体平移10ms并不改变包间间隔。只有额外明确每10ms至多释放一个包等服务节奏，才有该输出频率。课件p.44的例子说明shaping降速，数值解释需保留调度假设。

不能丢的条件

10ms＝0.01s，但1/0.01得到packet/s需要“一次服务一个包”等明确条件。

闭卷验收

先口头用中文解释，再手写或打出英文；对照上面的回答顺序，逐点检查。另换一组数字独立计算并校验。

回本题深入解析与Moodle定位 →

T090 · Queue 与 scheduling · 133词

题目：Compare FIFO, priority, round robin and weighted fair queuing.

比较排队与调度方法。

先记回答顺序

  1. 先区分queuing存包与scheduling选包
  2. 按FIFO、priority、RR、WFQ说明服务顺序
  3. 比较延迟、公平性与starvation风险
  4. 另说明RED/WRED属于queue management并补buffer取舍

第1块

Queue存等待的packet，scheduling决定下一包/队列何时被服务，两者功能不同。

起句提示：Queuing stores packets while they wait for processing or transmission.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Queuing stores packets while they wait for processing or transmission. Scheduling decides which waiting packet or queue is served next. These are related functions, but they are not identical.

第2块

FIFO按到达顺序；strict priority可减高类时延却可能饿死低类；round robin轮流；WFQ按权重分配。

起句提示：FIFO serves packets in arrival order and is simple.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

FIFO serves packets in arrival order and is simple. Strict priority serves higher-priority traffic first, which can reduce delay for that class but may leave lower-priority traffic waiting under sustained load. Round robin cycles through queues and serves each in turn. Weighted fair queuing assigns different shares according to configured weights.

第3块

RED/WRED等管理策略可在缓冲满前提前丢部分包，以控制拥塞；选法应跟随服务目标与公平要求。

起句提示：Queue management, including RED or WRED, can discard packets before a buffer becomes completely full to help control congestion.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Queue management, including RED or WRED, can discard packets before a buffer becomes completely full to help control congestion. The choice should reflect service priorities and fairness. A larger buffer may absorb a burst but can also increase delay. Test behaviour under mixed traffic rather than assuming one queue policy meets every requirement.

不能丢的条件

queue management、scheduling是相关但不同层面的机制。

闭卷验收

T091 · SLA、policy 与 QoS 的闭环 · 130词

题目：Explain how SLAs, policies and QoS work together.

解释承诺、规则、执行和验证的联系。

先记回答顺序

  1. SLA明确目标、责任和测量条件
  2. policy把组织目标转成规则
  3. QoS在设备落实处理
  4. monitoring对照承诺并反馈调整

第1块

SLA写服务目标和提供方责任，可约定速率、突发、上下行、时延与可用性等条件。

起句提示：A service level agreement, or SLA, records expected service and the provider’s responsibilities.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A service level agreement, or SLA, records expected service and the provider’s responsibilities. Performance elements may include data rate, burst tolerance, upstream and downstream service, delay and RMA. A useful SLA also needs clear conditions and measurement methods.

第2块

Policy把组织目标写成资源、访问和优先级规则，QoS机制在设备上执行相关待遇。

起句提示：Policies connect the organization’s management goals with rules about resources, access and service priorities.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Policies connect the organization’s management goals with rules about resources, access and service priorities. QoS mechanisms enforce relevant treatment at network devices. Monitoring then checks whether the delivered service matches the commitment and provides feedback for adjustment.

第3块

如教学流有约定时延与容量，配置后还要沿路径测量，比较SLA；未达标就回查政策、资源和设备。

起句提示：For example, a teaching service may have an agreed delay target and capacity level.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

For example, a teaching service may have an agreed delay target and capacity level. A policy gives it suitable priority, devices implement that policy, and measurements verify the result. An SLA document alone does not reserve resources. Equally, enabling QoS without a clear goal does not show that users are receiving the service they need.

不能丢的条件

承诺不能超出机制和资源；上/下行需分别定义。

闭卷验收

先口头用中文解释，再手写或打出英文；对照上面的回答顺序，逐点检查。再做题库中的变式，确认能按新问法重组。

T142 · 性能机制放置与组件之间的取舍 · 158词

题目：How should performance mechanisms be placed, and how do performance architecture, management and security interact?

说明逐流/聚合机制位置，管理验证与安全处理成本。

先记回答顺序

  1. 从flow map决定逐流或聚合处理位置
  2. 解释细粒度、扩展性与运行成本的内部取舍
  3. 说明management配置/监测验证，security增加处理成本
  4. 用教学与backup混合负载给出测试内容

第1块

机制放置要看需求、flow图、目标和运行环境；靠近接入处可细分流，骨干处可能更适合按类处理。

起句提示：Performance mechanisms should follow requirements, flow maps, goals and the operating environment.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Performance mechanisms should follow requirements, flow maps, goals and the operating environment. Detailed treatment of individual flows may be useful near access points, while aggregate treatment can reduce complexity where many flows meet. These are placement guidelines rather than mandatory locations for every network.

第2块

内部取舍是细节程度、可扩展性和运维成本；机制要对目标必要且在预期条件下够用。

起句提示：Internal choices must balance service detail, scalability and operating effort.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Internal choices must balance service detail, scalability and operating effort. A mechanism should be necessary for the goal and sufficient under the expected conditions. The complete path still needs appropriate resources.

第3块

Management负责配置和测量却也占用资源；security检查可能增加处理与时延，routing改变路径也会影响效果。

起句提示：Management configures mechanisms, observes performance and verifies service commitments.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Management configures mechanisms, observes performance and verifies service commitments. Its own measurements consume traffic and processing resources. Security inspection and encryption can also consume capacity and add delay. Increasing link bandwidth alone may not remove a processing bottleneck.

第4块

可给教学流优先级、备份整形，并在关键边界监测；然后用端到端测试检查收益与副作用。

起句提示：For example, an interactive teaching flow can receive controlled priority while backups are shaped, with monitoring at relevant boundaries.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

For example, an interactive teaching flow can receive controlled priority while backups are shaped, with monitoring at relevant boundaries. The designer should then test delay, loss, useful access and recovery under realistic load. Documenting these relationships makes trade-offs visible and connects performance claims to evidence.

不能丢的条件

management配置并验证QoS，也占资源；security处理影响delay/capacity。测试需包括混合负载、允许访问与恢复，不能只测一条空闲链路。

T092 · 管理层级与 FCAPS · 129词

题目：Describe the network management hierarchy and the FCAPS model.

说明管理层级与FCAPS功能。

先记回答顺序

  1. 按business→service→network→element→network-element列层级
  2. 解释FCAPS五功能
  3. 说明层级与功能是不同观察维度
  4. 用服务目标落到设备动作的例子连接

第1块

网络管理从business、service、network、element group到individual element逐层细化，分别看预算目标、交付服务、整体网络、设备群和单设备。

起句提示：The course views network management as several levels.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The course views network management as several levels. Business management handles budgets, resources and planning. Service management concerns delivered services, such as access bandwidth and application delivery. Network management covers the network as a whole. Element management handles groups of similar devices, while network-element management concerns an individual device.

第2块

FCAPS是fault、configuration、accounting、performance、security五类管理工作，关注故障、设置、用量、性能和安全。

起句提示：FCAPS describes five management areas: fault, configuration, accounting, performance and security management.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

FCAPS describes five management areas: fault, configuration, accounting, performance and security management. These include detecting faults, controlling settings, tracking usage, measuring service and managing protection.

第3块

管理层级与FCAPS可结合：教学可靠的业务目标要变成服务目标，再落实成网络与设备测量和行动。

起句提示：The levels and functions connect high-level goals with concrete device parameters.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The levels and functions connect high-level goals with concrete device parameters. For example, a business commitment to reliable teaching leads to a service target, network monitoring and configuration, and specific interface or device actions. Management must also transport and store the information needed to make those decisions, so its own traffic and infrastructure need planning.

不能丢的条件

层级是管理范围，FCAPS是管理功能，不是同一分类。

T093 · SNMP、CMIP 与 CMOT · 136词

题目：Compare SNMP with CMIP/CMOT and explain their emphasis in this course.

比较管理协议，并保留课程的主次。

先记回答顺序

  1. 说明SNMP的manager/agent/MIB及poll/trap
  2. 说明CMIP的OSI背景和功能复杂度
  3. 解释CMOT全称
  4. 比较能力与复杂度，并标明本课重点和历史背景

第1块

SNMP让manager从设备agent收集或配置定义好的管理对象，MIB组织对象，trap可主动报告事件；课程重点学SNMP。

起句提示：SNMP provides mechanisms for collecting and configuring information from managed network devices.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

SNMP provides mechanisms for collecting and configuring information from managed network devices. Managers use defined objects, grouped in MIBs, and agents can send unsolicited event notifications through traps. The course identifies SNMP as the main protocol to study.

第2块

CMIP属OSI管理方法，操作较多但复杂；CMOT是CMIP Over TCP/IP，本课主要作历史背景。

起句提示：CMIP, the Common Management Information Protocol, is associated with the OSI management approach.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

CMIP, the Common Management Information Protocol, is associated with the OSI management approach. It supports parameter collection and setting and provides a wider range of operation types, but it is more complicated. CMOT means CMIP Over TCP/IP. The lecture treats these protocols mainly as historical background.

第3块

功能多不自动更合适，还要看任务、设备支持与运维；MIB定义对象，不是保存历史测量的数据库。

起句提示：The comparison explains a trade-off between capability and complexity.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The comparison explains a trade-off between capability and complexity. More operation types do not automatically make a management system more suitable. The required tasks, device support and operating effort still matter. Also distinguish a MIB, which defines managed objects, from the storage system that keeps the actual history of collected measurements.

不能丢的条件

低优先级历史内容不标必考；CMOT不是SNMP的新版本。

T094 · SNMP、agent 与 MIB · 135词

题目：Explain the relationship between SNMP, agents, managers and MIBs.

解释管理协议、代理、管理者与对象定义。

先记回答顺序

  1. manager通过SNMP与device agent通信
  2. MIB定义对象及含义
  3. 说明poll与trap及counter怎样得到rate
  4. 补SNMPv3和按任务选对象的条件

第1块

SNMP管理系统与设备上的agent通信，读取/设置管理信息；agent向管理方呈现设备对象。

起句提示：SNMP supports the collection and configuration of management information from network devices.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

SNMP supports the collection and configuration of management information from network devices. A management system communicates with an agent on a managed device. The agent provides access to managed objects and may send event notifications.

第2块

MIB定义对象名称和含义，例如接口计数器；它不是所有历史数据的存储库。

起句提示：A Management Information Base, or MIB, defines the managed objects and their meaning.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A Management Information Base, or MIB, defines the managed objects and their meaning. It is not the same as a historical database of every collected measurement. For example, MIB-II includes interface counters such as received and transmitted octets and error counts.

第3块

Manager可主动poll，设备可用trap主动报事件；counter要用两次差值和时间间隔才能推速率。

起句提示：Managers can poll for values, while traps provide unsolicited event notification.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Managers can poll for values, while traps provide unsolicited event notification. Counter values often need processing: a rate normally comes from the change in a counter over a known interval, not from the total counter alone. SNMPv3 adds security capabilities, but settings and access restrictions still need to be planned. Choose objects that support actual service and operational needs.

不能丢的条件

counter累计字节不是瞬时bps；MIB不是性能历史数据库本身。

T143 · MIB-II 的计数器选择与解释 · 152词

题目：Choose MIB-II interface counters for traffic volume, packet counts and errors. Explain how to derive a rate and why counters need careful interpretation.

按问题选对象，区分累计值、速率、octet和packet。

先记回答顺序

  1. 按In/Out及Octets/Packets/Errors选择MIB对象
  2. 用两次同对象读数差×8/秒求bit rate
  3. 检查reset、wrap、时间和兼容分母
  4. 区分SNMPv2 bulk操作与v3安全，按管理目标采集

第1块

MIB-II不同接口对象计入/出octet、单播/非单播包和错误；octet是8bit，选对对象才能回答目标问题。

起句提示：MIB-II interface objects describe different aspects of a device interface.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

MIB-II interface objects describe different aspects of a device interface. ifInOctets and ifOutOctets count received and transmitted octets. An octet is eight bits. Unicast and non-unicast packet counters describe packet numbers, while ifInErrors and ifOutErrors record relevant interface errors. These counters answer different questions.

第2块

累计counter不是速率；两次octet差×8÷经过秒数才得bit/s，错误率也需匹配的包数分母。

起句提示：A cumulative value is not a rate.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A cumulative value is not a rate. For example, subtracting two octet readings gives the transferred amount during the interval; multiplying by eight and dividing by elapsed seconds gives bits per second. State the interface, direction, timestamps and units.

第3块

读取前后检查设备重启、回卷和口径变化，避免把counter变小直接解释为流量下降。

起句提示：Before trusting the result, check for device reset, counter wrap and changes in what is being measured.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Before trusting the result, check for device reset, counter wrap and changes in what is being measured. A packet-error ratio also needs a compatible packet-count denominator and a defined interpretation. Bulk retrieval belongs to SNMPv2 protocol operations; it should not be memorized as a feature introduced only by SNMPv3. SNMPv3’s security capabilities are a separate issue. Collect the objects needed for the management goal rather than every available counter.

不能丢的条件

两次同对象counter需检查reset/wrap，error ratio需兼容packet分母。GETBULK在SNMPv2已有，SNMPv3重点安全，避免按课件误记版本。

闭卷验收

T095 · Monitoring、instrumentation、configuration · 129词

题目：Distinguish monitoring, instrumentation and configuration mechanisms.

区分观察、采集手段与改变设置。

先记回答顺序

  1. monitoring说明采集处理显示归档
  2. instrumentation说明工具与访问能力
  3. configuration说明修改参数
  4. 用检测拥塞→改queue policy说明配合与测量边界

第1块

Monitoring收集、分析、展示和归档网络表现，可观察事件、趋势、容量或服务。

起句提示：Monitoring obtains and processes information about network behaviour.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Monitoring obtains and processes information about network behaviour. It includes collection, event or trend analysis, display and archiving. Measurements may concern an element, link, network or end-to-end path.

第2块

Instrumentation提供取得信息的工具和接入，如SNMP、counter、探针、抓包、ping或traceroute。

起句提示：Instrumentation provides the tools and access needed to obtain that information.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Instrumentation provides the tools and access needed to obtain that information. Examples include SNMP access, counters, probes, packet capture, ping and traceroute. Accuracy and dependability matter, so measurements from different locations may need comparison.

第3块

Configuration改变实际运行参数，可依据监测结果调整；能观察不代表能配置，改完要再监测。

起句提示：Configuration changes operational parameters through direct access, remote access or configuration files.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Configuration changes operational parameters through direct access, remote access or configuration files. It can implement a response to information found by monitoring. For example, instrumentation supplies interface data, monitoring detects sustained congestion, and configuration changes an appropriate queue policy. These mechanisms form a practical management loop. Observing a problem does not itself fix it, and a successful connectivity probe does not establish every aspect of service quality.

不能丢的条件

ping成功不证明吞吐、全部应用、所有路径都正常。

T096 · Event notification 与 trend analysis · 124词

题目：Compare event notification with trend analysis and explain polling trade-offs.

比较事件通知和趋势分析。

先记回答顺序

  1. 按时间尺度和目的比较event与trend
  2. event用来及时发现变化，trend建立baseline和看增长
  3. 比较短/长poll interval的分辨率与开销
  4. 说明组合使用和时间单位上下文

第1块

Event notification针对故障或越阈值等短期事件，短暂问题可能需要较密采样或主动告警。

起句提示：Event notification identifies changes that need attention, such as failures, threshold crossings or important informational events.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Event notification identifies changes that need attention, such as failures, threshold crossings or important informational events. Short-lived problems may require frequent collection and prompt alarms, logs or displays.

第2块

Trend analysis用持续一致的数据建基线，看利用率、时延和可用性的长期变化，支持容量规划。

起句提示：Trend analysis studies longer-term behaviour.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Trend analysis studies longer-term behaviour. Continuous and consistent measurements establish a baseline and reveal changes in utilization, delay or availability. This supports planning before service becomes unacceptable.

第3块

日均值可能藏短故障，零碎警报也看不出增长；轮询越密越及时但流量与处理成本越高。

起句提示：The methods use different time scales.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The methods use different time scales. A daily average may hide a brief outage, while a stream of isolated alarms may not show gradual growth. Shorter polling intervals improve time resolution but create more traffic, processing and stored data. Longer intervals reduce overhead but may miss short events. A useful design combines event and trend information and records timestamps, units and context so that measurements can be interpreted correctly.

不能丢的条件

实时分析与长期平均不能互相代替；poll越频繁并非无成本。

T097 · Polling 带宽 · 41词

题目：For 100 devices, 4 interfaces each and 8 parameters per interface, each value is 8 bytes and, for this exercise, incurs its own 60 bytes of transmission overhead. Poll every 5 seconds. Find average traffic.

复算课件管理流量例子。

先记回答顺序

  1. 设备×接口×参数求每轮测量数
  2. value+wire overhead求每项bytes
  3. 乘8并除poll interval得平均bit/s
  4. 若问日量再乘日轮次
  5. 区分平均与同步突发

结果与条件

先算一次轮询的请求加回复字节，再乘设备、接口、参数和次数，转bit/s。题给是否含协议开销必须看清，平均负载不能代替突发峰值。

起句提示：Each polling cycle generates 1,740,800 bits.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Each polling cycle generates 1,740,800 bits. With a five-second interval, average management traffic is 348,160 bit/s, or 348.16 kb/s. The average is not the peak rate; synchronized polling can create a much larger short-term burst.

步骤必须会独立重做

测量数100×4×8=3200/cycle。 每项wire数据8+60=68bytes；每轮3200×68=217600bytes=1740800bits。 平均1740800/5=348160bit/s=348.16kb/s（十进制）。 日轮次86400/5=17280；日wire traffic=30081024000bits≈30.081Gb。 轮询同步突发可能显著高于平均；精确瞬时速率还需发送持续时间。

不能丢的条件

不能把1.7408Mb/cycle直接写1.7408Mb/s，除非说明1秒发完。

闭卷验收

先口头用中文解释，再手写或打出英文；对照上面的回答顺序，逐点检查。另换一组数字独立计算并校验。

T098 · 存储量与counter速率 · 40词

题目：Using T097 (100 devices, 4 interfaces per device, 8 parameters per interface, polled every 5 seconds), store only the 8-byte values. Find daily storage. Separately, a byte counter rises from 1,000,000 to 1,750,000 in 10 s; find the rate.

区分线上流量、存储和累计计数器。

先记回答顺序

  1. 存储只用实际保留的value bytes
  2. 乘每轮数量和86400/interval
  3. 另题以counter差×8/时间求bit rate
  4. 核对metadata、复制和counter重置/回卷

结果与条件

按记录数×每条存储字节×采样次数求总量；网络传输开销与数据库存储开销要分开。Counter速率需两次采样差值与时间，且检查重置或回卷。

起句提示：Raw daily value storage is 442.368 MB, before metadata or database overhead.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Raw daily value storage is 442.368 MB, before metadata or database overhead. Counter-based rate uses the difference between samples: (1,750,000−1,000,000)×8/10 = 600 kb/s. Validate the counter and interval before trusting the result.

步骤必须会独立重做

日storage=3200×8×17280=442368000bytes=442.368MB（不含metadata/index/复制）。 年按365天≈161.46432GB。课件“720轮/day”应为每hour；日轮次为17280。 Counter delta=750000bytes；速率750000×8/10=600000bit/s=600kb/s。 先检查counter重置/回卷，确认采样点与时间一致。

不能丢的条件

不要把wire的60byte overhead计入“只存8byte值”的问题。

T099 · 带内与带外网络管理 · 151词

题目：What are differences between in-band and out-of-band approaches in network management?

网络管理中的带内与带外方法有什么区别？

先记回答顺序

  1. 先按路径是否与用户共享比较
  2. 再按业务网故障时的访问能力比较
  3. 再写成本、复杂度与安全条件
  4. 说明带外仍有供电等限制，可按需求混合

第1块

In-band管理与普通业务共用网络路径，省独立设施，但同样受拥塞或断网影响，最需要排错时可能进不去。

起句提示：In-band management sends management traffic through the same network paths used by users and their applications.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

In-band management sends management traffic through the same network paths used by users and their applications. It normally avoids the cost of a separate management network. However, management traffic competes for shared resources and may be affected by the same congestion or failures as user traffic. Administrators may lose access when they most need to investigate a problem.

第2块

Out-of-band提供替代管理路径，可在主业务路径故障时提供观察和恢复，但需额外建设与安全控制。

起句提示：Out-of-band management provides an alternative path for management traffic, often through a separate management network.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Out-of-band management provides an alternative path for management traffic, often through a separate management network. It can support monitoring and recovery during many failures of the user network. It also adds equipment, cost and operational complexity. Both approaches still require appropriate access protection.

第3块

它也不能保证所有故障下可用，例如设备完全断电；可日常用带内、关键恢复用替代路径，并测试。

起句提示：Out-of-band access is not guaranteed during every failure, for example if a device loses all power.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Out-of-band access is not guaranteed during every failure, for example if a device loses all power. A hybrid design can use in-band access for routine management and an alternative path for recovery. The choice should follow availability needs, risk and budget, rather than assuming that one approach is always best.

不能丢的条件

比较维度不对齐；把带外说成永不失效；把in-band/out-of-band等同于centralized/distributed。

闭卷验收

先口头用中文解释，再手写或打出英文；对照上面的回答顺序，逐点检查。再做题库中的变式，确认能按新问法重组。

T100 · Centralized、distributed、hierarchical management · 132词

题目：Compare centralized, distributed and hierarchical network management.

比较三种管理架构。

先记回答顺序

  1. 按信息/控制放置比较集中、分布、层级
  2. 逐项写管理便利、汇聚故障、扩展和协调代价
  3. 给多校园组织方式
  4. 说明冗余与通信可达性条件

第1块

Centralized在主要系统汇聚管理和数据，易统一但可能集中流量和故障风险。

起句提示：Centralized management collects control and information at a main management system.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Centralized management collects control and information at a main management system. It can simplify administration and provide a common view, but it can concentrate traffic and create a significant failure point unless protected.

第2块

Distributed在多个位置处理本地信息，利于规模与本地响应，但协调和一致性更复杂；hierarchical分层汇总。

起句提示：Distributed management places systems across different domains or locations.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Distributed management places systems across different domains or locations. Local processing can improve scalability and reduce dependence on a single site, but coordination and consistent policy become harder. Hierarchical management separates responsibilities across levels, allowing local collection and higher-level summaries or control.

第3块

按规模、地点、可靠、人员与成本选择；多校区可本地采集、中心总览，故障时保留本地能力。

起句提示：Choose the architecture according to network size, geography, reliability, staffing and cost.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Choose the architecture according to network size, geography, reliability, staffing and cost. For example, a multi-campus network may benefit from local collection with a central service view. Distribution does not automatically remove every failure risk, and centralization does not forbid redundancy. State where information is collected, stored and acted on, and how those management systems remain reachable.

不能丢的条件

centralized≠必然一台物理主机；logical集中可有冗余。

闭卷验收

T101 · Management traffic scaling · 133词

题目：How should management traffic and storage be estimated and controlled?

如何估计与控制管理开销？

先记回答顺序

  1. 用device×interfaces×parameters得到每轮测量数
  2. 乘每项wire bytes并除interval求速率
  3. 存储按保留内容和retention另算
  4. 用减少无用采集、分布和聚合控制负载并保留时效

第1块

先数设备、接口、参数、一次值及协议开销和轮询周期；每轮bit数除周期得平均管理带宽。

起句提示：Estimate the number of devices, interfaces per device, parameters per interface and polling frequency.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Estimate the number of devices, interfaces per device, parameters per interface and polling frequency. Multiply the number of measurements by the bytes generated per measurement, including relevant protocol overhead, to estimate traffic per polling cycle. Divide by the interval to obtain an average rate.

第2块

Storage另算保存的值、metadata和保留时长，链路协议开销不一定全存入数据库；同步轮询还有峰值。

起句提示：Storage is a separate calculation.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Storage is a separate calculation. It depends on which values and metadata are kept, their size and the retention period. Network protocol overhead is not automatically stored with every value. Polling bursts may also create higher short-term load than the average suggests.

第3块

课件2–5%与超过10%复查是LAN经验值，不是通用标准；可减不必要参数、调整频率或分布采集。

起句提示：The lecture gives LAN planning guidelines of roughly 2–5% management load and suggests review when it exceeds 10%.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The lecture gives LAN planning guidelines of roughly 2–5% management load and suggests review when it exceeds 10%. These are course heuristics, not universal limits. Reduce unnecessary parameters or polling frequency, distribute collection and use suitable aggregation. Critical management information still needs sufficient resources and timely delivery.

不能丢的条件

2–5%、10%是课件经验参考；不是协议硬上限。

闭卷验收

T102 · LAN/WAN 管理监测点的放置 · 139词

题目：Where does the lecture suggest placing monitoring devices in LANs and WANs, and why?

解释课件LAN/WAN采集点建议。

先记回答顺序

  1. LAN先按每subnet一监测点的课件建议
  2. WAN在WAN–LAN接口增加适当监测
  3. 说明一个点可兼具多个观察角色
  4. 用定位delay/loss解释位置价值及实际条件

第1块

LAN可先以每subnet一个监测点估算，再计算设备、参数、频率带来的负载。

起句提示：For a LAN, the lecture suggests starting with one monitoring device per subnet.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

For a LAN, the lecture suggests starting with one monitoring device per subnet. Estimate device numbers, interfaces, measured parameters and polling frequency to check the resulting management traffic.

第2块

WAN还要看每个WAN–LAN接口；已有合适监测器可兼任，不必机械重复安装。

起句提示：For a WAN, it suggests monitoring at each WAN–LAN interface in addition to relevant local monitoring.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

For a WAN, it suggests monitoring at each WAN–LAN interface in addition to relevant local monitoring. If a monitor already sits on a LAN subnet at that interface, it may collect information for both roles. Separate physical monitors are not automatically required for every label.

第3块

本地与边界数据可比较，以定位问题并验证SLA；实际位置仍需按成本、负载和安全调整。

起句提示：This placement helps observe conditions at each location and verify service across the wider network.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

This placement helps observe conditions at each location and verify service across the wider network. For example, local measurements can be compared with WAN-boundary measurements to investigate where delay or loss occurs. These are starting guidelines rather than fixed universal rules. The final placement depends on traffic visibility, failure domains, security, cost and measurement needs. End-to-end service still requires suitable measurements across the complete relevant path.

不能丢的条件

per-subnet与per-interface是规划起点；别机械重复部署同一位置，也别把一条链路测量当全路径保证。

闭卷验收

T103 · Checks、数据与 MIB 选择 · 131词

题目：Explain checks and balances, management-data handling and MIB selection.

解释测量验证、存储和对象选择。

先记回答顺序

  1. checks/balances用独立或交叉测量验证数据
  2. data handling按本地、长期、归档和metadata规划
  3. MIB selection按任务、SLA和vendor支持选择
  4. 补OSS northbound要求及不盲采集

第1块

Checks and balances用复测或独立点比较管理数据，识别记录错、counter回卷、设备或厂商差异。

起句提示：Checks and balances validate management information by comparing measurements or using independent collection points.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Checks and balances validate management information by comparing measurements or using independent collection points. They help detect recording errors, counter rollover, stuck values, changed variables and differences between vendors.

第2块

短期本地数据有助排故，选取信息做长期归档并保留时间、单位和上下文。

起句提示：Management data needs a storage plan.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Management data needs a storage plan. Keep suitable information locally for event analysis and short-term trends, copy selected values for longer analysis and archive data when appropriate. Metadata should describe data types, timestamps and relationships so that later users understand what the values mean.

第3块

按任务选MIB：基本健康可用标准对象，特殊服务或厂商功能可能需额外对象；不为“能采到”就存一切。

起句提示：Choose MIBs according to the required tasks.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Choose MIBs according to the required tasks. Basic network health may need standard objects, while supported services, SLAs or vendor features may need additional objects. If management integrates with an operations support system, its northbound interface and data requirements must also be considered. Collecting everything without a purpose increases cost and can make useful information harder to find.

不能丢的条件

计数器回卷可能造成错误负数速率；不要直接当真实流量下降。

闭卷验收

先口头用中文解释，再手写或打出英文；对照上面的回答顺序，逐点检查。再做题库中的变式，确认能按新问法重组。

T104 · 管理与其他组件的关系 · 137词

题目：Explain the dependencies and external relationships of network management.

解释管理组件依赖及与其他组件互动。

先记回答顺序

  1. 先列管理依赖的路径、容量、存储与device support
  2. 再连addressing/routing、performance、security
  3. 写管理消耗资源与需要受控访问的双向关系
  4. 用恢复路径说明flow估计与优先级

第1块

Management需要可靠路径、容量、存储和设备支持，与OSS对接还需接口与流程。

起句提示：Management depends on reliable communication paths, sufficient capacity, storage and suitable device support.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Management depends on reliable communication paths, sufficient capacity, storage and suitable device support. Integration with an operations support system adds interface and information requirements. These are important dependencies within the management solution.

第2块

Addressing/routing决定管理可达；performance需要监测且也受管理流量影响；security控制管理员访问。

起句提示：Management also interacts with other architectures.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Management also interacts with other architectures. Addressing and routing determine device reachability and management domains. Performance management needs measurements, but collecting them consumes resources and can affect user traffic. Security policies may restrict access even though management requires controlled visibility and configuration access.

第3块

设计应保证关键管理流既可达又受保护、负载不过大；替代管理路径可帮助主网故障恢复。

起句提示：The design must support important management flows without creating unnecessary exposure or overhead.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The design must support important management flows without creating unnecessary exposure or overhead. For example, a separate management path can provide a fallback when the production path fails, but it must also be secured and maintained. Include management traffic in flow estimates and explain which information receives priority. A network is not fully manageable simply because its user traffic can be forwarded.

不能丢的条件

S3/Q03讲带内带外；本题重跨组件因果。

T105 · 设计过程的五类产物 · 151词

题目：What are expected outcomes of the design process? Describe them.

设计过程的预期产物是什么？描述它们。

先记回答顺序

  1. 先完整点出五类产物
  2. blueprints写物理建设安排
  3. component plan写功能机制
  4. selections写供应商设备服务选择
  5. traceability写依据链，metrics写怎样验收成功

第1块

Network blueprints写设备、连接、线缆、位置及相关物理安全，供实施人员建设。

起句提示：The design process should produce five main outputs.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The design process should produce five main outputs. Network blueprints describe the physical arrangement, including device locations, connections, cables and relevant physical security. They help people build the proposed network.

第2块

Component plan写某功能怎样由机制实现，以及内部和外部关系，如安全或管理。

起句提示：A component plan explains how a particular function or service will be implemented.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A component plan explains how a particular function or service will be implemented. It identifies mechanisms, internal relationships and interactions with other functions, for example security or management.

第3块

供应商、设备和服务选择记录具体选项，并与准则和架构相符。

起句提示：Vendor, equipment and service-provider selections record which suppliers, products and services have been chosen.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Vendor, equipment and service-provider selections record which suppliers, products and services have been chosen. These choices should meet the design criteria and fit the architecture.

第4块

Traceability把选择连接需求和架构决策，让审核者知道为什么这样选。

起句提示：Traceability links design decisions to requirements and architectural decisions.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Traceability links design decisions to requirements and architectural decisions. It helps reviewers understand why a choice was made and whether it has a clear basis.

第5块

Design metrics写成功如何测量，如容量、时延、可用性；五类合起来比购物清单完整。

起句提示：Finally, design metrics define how success will be measured.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Finally, design metrics define how success will be measured. Measures may include capacity, delay and availability, as well as relevant user or device requirements. Together, these outputs support implementation, review and later evaluation; a device shopping list alone is not a complete design.

不能丢的条件

只写拓扑图；漏traceability或metrics；把architectural design的outcomes当作这五项的同名清单。

闭卷验收

T106 · 供应商、设备和服务评价 · 136词

题目：Describe a defensible process for evaluating vendors, equipment and service providers.

说明客观、可追溯的评价过程。

先记回答顺序

  1. 先由需求与架构确定评价目标、标准和规则
  2. 收集可比证据并固定评分方法与权重
  3. 独立比较成本、技术、性能、风险和扩展
  4. 必备要求先检查，保留选择理由与未达标处理

第1块

先从需求和reference architecture设目标、准则及比较规则，再收集候选产品、供应商和服务的证据。

起句提示：Begin with the requirements and reference architecture.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Begin with the requirements and reference architecture. Define the evaluation goals, criteria and rules before comparing candidates. Gather and organize documentation, discuss relevant questions and collect evidence about each option.

第2块

使用同一评分尺度和按优先级确定权重，可比较成本、技术、性能、风险和扩展性；记录信息来源。

起句提示：Develop a consistent rating method.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Develop a consistent rating method. Criteria may include cost, technology, performance, risk and scalability. Weights should reflect agreed priorities, and scores should be supported by comparable evidence. Ratings and ranking should be made independently of the vendors being assessed.

第3块

首选方案还须通过硬性条件并兼顾支持、长期费用和运维，最低购买价不必然最合适。

起句提示：The preferred option must meet essential requirements and provide a suitable overall balance.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The preferred option must meet essential requirements and provide a suitable overall balance. Purchase price alone is not enough: support, reputation, backup resources, operating costs and compatibility also matter. Keep the process transparent and document why candidates are accepted or rejected. If the evidence shows that no candidate meets a primary requirement, review the options or requirements rather than hiding that failure inside a favourable average score.

不能丢的条件

加权分最高不能抵消不满足必备条件。

闭卷验收

T107 · 五项加权评分与必备条件 · 54词

题目：Use weights 20%, 30%, 30%, 10% and 10% for cost, technology, performance, risk and scalability. Higher scores are better on a 1–5 scale. A scores 5,3,4,2,4 and B scores 3,4,5,4,3. Rank them, then reconsider if B fails a mandatory security condition.

使用课件p.18权重、自拟候选分数，计算并检查必备条件。

先记回答顺序

  1. 先明确所有原分越高越好
  2. 按各项weight×rating逐项求和
  3. 检查权重和为1并比较A/B总分
  4. 再检查mandatory security，失败者排除或整改重评

结果与条件

每项评分×权重后相加，权重须合适且条件相同。先检查必须条件；若候选不满足核心要求，较高总分也不应直接获选。

起句提示：A scores 3.70 and B scores 4.00 on the stated weighted scale, so B ranks higher by 0.30.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A scores 3.70 and B scores 4.00 on the stated weighted scale, so B ranks higher by 0.30. However, a weighted score cannot compensate for failure to meet a mandatory security condition. B must be excluded or corrected and reassessed. A is selectable only if it also passes all essential conditions.

步骤必须会独立重做

所有列都先转为“越高越好”：cost高分表示成本表现更好，risk高分表示风险更可接受，不是花钱或风险越大越好。 | 标准 | 权重 | A原分 | A加权 | B原分 | B加权 | |---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:| |Cost|0.20|5|1.00|3|0.60| |Technology|0.30|3|0.90|4|1.20| |Performance|0.30|4|1.20|5|1.50| |Risk|0.10|2|0.20|4|0.40| |Scalability|0.10|4|0.40|3|0.30| |Total|1.00|—|3.70|—|4.00| S=Σwᵢrᵢ；w是权重，r是同尺度评分。B领先0.30。但若B不满足强制安全条件，应判不合格/要求整改后重新评价，不能只靠总分选B。A也需通过所有必备条件才能最终选定。

不能丢的条件

权重不能随候选人临时改变；原始价格/延迟等不同单位不能未经规则直接相加。

T108 · 原课件九项评价表与候选排序 · 118词

题目：Recalculate the weighted totals of the five candidates in Week11 PDF p.20. Explain the x/y cells and the rank deltas on p.21.

完整复算原图九项表，不把相对权重强当百分数。

先记回答顺序

  1. 从原表逐列抄相对权重和原始评分
  2. 明确x/y表示原分/加权分而非除法
  3. 求各候选加权合计，必要时除权重和5归一化
  4. 按高分排序
  5. 区分与前一名差及与第一名累计差

结果与条件

逐项评分乘权重求和，再按原表比较候选；核对权重、算术、评分方向与约束。总分只是有记录的取舍依据，不能掩盖硬性不合格项。

起句提示：The weighted totals are 17.6, 21.6, 12.7, 10.9 and 12.2 for candidates 1–5.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The weighted totals are 17.6, 21.6, 12.7, 10.9 and 12.2 for candidates 1–5. The ranking is 2, 1, 3, 5, 4. Each x/y cell shows the original score x and its weighted contribution y; the slash is a separator, not division. In ranked order, signed differences from the preceding candidate are 0, −4.0, −4.9, −0.5 and −1.3, with 0 used for the first row. Differences from the top candidate are 0, −4.0, −8.9, −9.4 and −10.7. The relative weights total 5.0; dividing every total by 5 normalizes the scale without changing the ranking. These gaps do not by themselves define an elimination threshold.

步骤必须会独立重做

原表每格x/y＝原始评分x / 加权得分y，不是除法。相对权重[0.8,1.0,0.5,0.2,0.9,0.8,0.2,0.5,0.1]之和为5.0，不是1。标准依次为initial cost、recurring cost、technology、standards compliance、risk、performance、available services、operations、scalability。 |候选|九项原始评分|原分合计|加权合计|归一化加权值（÷5）| |---|---|---:|---:|---:| |1|3,4,3,4,3,4,5,3,5|34|17.6|3.52| |2|5,5,4,1,4,5,1,5,1|31|21.6|4.32| |3|4,3,1,3,1,2,3,4,3|24|12.7|2.54| |4|2,1,2,2,5,1,2,2,2|19|10.9|2.18| |5|1,2,5,5,2,3,4,1,4|27|12.2|2.44| 以候选1为例：3×.8+4×1+3×.5+4×.2+3×.9+4×.8+5×.2+3×.5+5×.1=17.6。 按加权值排名2→1→3→5→4。与前一名的差为0、−4.0、−4.9、−0.5、−1.3；相对第一名的累计差为0、−4.0、−8.9、−9.4、−10.7。 是否淘汰某候选还需预先约定规则，图中差值不自动定义“淘汰阈值”。

不能丢的条件

不能按原分合计把候选1排第一；图中17.6不是17.6%。

T109 · Primary/secondary criteria 与约束 · 133词

题目：Distinguish primary and secondary design criteria and explain technology selection.

区分主次标准并说明技术选择。

先记回答顺序

  1. primary说明成功必需，secondary说明可让步
  2. 把目标转成明确可验证标准
  3. 按flowspec、预算、现网、设施和支持技能选技术
  4. 分区评价接口，并说明规划指导值有条件

第1块

Primary criteria是项目成功必须满足的条件；secondary是可权衡的加分项，冲突时可能让步。

起句提示：Primary design criteria are conditions required for project success.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Primary design criteria are conditions required for project success. Secondary criteria are desirable features that may be sacrificed when they conflict with essential goals. This distinction gives the design a clear priority structure.

第2块

把目标写成短而具体的容量、时延、RMA或可维护准则，便于评价。

起句提示：Translate goals into short and specific criteria.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Translate goals into short and specific criteria. A performance goal may require particular capacity, delay or RMA levels. A supportability goal may favour technologies the staff can operate. Compare alternatives in a consistent table and explain the trade-offs.

第3块

选技术还受预算、现网与设施限制；flowspec给服务要求，大网络可分区域评价，不能只按一项速度排名。

起句提示：Technology selection is constrained by cost, existing networks and facilities.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Technology selection is constrained by cost, existing networks and facilities. Flow specifications provide service and capacity requirements. A large network can be divided into manageable sections, with each section evaluated against its own needs and interfaces. The course’s utilization guidelines must be adjusted for burstiness and local conditions. Choosing the cheapest option is not successful if it fails a primary service requirement.

不能丢的条件

60%/80%课件规则是指导值，不是所有链路的普遍硬阈值。

闭卷验收

T110 · Architectural design 的要素 · 129词

题目：What concept and technology elements should an architectural design cover?

架构设计应包含哪些概念及技术要素？

先记回答顺序

  1. 先说明architectural design的高层作用
  2. 列concept elements并按目标和约束组织
  3. 列technology elements
  4. 用多校园例子区分架构指导与最终物料细节

第1块

Architectural design给高层技术、协议、通信能力及网络分段关系，把组织方向连到后续详细设计。

起句提示：Architectural design sets high-level technologies, protocols, communication capabilities and segment relationships.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Architectural design sets high-level technologies, protocols, communication capabilities and segment relationships. It connects organizational direction with the detailed design that follows.

第2块

概念方面考虑业务、扩展、可靠、安全、管理、旧系统兼容、地理、资金、风险、性能与简单性。

起句提示：Concept elements include business environment, technology choices, scalability, dependability, security, management and maintainability.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Concept elements include business environment, technology choices, scalability, dependability, security, management and maintainability. Compatibility with legacy systems, limits, flexibility, geography, finances, risk, performance and simplicity also matter. These define the conditions the solution must satisfy.

第3块

技术方面写node、link、topology、interface、service、protocol和traffic map；需具体到能指导选择，但不等于最终型号/线缆长度清单。

起句提示：Technology elements describe nodes, links, topology, interfaces, services, protocols and traffic mapping.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Technology elements describe nodes, links, topology, interfaces, services, protocols and traffic mapping. They explain what kinds of components connect, how services overlay the network and where traffic requirements apply. For example, a multi-campus design needs geographical reach, growth and availability goals as well as suitable link types and protocols. Architectural design should be specific enough to guide choices without being confused with a final list of device models, cable lengths and installation details.

不能丢的条件

conceptual/architectural设计与最终detailed design不是同一粒度。

T111 · 60%与80%容量规划指导值 · 53词

题目：Using the lecture guidelines, estimate candidate capacity for (a) 60 Mb/s of only best-effort traffic and (b) 80 Mb/s of only predictable traffic. For a separate mixed case, assume the specification explicitly requires at most 80% utilization for its predictable component and 60% for the total aggregate: predictable 40 Mb/s and best effort 20 Mb/s.

分别计算纯BE、纯predictable；混合题明确给出口径后再比较。

先记回答顺序

  1. 写capacity≥load/utilization limit
  2. 分别计算纯BE与纯predictable
  3. 混合例按题目明确的两条约束各求下界
  4. 取更严格下界并检验候选
  5. 说明burst、overhead与端系统仍需验证

结果与条件

按题给60%或80%指导阈值，把所需业务负载除以允许占用率求最低标称容量；阈值是规划指导，不是物理定律或所有应用的统一保证。

起句提示：The pure best-effort and pure predictable examples each suggest 100 Mb/s.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The pure best-effort and pure predictable examples each suggest 100 Mb/s. In the explicitly defined mixed case, the two bounds are 50 Mb/s and 100 Mb/s, so the stricter bound is 100 Mb/s. The result is a planning estimate under the stated assumptions, not proof of an end-to-end guarantee.

步骤必须会独立重做

(a)60/0.60=100Mb/s；(b)80/0.80=100Mb/s。 混合例是AI自拟，并明确total aggregate=40+20=60Mb/s，避免对课件“combined”文字作未说明的推断。 条件1：L≥40/.8=50Mb/s；条件2：L≥60/.6=100Mb/s；取较严格者L≥100Mb/s。 若候选只有50和100Mb/s，则50不满足条件2，100在这些假设下满足。若端口、处理、burst、协议overhead等限制未计入，还需继续验证。 这些是Week11规划guidelines，p.68明确需结合经验和burstiness，不是任何协议或任何应用强制通用的利用率界限。

不能丢的条件

纯流与混合口径不能悄悄互换；总容量够不表示时延或availability达标。

闭卷验收

T112 · 布局、traceability 与成功指标 · 131词

题目：Explain network layout, design traceability and success metrics.

解释布局、决策追溯和成功验证。

先记回答顺序

  1. layout区分logical关系与physical blueprints
  2. traceability把问题/需求接到架构和详细决定
  3. metrics说明怎样测量成功
  4. 用一个服务写需求→决定→测试完整链

第1块

Layout结合拓扑、技术、设备、关系和关键位置；逻辑图强调通信与服务关系，物理图强调实际摆放和连接。

起句提示：Network layout combines topology, technology, equipment, relationships and strategic locations.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Network layout combines topology, technology, equipment, relationships and strategic locations. Logical diagrams emphasize connectivity and service relationships. Physical blueprints describe actual devices, cables, rooms and security locations. Component plans isolate a function, such as security or network management, for clearer explanation.

第2块

Traceability把问题与需求连到架构和详细选择，留下客观可辩护的证据链。

起句提示：Design traceability links problem statements and requirements to architecture and detailed design decisions.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Design traceability links problem statements and requirements to architecture and detailed design decisions. This creates evidence that choices are objective and defensible. It also helps assess the effect of a changed requirement.

第3块

Success metrics用容量、时延、RMA以及用户/设备要求检查是否达成目标；没有测量就不能宣称设计成功。

起句提示：Success metrics check whether the design achieves its goals.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Success metrics check whether the design achieves its goals. Capacity, delay and RMA are important, but user and device requirements may add other measures. For example, a teaching-service requirement should connect to a traffic policy, suitable devices and a defined performance test. A diagram or equipment list alone does not prove that the final service meets the requirement.

不能丢的条件

可追溯性不是“保留采购收据”；metric应能检验目标。

T113 · SDN的优点 · 155词

题目：What are advantages of SDN?

SDN有哪些优点？

先记回答顺序

  1. 先点出自动化、政策一致、协调与灵活性收益
  2. 用control/data separation解释收益来源
  3. 用programmability和较完整视图解释怎样实现
  4. 补controller可用性、安全、扩展与兼容条件

第1块

SDN把控制决策与逐包转发分开，软件控制规则，设备按规则执行，减轻逐台手工改配置。

起句提示：SDN separates the control plane from the data plane.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

SDN separates the control plane from the data plane. Forwarding devices handle traffic according to rules, while software-based control decides how those rules are managed. This makes it easier to change network behaviour without configuring every device separately.

第2块

可编程接口让应用提出网络需要，支持快速变更、自动化与较一致政策；控制器较广的视图有利于协同资源。

起句提示：Programmability allows applications to request network functions through interfaces.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Programmability allows applications to request network functions through interfaces. It can support faster configuration changes, automation and more consistent policies across many devices. A controller's broader network view can also help coordinate traffic handling and resource use instead of relying only on isolated device decisions.

第3块

控制软件和转发设备可较独立演进，便于引入服务和适应变化。

起句提示：The separation allows control software and forwarding devices to evolve more independently.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The separation allows control software and forwarding devices to evolve more independently. It can make introducing new services and adapting to changing requirements more flexible.

第4块

收益取决于控制器可用、安全、规模和接口兼容；逻辑集中不等于只有一台物理控制器，SDN也不自动更快更安全。

起句提示：These benefits depend on suitable design and implementation.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

These benefits depend on suitable design and implementation. Controller availability, security, scalability and interface compatibility still matter. Logically centralized control does not require a single physical controller, and SDN does not automatically guarantee higher speed or complete security. Its value should be judged against the network's requirements.

不能丢的条件

只列“快、便宜、安全”；把controller当作所有数据包必经转发设备；把逻辑集中当物理单点。

闭卷验收

先口头用中文解释，再手写或打出英文；对照上面的回答顺序，逐点检查。再做题库中的变式，确认能按新问法重组。

T114 · SDN 三平面 · 132词

题目：Describe the three planes of SDN and how they interact.

描述SDN三个平面的分工。

先记回答顺序

  1. 列application、control、data三平面
  2. 分别说明意图、控制决定、转发执行
  3. 用应用请求→controller规则→switch动作串联
  4. 补逻辑集中与控制器保护条件

第1块

Application plane放网络应用与服务意图，control plane负责政策和规则，data plane执行转发。

起句提示：SDN separates the application, control and data planes.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

SDN separates the application, control and data planes. The application plane contains network services and applications that express requirements and use a view of the network.

第2块

一个或多个controller维护基础设施视图、作决策并给转发设备编程，data plane依据规则匹配packet。

起句提示：The control plane is managed by one or more controllers.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The control plane is managed by one or more controllers. It maintains information about the infrastructure, makes or implements policy decisions and programs forwarding behaviour. The data plane contains physical or virtual forwarding devices. They process traffic according to rules and report relevant network state.

第3块

例如应用提出教学流优先请求，控制器转为规则，交换机逐包执行；分层使职责清楚，但仍须验证通信与控制可靠性。

起句提示：The separation allows control logic and forwarding to evolve more independently.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The separation allows control logic and forwarding to evolve more independently. For example, an application can request a traffic policy, the controller translates it into device rules, and switches apply those rules to packets. This supports programmability and coordinated management. A logically centralized view does not require one unprotected physical controller. Controller architecture, communication and failure handling remain important design decisions.

不能丢的条件

application plane不是交换机端口；controller不等于逐个代替交换机传所有数据。

闭卷验收

T115 · SDN interfaces 与 OpenFlow · 127词

题目：Explain northbound, southbound and east–west interfaces in SDN.

解释三类接口及OpenFlow的角色。

先记回答顺序

  1. 按连接对象定义northbound与southbound
  2. 说明east–west连接controllers
  3. 把OpenFlow放在southbound例子的位置
  4. 用flow table匹配动作解释实施并补兼容/扩展条件

第1块

Northbound连接应用与controller，使应用表达需求和读取网络信息；southbound连接controller与转发设备落实决策。

起句提示：A northbound interface connects the SDN control plane with applications or higher-level services.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A northbound interface connects the SDN control plane with applications or higher-level services. It lets applications obtain network information and express requirements. A southbound interface connects controllers with forwarding infrastructure so that control decisions can be applied to devices.

第2块

East–west连接分布式controller之间；OpenFlow只是southbound协议之一，不等于SDN所有接口。

起句提示：An east–west interface supports communication between controllers in a distributed control architecture.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

An east–west interface supports communication between controllers in a distributed control architecture. OpenFlow is one example of a southbound protocol; it is not the only possible SDN interface or the complete definition of SDN.

第3块

交换机按flow table匹配并执行转发或丢弃；是否联系controller取决于已有规则与配置，接口还要考虑安全、规模与兼容。

起句提示：In an OpenFlow-style system, a switch matches packets against flow-table entries and performs the associated actions, such as forwarding or dropping.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

In an OpenFlow-style system, a switch matches packets against flow-table entries and performs the associated actions, such as forwarding or dropping. Controller involvement depends on installed rules and configuration. The interfaces must support the needed functions, security and scale. Different northbound APIs can also create portability and integration challenges between applications and control platforms.

不能丢的条件

northbound在不同管理架构语境有不同上层对象；先说明这里是SDN。

T144 · OpenFlow flow table 与逐包操作 · 156词

题目：Explain the header fields, counters and actions in the lecture’s OpenFlow switch diagram. What may happen when a packet arrives?

按图解释匹配、计数与动作；说明控制器不是每包必经路径。

先记回答顺序

  1. 解释header fields匹配、counters统计、actions执行
  2. 沿入包→匹配→本地动作解释正常转发
  3. 说明controller通过southbound编程和获取状态
  4. 未命中依table-miss与版本处理，保留上报/丢弃条件

第1块

OpenFlow表条目有header fields作匹配条件、counters记流量统计、actions规定匹配后怎样处理。

起句提示：The lecture’s OpenFlow switch diagram shows flow entries with header fields, counters and actions.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The lecture’s OpenFlow switch diagram shows flow entries with header fields, counters and actions. Header fields define which packets match an entry. Counters record relevant traffic statistics. Actions specify how matching traffic should be handled, such as sending it to an output port, involving the controller or dropping it.

第2块

Packet到达时查已装规则；匹配的转发规则可在设备本地执行，非每包都送controller。

起句提示：An arriving packet is checked against the installed rules.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

An arriving packet is checked against the installed rules. A matching forwarding rule can be applied locally, so every packet does not need to travel through the controller. The controller programs forwarding behaviour through a southbound interface and can obtain state information.

第3块

无匹配时按table-miss及版本配置处理，可送controller也可丢弃；还要考虑表容量、控制器负载、安全与故障。

起句提示：If no ordinary entry matches, handling depends on the configured table-miss behaviour and protocol version.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

If no ordinary entry matches, handling depends on the configured table-miss behaviour and protocol version. Traffic may be sent to the controller or dropped; neither outcome should be assumed universally. For example, an installed rule can forward a known teaching flow while a different case requires controller attention. This division supports programmability, but flow-table capacity, controller load, secure communication and failure handling remain design concerns.

不能丢的条件

controller通过southbound编程并收状态；未命中处理由table-miss设置和版本决定，可能上报也可能丢弃，不能说每包或每次miss必定上controller。

闭卷验收

T116 · SDN 的限制与部署条件 · 130词

题目：Discuss the challenges and trade-offs of deploying SDN.

讨论SDN挑战及收益条件。

先记回答顺序

  1. 先承认可编程收益并指出controller/接口的重要性
  2. 按failure/security、scale、portability、QoS列挑战
  3. 说明distributed controllers的收益与协调成本
  4. 用测试与需求比较判断是否适合部署

第1块

SDN能统一控制与编程，但controller及接口成为关键部分；故障或通信中断会影响新规则和观察。

起句提示：SDN can simplify coordinated control and make networks programmable, but the controller and its interfaces become important parts of the system.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

SDN can simplify coordinated control and make networks programmable, but the controller and its interfaces become important parts of the system. Controller failure or compromise can affect many devices, so availability and security need explicit design.

第2块

规模取决于控制负载、状态采集、flow table和分布控制器协调；多控制器增加韧性也增加一致性工作。

起句提示：Scalability depends on control workload, platform architecture, state collection and flow-rule management.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Scalability depends on control workload, platform architecture, state collection and flow-rule management. Distributed controllers can improve resilience and capacity, but they require coordination. Different northbound interfaces can make applications harder to move between platforms. Supporting QoS and diverse environments, including IoT, adds further requirements.

第3块

部署应测试设备支持、政策、管理和故障恢复，比较收益与成本；“可编程”不等于自动可靠。

起句提示：A practical deployment should compare these costs with the expected benefits.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A practical deployment should compare these costs with the expected benefits. Test device support, policy behaviour, management access and recovery under failures. SDN does not automatically make a network secure, reliable or fast. Those outcomes depend on how the architecture is designed, operated and measured against the organization’s actual requirements.

不能丢的条件

不能声称SDN自动消除全部故障或兼容性问题。

闭卷验收

T117 · SD-WAN · 135词

题目：What is SD-WAN, and what benefits and conditions should be considered?

解释SD-WAN及其收益条件。

先记回答顺序

  1. 定义SD-WAN面向广域网的软件管理
  2. 列应用体验、安全管理、云连接和简化收益
  3. 说明底层capacity/delay/loss/availability仍约束
  4. 用教学政策例子连接需求与成本

第1块

SD-WAN用软件方法管理相隔较远的WAN连接，如多校区或办公室之间。

起句提示：SD-WAN applies a software-based management approach to a wide area network.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

SD-WAN applies a software-based management approach to a wide area network. A WAN connects geographically separated networks, such as the LANs of different campuses or offices.

第2块

它可改善政策一致性、应用对现有路径的使用、云连接与管理效率，也可结合安全功能。

SD-WAN can make policy management more consistent and improve how applications use available WAN connectivity. These are potential benefits rather than outcomes guaranteed by the name alone.

第3块

起句提示：The design must still consider the capacity, delay, loss and availability of the underlying links.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The design must still consider the capacity, delay, loss and availability of the underlying links. It also needs suitable security policies, monitoring and controller resilience. For example, centrally applying a policy for teaching traffic can simplify operation across campuses, but it cannot remove a physical outage unless an alternative path exists. Select SD-WAN when its capabilities address the organization’s flows and management needs at an acceptable cost.

不能丢的条件

SD-WAN不创造链路容量，也不意味着可以忽略underlay故障。

T118 · SDN 与 Big Data 的双向关系 · 129词

题目：Explain how SDN supports big data and how big data can improve SDN.

解释双向促进及五个V。

先记回答顺序

  1. 先简述Big Data的5V
  2. 写SDN通过全局视图与可编程支持数据业务
  3. 反向写数据分析怎样帮助SDN决策
  4. 补data quality、control overhead、scale和及时性

第1块

Big Data的五V是量、种类、速度、价值和可信程度，帮助说明数据特点。

起句提示：Big data is commonly described by volume, variety, velocity, value and veracity.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Big data is commonly described by volume, variety, velocity, value and veracity. These concern how much data exists, its forms and speed, the value obtained and its trustworthiness.

第2块

SDN的广域视图、可编程控制和动态重配可帮助大量数据的运输与资源协调。

起句提示：SDN can support big-data applications through a global network view, programmable control and dynamic reconfiguration.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

SDN can support big-data applications through a global network view, programmable control and dynamic reconfiguration. These help coordinate data delivery, resource use and processing across data centres. Network decisions can be adjusted as application demand changes.

第3块

反过来，分析网络数据可帮助流量工程、跨层决策、安全检测与数据中心运行；但数据质量、控制器规模和规则更新仍是限制。

起句提示：The relationship also works in the other direction.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The relationship also works in the other direction. Analysis of network data can help SDN with traffic engineering, cross-layer decisions, security detection and data-centre operation. However, useful data does not automatically produce good decisions. The course identifies controller scalability, flow-table management and flexible programming abstractions as open issues. The designer must consider data quality, control overhead and the practical ability to apply decisions in time.

不能丢的条件

veracity=可信度/真实性；不是velocity。

T119 · Network automation · 134词

题目：Define network automation and discuss its benefits and risks.

定义网络自动化并讨论收益及控制。

先记回答顺序

  1. 定义automation覆盖的网络生命周期任务
  2. 说明减少重复、错误和不一致怎样产生收益
  3. 解释错误自动传播的风险
  4. 补访问控制、验证、分批、回退并区分automation与SDN

第1块

Network automation用软件处理配置、管理、测试、部署与运行等重复任务，可结合SDN或其他工具。

起句提示：Network automation uses software to perform tasks in the network life cycle, including configuration, management, testing, deployment and operation.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Network automation uses software to perform tasks in the network life cycle, including configuration, management, testing, deployment and operation. The course gives SDN, Ansible, Puppet and Python scripts as examples of approaches or tools.

第2块

自动化能减少手敲错误和不一致，让变化更快、规模更大，但也需要可靠输入和统一政策。

起句提示：Automation can reduce repetitive manual work, typing errors and inconsistent configuration.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Automation can reduce repetitive manual work, typing errors and inconsistent configuration. It can improve speed, scalability and network-wide policy consistency. It also frees engineers to focus on tasks that need judgement.

第3块

错误脚本也会把同一错误推广到很多设备；应控制权限、先测试、分批实施、监测并能回退。

起句提示：However, an incorrect automated instruction can repeat the same mistake across many devices.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

However, an incorrect automated instruction can repeat the same mistake across many devices. Access control, accurate input and verification are still needed. As a practical extension, testing changes, deploying them in stages and preparing rollback help manage this risk. Automation is broader than SDN: it can be used on traditional devices as well. Its success should be measured by reliable outcomes, not only by how quickly commands are sent.

不能丢的条件

自动化不等于AI；最后的测试/分批/回退是AI补充实施建议。

T120 · NFV 与三要素 · 126词

题目：Define NFV and describe NFVI, VNFs and MANO.

定义NFV及三项架构元素。

先记回答顺序

  1. 定义NFV把网络功能软件化并脱离专用设备
  2. NFVI写资源与虚拟化基础
  3. VNF写功能，MANO写管理编排
  4. 补共享资源、隔离、性能和故障条件

第1块

NFV把防火墙等网络功能作为软件运行在共享通用基础设施，而非每处都要一台专用盒子。

起句提示：Network Function Virtualization, or NFV, implements network functions as software on shared, general-purpose infrastructure.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Network Function Virtualization, or NFV, implements network functions as software on shared, general-purpose infrastructure. For example, a firewall or load-balancing function can run as a software instance instead of requiring a separate dedicated appliance for every deployment.

第2块

NFVI提供计算、存储、网络和虚拟化资源；VNF实现具体功能；MANO负责部署、生命周期与资源编排。

起句提示：The course identifies three main elements.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The course identifies three main elements. NFV infrastructure, or NFVI, provides the computing, storage, networking and virtualization resources. Virtual network functions, or VNFs, provide the actual network functions. Management and orchestration, or MANO, manages their deployment and life cycle and coordinates resources.

第3块

软件化增加放置与扩缩灵活性，但共享竞争、性能、隔离和故障仍须测试。

起句提示：Separating functions from dedicated equipment can improve flexibility and allow services to be placed or scaled when needed.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Separating functions from dedicated equipment can improve flexibility and allow services to be placed or scaled when needed. However, performance, isolation, shared-resource contention and infrastructure failure still matter. Virtualization changes how functions are delivered; it does not remove the requirement to test service capacity, availability and security.

不能丢的条件

VNF是功能软件，NFVI是承载资源，MANO不是转发业务本身。

闭卷验收

T121 · SDN 与 NFV 的区别和组合 · 138词

题目：Compare SDN and NFV and explain how they can work together.

比较SDN/NFV并说明组合方式。

先记回答顺序

  1. 按分离对象比较SDN和NFV
  2. 说明SDN控制连通/转发，NFV部署服务功能
  3. 用SDN引流经过VNF举例
  4. 明确MANO与controller分工及组合条件

第1块

SDN分开控制决策与数据转发；NFV把功能从专用实体设备移到软件与通用资源。

起句提示：SDN separates network control from data forwarding and makes forwarding behaviour programmable.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

SDN separates network control from data forwarding and makes forwarding behaviour programmable. NFV separates network functions from dedicated physical appliances and runs those functions as software on shared infrastructure.

第2块

前者更关注流量如何受控和连接，后者更关注防火墙等功能如何实现与放置；二者不同。

起句提示：They address different forms of flexibility.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

They address different forms of flexibility. SDN concerns how traffic is controlled and connected. NFV concerns how services such as firewalls or load balancers are implemented and deployed. One does not automatically require the other.

第3块

可用NFV部署虚拟防火墙、SDN按政策引导流量经过它；MANO管功能生命周期，组合仍需验证容量与安全。

起句提示：They can work together.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

They can work together. NFV can deploy a virtual firewall and other functions, while SDN can steer relevant traffic through them according to policy. MANO manages function life cycles and resources, while controllers manage appropriate connectivity and forwarding. The combined design must still consider capacity, availability, security and coordination. A software function is not automatically fast or reliable enough merely because it is virtual, and programmable connectivity does not itself provide the function’s protection.

不能丢的条件

不要把NFV简单定义成SDN另一个名字。

闭卷验收

T122 · AI、ML、DL 与学习类型 · 137词

题目：Explain AI, ML and DL and compare supervised, unsupervised and reinforcement learning.

解释包含关系及三类学习。

先记回答顺序

  1. AI包含ML，ML包含DL
  2. 按训练信息区分supervised、unsupervised、reinforcement
  3. supervised再区分classification与regression
  4. 用网络任务解释匹配并补数据和运维条件

第1块

AI是更大的智能任务领域，ML从数据/经验学模式，DL是用多层神经网络的ML一类。

起句提示：Artificial intelligence is the broader field of systems performing tasks associated with intelligent behaviour.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Artificial intelligence is the broader field of systems performing tasks associated with intelligent behaviour. Machine learning is a part of AI that learns patterns from data or experience. Deep learning is a part of ML that uses multi-layer neural-network approaches.

第2块

监督学习有标签，可做分类或数值回归；无监督从无目标标签数据找结构；强化学习在动作与奖励反馈中学习。

起句提示：Supervised learning uses examples with target labels or values.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Supervised learning uses examples with target labels or values. Classification predicts a category, while regression predicts a numerical value. Unsupervised learning looks for structure in data without supplied target labels, such as groups of similar traffic. Reinforcement learning learns actions through interaction and reward feedback.

第3块

攻击标签可用于分类，流量数据可用于预测，网络环境反馈可用于资源控制；方法必须匹配任务、数据和限制，名称叫AI不保证效果。

起句提示：These approaches suit different network tasks.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

These approaches suit different network tasks. A labelled attack dataset may support classification, traffic measurements may support rate prediction, and interaction with a network environment may support resource-control decisions. The learning method must match the task, available data and operational limits. No method guarantees useful predictions simply because it is called AI.

不能丢的条件

ML不全是监督学习；DL不等于整个AI。

闭卷验收

T123 · ML workflow、应用与挑战 · 135词

题目：Describe an ML workflow for networking, its applications and limitations.

说明ML流程、网络用途和局限。

先记回答顺序

  1. 从历史数据到建模、评价、优化再预测
  2. 列网络应用并说明支持的决策
  3. 解释算力/准确性、数据可得性、隐私、不平衡与真实性挑战
  4. 用稀有攻击例子说明验证与错误后果

第1块

ML先准备合适历史数据，建模、评价和优化要反复进行，之后才在新数据上预测并继续监测。

起句提示：An ML workflow starts with suitable historical data.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

An ML workflow starts with suitable historical data. Model building, evaluation and optimization form an iterative process. After evaluation, the model can make predictions or support decisions on new data. As a practical extension, new conditions should be monitored because old patterns may no longer represent the network.

第2块

可用于流量/链路预测、入侵检测、路由辅助、QoS和资源分配，任务与数据须匹配。

起句提示：Networking applications include predicting traffic volume or link quality, intrusion detection, routing support, QoS improvement and resource allocation.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Networking applications include predicting traffic volume or link quality, intrusion detection, routing support, QoS improvement and resource allocation. These applications can assist engineers but still require suitable operational controls.

第3块

算力、数据缺乏与隐私、类别不平衡及真实场景测试都会限制结果；旧数据高分不等于新网络可用。

起句提示：The course highlights computational load and the trade-off with accuracy, data availability and privacy, imbalanced datasets and the need for realistic testing.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The course highlights computational load and the trade-off with accuracy, data availability and privacy, imbalanced datasets and the need for realistic testing. A model trained mostly on normal traffic may perform poorly on rare attacks. Evaluate results against the actual task and consider the consequences of wrong predictions. More complex models are not automatically better for every network.

不能丢的条件

训练集上高准确率不能直接证明真实环境可用；罕见攻击可能被掩盖。

T124 · 安全、隐私与 Threat analysis · 974词

题目：Discuss the importance of security and privacy in strategic network design. Why is threat analysis important in developing a security and privacy plan? What should threat analysis consider? Give three examples of threats.

讨论重要性、威胁分析的作用、考虑因素，并给三个威胁例子。

先记回答顺序

  1. 拆出重要性、why threat analysis、考虑因素、三个威胁例子
  2. 用CIA/privacy说明组织后果
  3. 从asset→threat→likelihood/impact推到控制与优先级
  4. 三个案例各写资产、威胁、后果、回应和限制
  5. 结尾说明取舍与复查

Introduction

战略网络设计应长期支持组织目标。安全与隐私很重要，因为用户依赖网络获取信息、通信和提供服务。网络即使容量足够，如果信息被盗、被修改或不可用，也可能无法实现目标。因此应在需求分析与架构阶段考虑安全，而不是等选完设备再补加。有效计划连接业务需要、重要资产、可能威胁、适当控制和清楚责任。威胁分析为这些选择提供依据，并帮助解释为什么网络特定部分需要保护。

起句提示：Strategic network design should support an organization's goals over time.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Strategic network design should support an organization's goals over time. Security and privacy are important because users depend on the network to access information, communicate and provide services. A network may have enough capacity but still fail its purpose if information is stolen, changed or unavailable. Security should therefore be considered during requirements analysis and architecture development, rather than added only after equipment has been selected. A useful plan connects business needs, important assets, possible threats, suitable controls and clear responsibilities. Threat analysis provides evidence for these choices and helps explain why protection is needed in particular parts of the network.

Importance of security and privacy

CIA目标是一个有用起点。保密性防止未授权访问和披露；完整性防止未授权修改；可用性表示需要时能访问资源和服务。本课程在安全架构中讨论隐私，尤其强调未授权访问与披露；更完整的隐私还关注个人信息是否被适当收集、使用和披露。大学的学生记录需要保密性和完整性，在线教学还需要可用性。失效可能中断教学、损害信任并带来恢复费用。用户、应用和设备可能有不同需要，因此保护应依据需求与风险，而非在所有地方采用完全相同安排。

起句提示：The CIA goals provide a useful starting point.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The CIA goals provide a useful starting point. Confidentiality protects information from unauthorized access and disclosure. Integrity protects information from unauthorized changes. Availability means that resources and services are accessible when required. The course discusses privacy within security architecture, especially unauthorized access and disclosure. More broadly, privacy also concerns the appropriate collection, use and disclosure of personal information. For a university, student records need confidentiality and integrity, while online teaching also needs availability. Failure can interrupt teaching, damage trust and create recovery costs. Different users, applications and devices may have different needs, so protection should be based on requirements and risk rather than applied identically everywhere.

Why threat analysis supports the plan

威胁分析识别要保护什么，以及什么可能损害它。没有分析，设计者可能选择熟悉的产品，却遗漏重要风险。分析把威胁连接到资产和服务，考虑每个事件的发生可能性，并估计可能损害。这些结果支持优先级判断。发生可能性高且后果严重的威胁需要适当回应；较低优先级威胁可能采用不同处理程度。目的是在有限资源下，把风险降至可接受水平，同时支持组织主要目标。

起句提示：Threat analysis identifies what must be protected and what could harm it.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Threat analysis identifies what must be protected and what could harm it. Without this analysis, a designer may choose familiar products while leaving important risks untreated. The analysis connects threats to assets and services, considers the likelihood of each event and estimates the possible damage. These findings support priorities. A threat with both high likelihood and serious consequences needs a suitable response. A lower-priority threat may receive a different level of treatment. The purpose is to reduce risk to an acceptable level while using limited resources in a way that supports the organization's main goals.

From analysis to action

分析结果也指导政策、机制和责任。例如，未授权访问风险可以引出身份验证和访问规则，物理损害风险则可能需要受保护的机房与恢复安排。这是针对不同问题的不同回应。计划应说明由谁落实控制、怎样检查以及事件后怎么办。分析还使取舍变得清楚：某些控制增加处理、延迟、成本或管理工作。设计者应结合性能与管理要求评估这些影响，而不是假定增加更多控制就总能得到最好的整体设计。

起句提示：The results also guide policies, mechanisms and responsibilities.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The results also guide policies, mechanisms and responsibilities. For example, a risk of unauthorized access may lead to identity checks and access rules, while a risk of physical damage may require protected rooms and recovery arrangements. These are different responses to different problems. The plan should explain who applies each control, how it is checked and what happens after an incident. Analysis also makes trade-offs visible. Some controls add processing, delay, cost or management work. Designers should evaluate these effects with the performance and management requirements instead of assuming that adding more controls always gives the best overall design.

What to consider: assets and threats

第一步是列出资产并理解其重要性，包括用户硬件、服务器、网络设备、软件、服务和数据，还应考虑位置、用户和依赖。支持关键活动的服务可能比次要服务需要更强的可用性保护。接着识别相关威胁，例如未授权访问、披露、拒绝服务、盗窃、数据破坏、恶意软件和物理损害。每项威胁应连接具体资产。只列攻击名称，不能说明什么处于风险中，以及事件如何影响组织。

起句提示：The first task is to list assets and understand their importance.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The first task is to list assets and understand their importance. Assets include user hardware, servers, network devices, software, services and data. Their locations, users and dependencies should be considered. A service that supports an essential activity may deserve stronger availability protection than a less important service. The next task is to identify relevant threats, such as unauthorized access, disclosure, denial of service, theft, data corruption, malicious software and physical damage. Each threat should be connected to specific assets. A list of attack names alone does not show what is at risk or how an incident would affect the organization.

What to consider: likelihood, impact and review

对每项相关威胁，分析应考虑发生可能性及发生后的费用或损害。影响取决于业务目标，可以包括工作中断、数据丢失、恢复工作量和用户损害。现有弱点和控制措施有助于解释评估及额外行动需要。如果没有准确概率，应说明假设，而不是把它们包装成精确事实。分析含有判断，因此应让不同群体参与。组织应定期复查，尤其在用户、服务、设备或连接改变时；旧评估可能不再反映当前需要。

起句提示：For each relevant threat, the analysis should consider likelihood and the cost or damage if it occurs.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

For each relevant threat, the analysis should consider likelihood and the cost or damage if it occurs. Impact depends on business goals and may include interrupted work, lost data, recovery effort and harm to users. Existing weaknesses and controls can help explain the assessment and the additional action required. Where exact probabilities are unavailable, assumptions should be stated clearly rather than presented as precise facts. Different groups should contribute because threat analysis includes judgement. The organization should review the assessment periodically, especially when users, services, equipment or network connections change. An old assessment may no longer describe the current needs.

Example 1: unauthorized access

第一个例子是未授权访问学生记录。资产是记录服务及其数据。有人利用保护不当的账户，可能读取隐私信息或修改成绩；读取影响保密性，改分影响完整性。分析应检查访问如何发生、能接触哪些记录以及后果多严重。计划可结合身份认证、授权、受保护通信和访问监测。认证检查身份，授权限制允许行为。加密可保护传输中数据，但本身不能阻止已有合法访问权限者的滥用。

起句提示：The first example is unauthorized access to student records.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The first example is unauthorized access to student records. The asset is the records service and its data. Someone using an improperly protected account could read private information or change grades. Reading affects confidentiality, while changing grades affects integrity. The analysis should examine how access could occur, which records could be reached and how serious the consequences would be. The plan can combine authentication with authorization, protected communication and access monitoring. Authentication checks identity; authorization limits permitted actions. Encryption can protect data in transit, but it does not by itself stop misuse by someone who already has valid access.

Example 2: denial of service

第二个例子是针对在线教学系统的拒绝服务。资产是教学服务及其所需网络资源。大量不需要的流量可能占用容量或处理资源，使合法用户无法上课，主要威胁可用性。分析应考虑重要教学时段、共享链路和会话中断的后果。回应可包括流量过滤、监测、明确的事件响应与恢复流程。有效程度取决于事件与设计；不能把一台防火墙写成防住所有拒绝服务攻击的保证。

起句提示：The second example is denial of service against an online teaching system.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The second example is denial of service against an online teaching system. The asset is the teaching service and the network resources it needs. A large amount of unwanted traffic could consume capacity or processing resources and prevent legitimate users from joining classes. This mainly threatens availability. The analysis should consider important teaching periods, shared links and the consequences of interrupted sessions. Appropriate responses may include traffic filtering, monitoring and a defined incident response and recovery process. Their effectiveness depends on the event and the design. A single firewall should not be presented as a guarantee against every denial-of-service attack.

Example 3: malicious software

第三个例子是破坏文件或使服务器停用的恶意软件。资产包括存储成果、应用和受影响设备上运行的服务。破坏文件影响完整性，中断服务影响可用性。分析应识别软件如何进入、可能传播多广，以及哪些服务依赖该服务器。计划可包括维护与补丁程序、适当访问限制、监测、备份与恢复安排，并明确责任人。备份支持恢复，但不能防止所有感染，也不自动保证全部服务立即恢复。

起句提示：The third example is malicious software that corrupts files or disables a server.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The third example is malicious software that corrupts files or disables a server. The assets include stored work, applications and the services running on the affected device. Corruption can damage integrity, and interruption can damage availability. The analysis should identify how the software might enter, how widely it could spread and which services depend on that server. The plan can include maintenance and patching procedures, appropriate access restrictions, monitoring and backups with recovery arrangements. These actions should have clear owners. Backups support recovery, but they do not prevent every infection or automatically guarantee that all services can be restored immediately.

Conclusion

安全与隐私支持网络交付有用且可信赖的服务。威胁分析把计划明确连接到资产、可能性、后果和组织优先级。三个例子说明不同威胁需要不同控制组合。一个有依据的设计解释选择，考虑对其他网络功能的影响，并在条件变化时复查风险。这比在尚未理解问题前直接选设备，提供了更扎实的保护依据。

起句提示：Security and privacy support the network's ability to deliver useful and dependable services.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Security and privacy support the network's ability to deliver useful and dependable services. Threat analysis gives the plan a clear connection to assets, likelihood, consequences and organizational priorities. The examples show why different threats need different combinations of controls. A defensible design explains these choices, considers their effects on other network functions and reviews the risks as conditions change. This creates a stronger basis for protection than selecting devices without first understanding the problems.

按题干保留范围与假设。
