回本题深入解析与Moodle定位 →

T125 · 从战略目标到可交付网络设计 · 1027词

题目：Explain how strategic planning, requirements analysis, flow analysis, architecture and design evaluation work together. Use a multi-campus university as an example.

解释完整设计链，并用多校区大学案例说明。

先记回答顺序

  1. 围绕同一个多校园需求建立全文主线
  2. 依次写战略→需求→flow→architecture→评价与实施
  3. 各阶段说明输入、输出及下一步怎样使用
  4. 用traceability连接选择与测试
  5. 结尾保留预算和变更反馈

Introduction

引言把战略网络设计定位为长期支持组织目标的证据链：目标产生需求，需求形成流量与架构，架构引导实际选择，最后用测量检验结果。以大学教学、记录访问和研究为背景，说明后续证据也可能要求回头修改前面的决定。

起句提示：A strategic network design should support what an organization is trying to achieve over time.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A strategic network design should support what an organization is trying to achieve over time. A list of modern devices is not enough. The designer needs a chain of evidence from goals to requirements, traffic, architecture, practical choices and measurable results. Each stage provides information for the next, while later findings may require earlier decisions to change. For a university, the purpose may be reliable teaching, controlled access to records and support for research across campuses. The following process explains how these needs can guide a coherent and defensible solution.

Strategic planning 与 Crouch

战略网络计划把组织长期目标与行动和资源连接起来，先回答使命和当前状态。 Crouch的问题帮助问目标、差距、路径、策略和怎样判断成功。 例如扩校区须先说明教学业务目标，再分解网络措施；策略方向和实际行动都需能被检查。

起句提示：A strategic network plan connects long-term organizational goals with actions and resources.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A strategic network plan connects long-term organizational goals with actions and resources. It asks why the organization exists, how it operates, where it is now, where it wants to be, how it will get there and how it will know that it has arrived. These questions connect mission and values with the current situation, future direction and measures of success. Gap analysis identifies the difference between the present and desired states. Strategy sets the broad direction and deployment of resources. Tactics describe more specific actions used to carry out that direction. For example, a university may aim to support teaching across several campuses. Its plan must connect that aim to network reach, service quality, budgets, staff and milestones. A plan is useful only if people can implement it, monitor progress and revise it when conditions change.

需求分析五步

先从用户、管理层、现网和项目起点收集并列出需求，再制定可测指标。 研究用户和应用行为，包括人数、频率、持续时间和数据量，然后细化容量、时延与RMA。 最后映到具体地点，产出可维护的requirements specification与地图；新高峰数据出现时可回头修订。

起句提示：The process begins by gathering and listing requirements from users, management, the current network and initial project conditions.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The process begins by gathering and listing requirements from users, management, the current network and initial project conditions. The designer then develops service metrics so that needs can be measured and checked. Next, user and application behaviour is characterized. This includes user numbers, session frequency, duration, data sizes and traffic patterns. Requirements are then developed into more precise capacity, delay and RMA needs. Finally, the requirements are mapped to locations to show where services and performance are required. The main output is a maintained requirements specification, supported by measurements and maps. The activities may be revisited when new information appears. For example, measured peak use may show that the original capacity estimate was too low. A clear process helps later architecture and design choices remain connected to evidence rather than assumptions.

Flow 的定义与建立

Flow是具有共同源、目的、方向、信息类型或服务要求的一组网络流量。 先从应用需求确定谁与谁通信、在哪里、发什么、以什么速率及时限发送，可分个别、组合或关键流。 Flow图为后续容量与机制选择提供依据；不能只数总流量而不看方向和位置。

起句提示：A traffic flow is a set of network traffic with common attributes, such as source, destination, information type and direction.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A traffic flow is a set of network traffic with common attributes, such as source, destination, information type and direction. It is an end-to-end entity that can be associated with an application, device or user. To develop flows, identify application requirements and locations, locate data sources and sinks, apply suitable flow models and combine performance requirements into flow specifications. A source produces data, while a sink receives it. The same device may perform both roles. An individual flow represents a single application session. A composite flow combines flows sharing a link, path or network. A critical flow has particular importance or strict requirements. A flow map connects requirements to places in the network. This helps reveal shared paths, bottlenecks and where service mechanisms must be applied.

内部关系与外部关系

内部关系发生在同一组件架构的机制间，例如安全组件中的认证与访问控制。 外部关系发生在不同组件架构之间，例如安全检查增加性能处理量，或管理依赖路由可达。 无论内外都要说明trade-off、dependency或constraint的具体机制与方向，不能只写两个架构名称。

起句提示：Internal relationships exist between mechanisms within one component architecture.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Internal relationships exist between mechanisms within one component architecture. They include trade-offs, dependencies and constraints. For example, within performance architecture, a policy may depend on QoS mechanisms to enforce a service level agreement. External relationships exist between different component architectures. For example, security inspection can affect performance, management traffic depends on addressing and routing, and management access can create security exposure. The classification depends on the functions being compared, not simply whether the devices are in the same building. A reference architecture combines the component architectures and their relationships. It must balance them according to requirements and flow priorities. Optimizing each component alone may give a poor overall result. For instance, extensive inspection may improve one security goal while making a critical application miss its delay requirement.

供应商、设备和服务评价

先从需求和reference architecture设目标、准则及比较规则，再收集候选产品、供应商和服务的证据。 使用同一评分尺度和按优先级确定权重，可比较成本、技术、性能、风险和扩展性；记录信息来源。 首选方案还须通过硬性条件并兼顾支持、长期费用和运维，最低购买价不必然最合适。

起句提示：Begin with the requirements and reference architecture.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Begin with the requirements and reference architecture. Define the evaluation goals, criteria and rules before comparing candidates. Gather and organize documentation, discuss relevant questions and collect evidence about each option. Develop a consistent rating method. Criteria may include cost, technology, performance, risk and scalability. Weights should reflect agreed priorities, and scores should be supported by comparable evidence. Ratings and ranking should be made independently of the vendors being assessed. The preferred option must meet essential requirements and provide a suitable overall balance. Purchase price alone is not enough: support, reputation, backup resources, operating costs and compatibility also matter. Keep the process transparent and document why candidates are accepted or rejected. If the evidence shows that no candidate meets a primary requirement, review the options or requirements rather than hiding that failure inside a favourable average score.

布局、traceability 与成功指标

Layout结合拓扑、技术、设备、关系和关键位置；逻辑图强调通信与服务关系，物理图强调实际摆放和连接。 Traceability把问题与需求连到架构和详细选择，留下客观可辩护的证据链。 Success metrics用容量、时延、RMA以及用户/设备要求检查是否达成目标；没有测量就不能宣称设计成功。

起句提示：Network layout combines topology, technology, equipment, relationships and strategic locations.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Network layout combines topology, technology, equipment, relationships and strategic locations. Logical diagrams emphasize connectivity and service relationships. Physical blueprints describe actual devices, cables, rooms and security locations. Component plans isolate a function, such as security or network management, for clearer explanation. Design traceability links problem statements and requirements to architecture and detailed design decisions. This creates evidence that choices are objective and defensible. It also helps assess the effect of a changed requirement. Success metrics check whether the design achieves its goals. Capacity, delay and RMA are important, but user and device requirements may add other measures. For example, a teaching-service requirement should connect to a traffic policy, suitable devices and a defined performance test. A diagram or equipment list alone does not prove that the final service meets the requirement.

Applied example

远程校区案例先收集人数、课表、应用位置和增长，再分别绘制教学、研究、备份与管理流，保留方向和高峰。确定哪些服务需低时延、哪些可等候后再放置接入、汇聚、安全与管理功能。候选设备按这些需要比较；预算不足要公开冲突并让相关人员确定优先级。蓝图记录方案，正常和相关故障场景的测试检验服务。

起句提示：Consider a university adding a remote campus.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Consider a university adding a remote campus. Begin by identifying student numbers, teaching schedules, application locations and growth. Map teaching, research, backup and management flows separately, including their directions and peaks. Agree which services need low delay and which can wait. The architecture can then place suitable access, aggregation, security and management functions. Candidate links and devices are compared against those needs rather than a preferred brand. If the budget cannot support every requested feature, show the conflict and ask stakeholders to prioritize. The final blueprint and component plans record the solution, while tests check service quality during both normal use and relevant failures.

Evaluation and conclusion

结尾回到可解释和可检查：需求与选择记录必须随新应用和新地点更新。成功既要满足服务与约束，也要在日后运行中可持续支持，安装完成只是其中一步。

起句提示：The result is a design whose choices can be explained and checked.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The result is a design whose choices can be explained and checked. Keep requirements and decision records current, because a new application or site can change the balance. Success means delivering the required service within constraints and supporting it throughout operation, rather than merely completing installation.

不能丢的条件

按题干保留范围与假设。

先口头用中文解释，再手写或打出英文；对照上面的回答顺序，逐点检查。再做题库中的变式，确认能按新问法重组。

T126 · Flow、QoS 与性能保障 · 1044词

题目：Design a performance approach for a network carrying interactive teaching, critical transactions and backups. Discuss metrics, flowspecs, QoS, resource control, SLAs and verification.

为混合流量设计性能方案，解释指标、聚合、机制、承诺和验证。

先记回答顺序

  1. 先区分互动教学、关键交易、备份的服务需求
  2. 建立指标和flowspec
  3. 选逐flow/逐class及资源控制和调度
  4. 写SLA→policy→QoS→measurement闭环
  5. 用拥塞和故障测试验证并说明取舍

Introduction

引言指出三种业务共享资源却有不同需要，因此先定义可测指标和竞争位置，再选择必要且足够的机制。新功能只有解决明确问题并能长期运作才有价值；快速链路或优先标记都不能单独证明端到端服务达标。

起句提示：A performance architecture should give the right service to the right traffic.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A performance architecture should give the right service to the right traffic. Interactive lessons, critical transactions and backups share resources but do not have identical needs. The aim is therefore to define measurable requirements, identify where flows compete and select mechanisms that are necessary and sufficient. Extra features are useful only if they solve an agreed problem and can be operated over time. Capacity planning and service differentiation should be considered together, because neither a fast link nor a priority marking alone proves that an application receives the required end-to-end service.

Capacity、throughput、delay 与 jitter

Capacity是资源可能承载的速率，throughput是特定条件下实际收到的有用速率，二者不能画等号。 Delay是一段数据/响应的时间，单向和往返不同；jitter是delay变化，task completion time是一整个任务所用时间。 数据量除以允许时间得基础平均速率，还须加开销、峰值、并发和端点限制；应用持续时间也不是packet delay。

起句提示：Capacity is the data-carrying capability of a network resource.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Capacity is the data-carrying capability of a network resource. Throughput is the rate of useful transfer actually achieved under particular conditions. A high-capacity link does not mean an application always receives that rate. Delay is the time taken for data or a response to travel through the system. One-way delay and round-trip time describe different paths. Jitter is variation in delay. Task completion time is the time needed to finish an entire application task, such as transferring a file. Data size divided by the allowed transfer time gives a basic average rate requirement after units are converted. Actual planning must also consider overhead, peaks, competing flows and end-system limits. Real-time applications may be sensitive to delay and jitter even when average throughput is adequate. Application duration should not be confused with packet delay.

Flowspec 与聚合规则

Flowspec记录flow和性能要求；one-part只有best-effort，容量可合并；two-part再含predictable。 Predictable合容量，但时延取更严格的较小上限，RMA取更高目标，不能把百分比相加。 Multipart还含guaranteed，每条保证流应单独保留其要求；合并后的总容量不能抹掉逐流承诺。

起句提示：A flow specification lists flows and their performance requirements.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A flow specification lists flows and their performance requirements. A one-part flowspec contains best-effort traffic and combines its capacity requirements. A two-part flowspec includes predictable traffic and may also include best effort. For predictable traffic, capacity requirements are combined, while the most demanding relevant delay and RMA requirements must be preserved. In the course’s aggregation method, this means the minimum permitted delay and maximum required reliability or availability value. It does not mean adding availability percentages. A multipart flowspec also includes guaranteed flows. Each guaranteed flow keeps its own requirements rather than losing them inside a single average. This is important because an acceptable aggregate may hide failure to meet one flow’s commitment. Aggregation must also respect shared paths, direction and timing. Opposite-direction or non-overlapping traffic should not be combined without stating the assumptions.

IntServ、RSVP 与 DiffServ

IntServ给单条flow沿路径提供细粒度待遇，路由器需保留状态；RSVP负责请求/维持预留的信令，不传应用数据。 DiffServ把流量分成类、靠packet标记识别并逐跳处理，常见BE、AF、EF，比逐条预留更易扩展。 逐流控制和规模/运维复杂度要取舍；标记本身不保证端到端，途经设备和域需有一致政策及容量。

起句提示：IntServ provides support for individual flows along an end-to-end path.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

IntServ provides support for individual flows along an end-to-end path. Resource reservation can give a flow specific treatment, but routers must maintain relevant state. RSVP is a signalling protocol used to request and maintain reservations; it does not itself carry the application data. DiffServ groups traffic into classes and applies per-hop treatment to those classes. Packet markings help devices identify the intended class. The course includes best effort, assured forwarding and expedited forwarding. Class-based handling is generally more scalable than maintaining a reservation for every flow. The approaches trade detailed individual control against scalability and operational complexity. A marking alone does not guarantee end-to-end performance. Devices and domains along the path need appropriate policies, capacity and support. Select the approach according to flow requirements and the size of the network.

Resource control 与 traffic conditioning

Resource control决定有限资源分给谁：prioritization排先后，admission control在资源不足时可拒绝新flow。 Traffic conditioning先分类、计量速率/突发，再整形等待或丢弃超标流量。 Shaping平滑流量但增加排队和缓冲需求；dropping保护其他流却损害被丢流；要按应用容忍度选择。

起句提示：Resource control determines how limited network resources are used.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Resource control determines how limited network resources are used. Prioritization ranks traffic according to importance or urgency. Admission control may refuse a new flow when accepting it would prevent existing commitments from being met. Traffic conditioning includes classification, metering, shaping and dropping. Classification identifies the traffic class. Metering measures rates or bursts and compares them with a profile, such as an SLA. Shaping delays packets to make a flow fit an allowed profile. Dropping discards selected packets. These mechanisms have different effects. Shaping can smooth traffic but adds waiting time and needs buffers. Dropping may protect other traffic but harms the discarded flow. The designer should choose behaviour according to application tolerance and policy. For example, delaying a flexible backup may be acceptable, while the same delay could make an interactive application unusable.

Queue 与 scheduling

Queue存等待的packet，scheduling决定下一包/队列何时被服务，两者功能不同。 FIFO按到达顺序；strict priority可减高类时延却可能饿死低类；round robin轮流；WFQ按权重分配。 RED/WRED等管理策略可在缓冲满前提前丢部分包，以控制拥塞；选法应跟随服务目标与公平要求。

起句提示：Queuing stores packets while they wait for processing or transmission.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Queuing stores packets while they wait for processing or transmission. Scheduling decides which waiting packet or queue is served next. These are related functions, but they are not identical. FIFO serves packets in arrival order and is simple. Strict priority serves higher-priority traffic first, which can reduce delay for that class but may leave lower-priority traffic waiting under sustained load. Round robin cycles through queues and serves each in turn. Weighted fair queuing assigns different shares according to configured weights. Queue management, including RED or WRED, can discard packets before a buffer becomes completely full to help control congestion. The choice should reflect service priorities and fairness. A larger buffer may absorb a burst but can also increase delay. Test behaviour under mixed traffic rather than assuming one queue policy meets every requirement.

SLA、policy 与 QoS 的闭环

SLA写服务目标和提供方责任，可约定速率、突发、上下行、时延与可用性等条件。 Policy把组织目标写成资源、访问和优先级规则，QoS机制在设备上执行相关待遇。 如教学流有约定时延与容量，配置后还要沿路径测量，比较SLA；未达标就回查政策、资源和设备。

起句提示：A service level agreement, or SLA, records expected service and the provider’s responsibilities.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A service level agreement, or SLA, records expected service and the provider’s responsibilities. Performance elements may include data rate, burst tolerance, upstream and downstream service, delay and RMA. A useful SLA also needs clear conditions and measurement methods. Policies connect the organization’s management goals with rules about resources, access and service priorities. QoS mechanisms enforce relevant treatment at network devices. Monitoring then checks whether the delivered service matches the commitment and provides feedback for adjustment. For example, a teaching service may have an agreed delay target and capacity level. A policy gives it suitable priority, devices implement that policy, and measurements verify the result. An SLA document alone does not reserve resources. Equally, enabling QoS without a clear goal does not show that users are receiving the service they need.

Applied example

案例给交互教学定义时延和丢失目标，给关键事务保护可用性，给备份确定容量与完成窗口。测量高峰重叠，在共享瓶颈处按类调度；时间灵活的备份可整形或移到低峰，更强承诺可能需要准入控制。测试应同时看受保护业务和较低优先级业务。若瓶颈是服务器或外部链路，局部队列调整仍不足。

起句提示：For the university example, give interactive teaching a defined delay and loss target, protect critical transaction availability and identify the capacity and completion window for backups.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

For the university example, give interactive teaching a defined delay and loss target, protect critical transaction availability and identify the capacity and completion window for backups. Measure peak overlap rather than relying only on daily totals. A shared bottleneck may use class-based treatment and a scheduler with suitable shares. Backups can be shaped or moved to a quieter period when their timing is flexible. Admission control may be needed before accepting stronger commitments. Test whether protected traffic meets its target while lower-priority traffic still receives an acceptable service. If a server or external link is the real bottleneck, local queue changes alone will not solve the problem.

Evaluation and conclusion

结尾要求在服务端点和关键链路验证，记录负载、方向与观测周期，比较SLA后调整资源、规则或预期。这把需求、机制与证据连接起来，同时控制与组织需求相称的成本和复杂度。

起句提示：Verify performance at the service endpoints as well as important links.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Verify performance at the service endpoints as well as important links. Record the load, direction and measurement period. Compare the result with the SLA and adjust capacity, rules or expectations when necessary. This closes the loop from requirements to mechanisms and evidence, while keeping the cost and complexity proportionate to the organization’s needs.

不能丢的条件

按题干保留范围与假设。

闭卷验收

先口头用中文解释，再手写或打出英文；对照上面的回答顺序，逐点检查。再做题库中的变式，确认能按新问法重组。

回本题深入解析与Moodle定位 →

T127 · Addressing 与 Routing 的架构选择 · 1028词

题目：Discuss how an addressing and routing architecture should support a growing multi-site network. Explain addressing, subnetting, boundaries, routing choices and trade-offs.

讨论多站点地址与路由方案，包含子网、边界、协议及取舍。

先记回答顺序

  1. 从站点、增长、可达性和故障目标出发
  2. 写地址规划、CIDR和subnetting验证
  3. 划AS/内部边界
  4. 选内部与跨域路由并比较收敛和开销
  5. 用增长和故障场景检验

Introduction

引言说明地址不是买完设备后随便加的标签，它组织用户、应用和设备之间的连接。位置、流量关系、增长与服务需求应先决定地址计划；原分配块、子网数、管理边界及恢复预期必须明确，也要考虑现网和人员能力。

起句提示：Addressing and routing provide structured connectivity between users, applications and devices.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Addressing and routing provide structured connectivity between users, applications and devices. Their design should follow the organization’s locations, traffic relationships, growth and service requirements. Addresses are not simply labels added after buying equipment. A coherent plan can make routing, aggregation, management and security easier, while a poor plan can create unnecessary complexity. The designer should state the original allocations, subnet requirements, administrative boundaries and recovery expectations before selecting detailed routing mechanisms. Existing systems and the ability of staff to operate the result must also be considered.

MAC、IP、IPv4 与 IPv6

MAC用于以太网等本地链路的frame交付；IP是跨网络可路由的逻辑地址。 IPv4长32bit，IPv6长128bit，地址空间和表示方式不同；它们同属IP层地址。 跨router时本地frame/MAC会更新，通常最终IP目的保持，故两类地址各有职责。

起句提示：A MAC address is used for data-link communication, such as delivery of Ethernet frames on a local link.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A MAC address is used for data-link communication, such as delivery of Ethernet frames on a local link. An IP address is a logical network-layer address used to identify an interface and support delivery between networks. IPv4 addresses contain 32 bits, commonly written as four decimal octets. Each octet contains eight bits. IPv6 addresses contain 128 bits and are commonly written in hexadecimal groups. IPv6 therefore provides a much larger address space. Addressing must be planned together with routing, location and network structure. The address plan affects aggregation, management and security rules. For example, a computer sending to a remote network normally delivers the local frame to a gateway while the IP packet identifies the final remote destination. Knowing the roles of both addresses helps explain communication across multiple links.

Classful、CIDR 与 prefix

Classful旧方式按A/B/C给固定网络边界，不灵活。 CIDR明确写/prefix length；IPv4前p位是网络，余下32−p位可用于地址编号，mask左边连续为1。 计算时不要只凭地址第一组数字猜mask；例如/27需用题给前缀判断边界。

起句提示：Classful addressing used address classes with predetermined network boundaries.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Classful addressing used address classes with predetermined network boundaries. The familiar A, B and C classes used /8, /16 and /24 boundaries. This was inflexible and could waste address space or create blocks too small for users’ needs. Classless Interdomain Routing, or CIDR, uses an explicit prefix length. In IPv4, /p means that p of the 32 bits form the network prefix and the remaining bits form the host portion. The boundary can fall inside an octet. The subnet mask has ones for the prefix bits and zeros for host bits. For example, /26 corresponds to 255.255.255.192 and leaves six host bits. The address alone is not enough to determine its subnet: the prefix is essential. Do not infer a modern subnet size only from the first octet’s historical class.

Subnetting 通用步骤

先写原网络和prefix，以及要多少子网、每个多少普通主机。 等长子网按借位得到新prefix、mask、子网数与每块地址数；VLSM则按不同需求分大小。 列出边界、network/broadcast/可用范围，核对不重叠、都在原块且数量满足要求。

起句提示：Start with the original network and prefix, then state the required subnet count and usable hosts.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Start with the original network and prefix, then state the required subnet count and usable hosts. For equal-sized subnets, choose enough borrowed bits b so that 2 to the power b meets the subnet requirement. The new prefix is the original prefix plus b. The remaining host bits h equal 32 minus the new prefix. Each ordinary subnet has 2 to the power h addresses and normally two fewer usable host addresses because network and broadcast addresses are reserved. Find the subnet boundaries from the new prefix. For each subnet, identify its network address, broadcast address, first usable address and last usable address. Finally, check that the blocks do not overlap, stay within the original allocation and meet the requirements. State any special assumptions instead of applying the ordinary usable-host rule blindly.

Routing boundaries 与 AS

AS是共同路由政策管理下的网络集合；IGP处理AS内部可达，EGP/BGP处理跨AS信息。 边界可能是机构、功能或技术管理边界；内部和外部需要不同政策与规模考虑。 不同协议可协作，判断先看AS归属，不凭物理距离或名称猜用途。

起句提示：An autonomous system, or AS, is a group of IP prefixes under a common administrative routing policy.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

An autonomous system, or AS, is a group of IP prefixes under a common administrative routing policy. An interior gateway protocol, or IGP, distributes routing information inside an AS. RIP and OSPF are examples. BGP is used for routing policy and reachability between autonomous systems, although it also has an internal form. Routing boundaries divide a network physically or logically according to requirements. Physical examples include interfaces, isolation LANs and DMZs. Logical examples include functional areas, workgroups and administrative domains. The course describes boundaries between autonomous systems as hard boundaries and internal divisions as soft boundaries. Functional areas can group similar work, while workgroups may share location or applications. Boundaries help organize hierarchy, management and security. Their placement should follow requirements and flows rather than being chosen only because a convenient device is available.

Static 与 dynamic routing

Static route由人明确配置，协议开销小且容易理解，但变化时须人工调整。 Dynamic routing交换信息并随变化改路径，但带来协议流量、收敛时间和管理要求。 小而稳定的网络与多路径、频繁变化的网络需求不同；看规模、故障恢复与团队技能做取舍。

起句提示：Static routes are configured explicitly.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Static routes are configured explicitly. They avoid the control traffic and processing needed to exchange dynamic routing updates. They can be useful for a small, stable or stub network with a simple exit. Dynamic routing protocols exchange reachability information and adapt routes when topology changes. This supports larger or more interconnected networks, but it uses bandwidth, processor time and memory. The designer must also consider convergence, stability and staff skills. A static route does not automatically change simply because a better path appears. Maintaining many static routes can become difficult and error-prone. Conversely, adding a complex dynamic protocol to a simple network can create unnecessary work. Select the simplest approach that meets the actual resilience, growth and policy requirements. Re-evaluate it as hierarchy and interconnectivity increase.

RIP、OSPF 与 BGP 选择

RIP是按hop count的distance-vector IGP，可用跳数上限15，简单但规模和收敛受限。 OSPF是link-state IGP，支持区域和较大层级网络；BGP是跨AS、重政策的path-vector协议。 评价收敛、开销、内存CPU、稳定、扩展、互通和团队能力；内部选路与域间选路不是同一任务。

起句提示：RIP is a distance-vector IGP that uses hop count.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

RIP is a distance-vector IGP that uses hop count. Its maximum usable hop count is 15, and it can be slow to converge. Its simplicity suits limited networks but restricts scalability. OSPF is a link-state IGP. It supports areas and is better suited to larger hierarchical networks, although operation is more complex. BGP is a path-vector protocol that supports policy-based routing between autonomous systems. It is important for inter-domain connectivity. Protocol evaluation should consider convergence time, bandwidth overhead, processor and memory use, stability, scalability, hierarchy and interconnectivity. The choice should also fit service requirements and management skills. A network requiring fast recovery and many alternative paths places different demands on routing from a small stub network. No protocol is best for every role; internal routing and inter-domain routing solve different problems.

Applied example

案例给各校区及功能分配互不重叠且留有增长空间的块，私网使用与外部连接/NAT分别规划。教学、行政及公开服务需要相应边界；计算时同时满足主机数和子网数。单出口稳定地点可用静态或默认路由，多路径内部网可能适合OSPF，域间政策需要可能适合BGP。最后验证可达、恢复和访问限制。

起句提示：For example, assign a university’s sites and functions suitable non-overlapping address blocks, leaving realistic room for growth.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

For example, assign a university’s sites and functions suitable non-overlapping address blocks, leaving realistic room for growth. Private IPv4 addresses can be used internally, but external connectivity and translation must be planned separately. Identify where teaching, administration and public services require controlled boundaries. Calculate subnet sizes from both host counts and subnet counts, then verify network and broadcast boundaries. A small stable site with one exit may use a simple default or static route. A large internal network with multiple paths may justify OSPF, while an inter-AS policy requirement may justify BGP. Do not deploy every protocol merely because it is available. Test reachability, failure recovery and required access restrictions.

Evaluation and conclusion

结尾将地址组织、路由政策与管理可见性连接，记录每个协议和边界的理由。规模、互联和服务需求变化时复评，在连接与恢复要求和运维成本之间保持可解释的平衡。

起句提示：The final architecture should connect address organization with routing policy and management visibility.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The final architecture should connect address organization with routing policy and management visibility. Record why each protocol or boundary is needed. Review decisions as hierarchy, interconnectivity and service needs change. A successful plan meets connectivity and resilience requirements while keeping operational cost and complexity under control.

T128 · 可扩展且可恢复的 Network management · 1017词

题目：Propose a network management architecture for a multi-campus network. Discuss management functions, information, monitoring, traffic load, organization and recovery access.

为多校区网络提出管理架构，讨论采集、流量、组织和故障访问。

先记回答顺序

  1. 先写服务目标与FCAPS职责
  2. 设计manager/agent/MIB及数据流
  3. 组合event与trend，估算polling和storage
  4. 选择集中/分布/层级并说明通信路径
  5. 补恢复访问、安全和管理服务验证

Introduction

引言说明已部署网络仍需持续观察、控制和支持。管理设计要确定对象、采集位置、信息传输路径及人员响应方式。多校区既需要共同服务视图，也需要本地知识和恢复能力；采集本身消耗流量、计算、存储和权限。

起句提示：A deployed network needs continuing observation, control and support.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A deployed network needs continuing observation, control and support. Network management should connect service goals to reliable information and appropriate actions. Its design must identify what is managed, where information is collected, how it reaches management systems and how staff respond. A multi-campus network also needs to balance a common service view with local knowledge and resilience. Management is not free: its traffic, processing, storage and access rights can affect the network that it is intended to support.

管理层级与 FCAPS

网络管理从business、service、network、element group到individual element逐层细化，分别看预算目标、交付服务、整体网络、设备群和单设备。 FCAPS是fault、configuration、accounting、performance、security五类管理工作，关注故障、设置、用量、性能和安全。 管理层级与FCAPS可结合：教学可靠的业务目标要变成服务目标，再落实成网络与设备测量和行动。

起句提示：The course views network management as several levels.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The course views network management as several levels. Business management handles budgets, resources and planning. Service management concerns delivered services, such as access bandwidth and application delivery. Network management covers the network as a whole. Element management handles groups of similar devices, while network-element management concerns an individual device. FCAPS describes five management areas: fault, configuration, accounting, performance and security management. These include detecting faults, controlling settings, tracking usage, measuring service and managing protection. The levels and functions connect high-level goals with concrete device parameters. For example, a business commitment to reliable teaching leads to a service target, network monitoring and configuration, and specific interface or device actions. Management must also transport and store the information needed to make those decisions, so its own traffic and infrastructure need planning.

SNMP、agent 与 MIB

SNMP管理系统与设备上的agent通信，读取/设置管理信息；agent向管理方呈现设备对象。 MIB定义对象名称和含义，例如接口计数器；它不是所有历史数据的存储库。 Manager可主动poll，设备可用trap主动报事件；counter要用两次差值和时间间隔才能推速率。

起句提示：SNMP supports the collection and configuration of management information from network devices.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

SNMP supports the collection and configuration of management information from network devices. A management system communicates with an agent on a managed device. The agent provides access to managed objects and may send event notifications. A Management Information Base, or MIB, defines the managed objects and their meaning. It is not the same as a historical database of every collected measurement. For example, MIB-II includes interface counters such as received and transmitted octets and error counts. Managers can poll for values, while traps provide unsolicited event notification. Counter values often need processing: a rate normally comes from the change in a counter over a known interval, not from the total counter alone. SNMPv3 adds security capabilities, but settings and access restrictions still need to be planned. Choose objects that support actual service and operational needs.

Monitoring、instrumentation、configuration

Monitoring收集、分析、展示和归档网络表现，可观察事件、趋势、容量或服务。 Instrumentation提供取得信息的工具和接入，如SNMP、counter、探针、抓包、ping或traceroute。 Configuration改变实际运行参数，可依据监测结果调整；能观察不代表能配置，改完要再监测。

起句提示：Monitoring obtains and processes information about network behaviour.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Monitoring obtains and processes information about network behaviour. It includes collection, event or trend analysis, display and archiving. Measurements may concern an element, link, network or end-to-end path. Instrumentation provides the tools and access needed to obtain that information. Examples include SNMP access, counters, probes, packet capture, ping and traceroute. Accuracy and dependability matter, so measurements from different locations may need comparison. Configuration changes operational parameters through direct access, remote access or configuration files. It can implement a response to information found by monitoring. For example, instrumentation supplies interface data, monitoring detects sustained congestion, and configuration changes an appropriate queue policy. These mechanisms form a practical management loop. Observing a problem does not itself fix it, and a successful connectivity probe does not establish every aspect of service quality.

Event notification 与 trend analysis

Event notification针对故障或越阈值等短期事件，短暂问题可能需要较密采样或主动告警。 Trend analysis用持续一致的数据建基线，看利用率、时延和可用性的长期变化，支持容量规划。 日均值可能藏短故障，零碎警报也看不出增长；轮询越密越及时但流量与处理成本越高。

起句提示：Event notification identifies changes that need attention, such as failures, threshold crossings or important informational events.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Event notification identifies changes that need attention, such as failures, threshold crossings or important informational events. Short-lived problems may require frequent collection and prompt alarms, logs or displays. Trend analysis studies longer-term behaviour. Continuous and consistent measurements establish a baseline and reveal changes in utilization, delay or availability. This supports planning before service becomes unacceptable. The methods use different time scales. A daily average may hide a brief outage, while a stream of isolated alarms may not show gradual growth. Shorter polling intervals improve time resolution but create more traffic, processing and stored data. Longer intervals reduce overhead but may miss short events. A useful design combines event and trend information and records timestamps, units and context so that measurements can be interpreted correctly.

Management traffic scaling

先数设备、接口、参数、一次值及协议开销和轮询周期；每轮bit数除周期得平均管理带宽。 Storage另算保存的值、metadata和保留时长，链路协议开销不一定全存入数据库；同步轮询还有峰值。 课件2–5%与超过10%复查是LAN经验值，不是通用标准；可减不必要参数、调整频率或分布采集。

起句提示：Estimate the number of devices, interfaces per device, parameters per interface and polling frequency.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Estimate the number of devices, interfaces per device, parameters per interface and polling frequency. Multiply the number of measurements by the bytes generated per measurement, including relevant protocol overhead, to estimate traffic per polling cycle. Divide by the interval to obtain an average rate. Storage is a separate calculation. It depends on which values and metadata are kept, their size and the retention period. Network protocol overhead is not automatically stored with every value. Polling bursts may also create higher short-term load than the average suggests. The lecture gives LAN planning guidelines of roughly 2–5% management load and suggests review when it exceeds 10%. These are course heuristics, not universal limits. Reduce unnecessary parameters or polling frequency, distribute collection and use suitable aggregation. Critical management information still needs sufficient resources and timely delivery.

Centralized、distributed、hierarchical management

Centralized在主要系统汇聚管理和数据，易统一但可能集中流量和故障风险。 Distributed在多个位置处理本地信息，利于规模与本地响应，但协调和一致性更复杂；hierarchical分层汇总。 按规模、地点、可靠、人员与成本选择；多校区可本地采集、中心总览，故障时保留本地能力。

起句提示：Centralized management collects control and information at a main management system.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Centralized management collects control and information at a main management system. It can simplify administration and provide a common view, but it can concentrate traffic and create a significant failure point unless protected. Distributed management places systems across different domains or locations. Local processing can improve scalability and reduce dependence on a single site, but coordination and consistent policy become harder. Hierarchical management separates responsibilities across levels, allowing local collection and higher-level summaries or control. Choose the architecture according to network size, geography, reliability, staffing and cost. For example, a multi-campus network may benefit from local collection with a central service view. Distribution does not automatically remove every failure risk, and centralization does not forbid redundancy. State where information is collected, stored and acted on, and how those management systems remain reachable.

Applied example

案例由本地采集器观察校区链路和设备，高层系统整合服务信息。短故障需及时检测，增长规划需长期记录；凭据和配置权需保护。In-band可能随业务路径失效，out-of-band只有自身链路、电源和系统仍可用才提供恢复访问。对照多个测点、保存时间戳和对象定义，可减少counter解释错误引发的误操作。

起句提示：For a university, local collectors can monitor campus links and important devices while higher-level systems combine service information.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

For a university, local collectors can monitor campus links and important devices while higher-level systems combine service information. Use frequent event detection where brief failures matter and longer-term records for growth planning. Protect management credentials and restrict configuration rights. In-band management uses the production network, so it may become unreachable when that path fails. An independent out-of-band path can provide recovery access, but only if its own links, power and systems remain available. It also requires security and maintenance. Compare measurements from different points and keep timestamps and object definitions so that a counter error does not trigger a wrong operational decision.

Evaluation and conclusion

结尾要求验证管理服务本身：告警是否有用、数据是否准确、开销是否合理、恢复路径是否可达、员工能否执行流程。上层系统坏时尽量保留有用的本地操作，并用明确职责和故障测试支持设计选择。

起句提示：Evaluate the management service itself.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Evaluate the management service itself. Check alarm usefulness, data accuracy, polling overhead, recovery access and the ability of staff to carry out procedures. Preserve useful local operation if a higher-level system fails. The preferred design is the one that provides dependable control and evidence at a manageable cost, with clear responsibilities and tested failure behaviour.

按题干保留范围与假设。

T129 · Reference architecture 与模型组合 · 1039词

题目：Explain the architectural models discussed in class and how component interactions should be balanced in a reference architecture. Illustrate with a campus network.

说明全部模型、参考架构的组合方式与组件取舍。

先记回答顺序

  1. 列齐topological两类、flow-based四类、functional三类
  2. 说明这些视角怎样组合reference architecture
  3. 区分并解释组件内部和外部关系
  4. 用校园流量把安全、管理、性能、路由联系起来
  5. 以需求、traceability和metrics评价平衡

Introduction

引言解释多个模型的必要性：地理、流量关系和服务功能描述同一网络的不同侧面。Reference architecture把这些视角与由需求和flow选定的组件架构结合，重点是整体协调及机制之间的帮助与限制。

起句提示：A network architecture needs several views because geography, traffic relationships and service functions describe different aspects of the same system.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A network architecture needs several views because geography, traffic relationships and service functions describe different aspects of the same system. A reference architecture combines these views with the component architectures selected from requirements and flow analysis. Its purpose is to make the complete network coherent. The designer should explain not only what functions are present but also how their mechanisms help or constrain one another. The balance depends on the organization’s priorities, rather than on maximizing every individual feature in isolation.

课堂讨论的全部架构模型

Topological模型按地理范围或access/distribution/core功能层次组织网络。 Flow模型包括peer-to-peer、client-server、hierarchical client-server和distributed computing，描述通信参与者和方向。 Functional模型按服务或访问组织，如service-provider、intranet/extranet和end-to-end视角。 多种模型可一起使用：先有拓扑骨架，再叠加流量和功能，形成符合需求的reference architecture。

起句提示：The lectures discuss three groups of architectural models.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The lectures discuss three groups of architectural models. Topological models include LAN/MAN/WAN, which organizes the network by geographical scale, and access/distribution/core, which organizes it by functional layers. Flow-based models describe communication patterns. Peer-to-peer places communicating entities at a similar level. Client-server focuses on communication between clients and servers. Hierarchical client-server adds server tiers and communication between them. Distributed computing describes cooperating computing resources and their data exchanges. Functional models organize the architecture around services or access requirements. The service-provider model focuses on provider functions such as service delivery, privacy and security. The intranet/extranet model separates access according to internal and permitted external use. The end-to-end model considers all components along a complete traffic path. These views can be combined. A designer can start with a topological model and add flow-based and functional views to form a reference architecture that meets the requirements. They are complementary views rather than a single universal solution.

Reference architecture 的形成

Reference architecture整合各组件架构及其关系，让网络功能在总图中协调。 Topological model说明连接与层次，flow model说明谁与谁通信，functional model说明服务/访问功能。 把这些视角组合，再用需求验证，才可指导后续设备与位置选择；一张拓扑图不足以代替完整架构。

起句提示：A reference architecture describes the complete network architecture.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A reference architecture describes the complete network architecture. It combines the selected component architectures, their mechanisms and the internal and external relationships that affect the whole system. The course suggests starting with a topological model, such as LAN/MAN/WAN or access/distribution/core. Flow-based models then describe important communication relationships. Functional models add perspectives such as service delivery, intranet/extranet separation or complete end-to-end paths. These models are complementary views. For example, a campus can use an access/distribution/core structure, support client-server flows and provide controlled extranet access for partners. The designer then checks how routing, management, performance and security work together within that structure. Requirements and flow priorities determine the balance. A reference architecture is therefore more than a topology drawing or a collection of separate product choices.

内部关系与外部关系

内部关系发生在同一组件架构的机制间，例如安全组件中的认证与访问控制。 外部关系发生在不同组件架构之间，例如安全检查增加性能处理量，或管理依赖路由可达。 无论内外都要说明trade-off、dependency或constraint的具体机制与方向，不能只写两个架构名称。

起句提示：Internal relationships exist between mechanisms within one component architecture.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Internal relationships exist between mechanisms within one component architecture. They include trade-offs, dependencies and constraints. For example, within performance architecture, a policy may depend on QoS mechanisms to enforce a service level agreement. External relationships exist between different component architectures. For example, security inspection can affect performance, management traffic depends on addressing and routing, and management access can create security exposure. The classification depends on the functions being compared, not simply whether the devices are in the same building. A reference architecture combines the component architectures and their relationships. It must balance them according to requirements and flow priorities. Optimizing each component alone may give a poor overall result. For instance, extensive inspection may improve one security goal while making a critical application miss its delay requirement.

安全架构的 trade-offs

安全机制之间有依赖：加密要密钥管理，按地址过滤要准确的地址资料。 安全还影响性能、管理和地址：检查与加密可增加延迟，规则可能挡监控，NAT可使日志解释困难。 按优先级选择位置与力度，测量真实效果并记录剩余风险，而不是假定机制越多越好。

起句提示：Within security architecture, mechanisms can depend on or constrain one another.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Within security architecture, mechanisms can depend on or constrain one another. Encryption requires key management, while address-based rules depend on accurate addressing information. Combining mechanisms therefore needs more than selecting each one separately. Security also interacts with other component architectures. Inspection and encryption can add processing work and delay. Security rules can block management traffic, even though monitoring and configuration are needed to verify protection. Dynamic addressing and NAT can make address-based access rules and logs harder to interpret. A designer should compare these effects with the priorities identified during analysis. Selective protection and well-placed zones can protect important assets while controlling unnecessary overhead. Measure the resulting service rather than assuming every security mechanism has the same cost. Document the remaining risks and review the balance when applications or threats change.

SLA、policy 与 QoS 的闭环

SLA写服务目标和提供方责任，可约定速率、突发、上下行、时延与可用性等条件。 Policy把组织目标写成资源、访问和优先级规则，QoS机制在设备上执行相关待遇。 如教学流有约定时延与容量，配置后还要沿路径测量，比较SLA；未达标就回查政策、资源和设备。

起句提示：A service level agreement, or SLA, records expected service and the provider’s responsibilities.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A service level agreement, or SLA, records expected service and the provider’s responsibilities. Performance elements may include data rate, burst tolerance, upstream and downstream service, delay and RMA. A useful SLA also needs clear conditions and measurement methods. Policies connect the organization’s management goals with rules about resources, access and service priorities. QoS mechanisms enforce relevant treatment at network devices. Monitoring then checks whether the delivered service matches the commitment and provides feedback for adjustment. For example, a teaching service may have an agreed delay target and capacity level. A policy gives it suitable priority, devices implement that policy, and measurements verify the result. An SLA document alone does not reserve resources. Equally, enabling QoS without a clear goal does not show that users are receiving the service they need.

布局、traceability 与成功指标

Layout结合拓扑、技术、设备、关系和关键位置；逻辑图强调通信与服务关系，物理图强调实际摆放和连接。 Traceability把问题与需求连到架构和详细选择，留下客观可辩护的证据链。 Success metrics用容量、时延、RMA以及用户/设备要求检查是否达成目标；没有测量就不能宣称设计成功。

起句提示：Network layout combines topology, technology, equipment, relationships and strategic locations.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Network layout combines topology, technology, equipment, relationships and strategic locations. Logical diagrams emphasize connectivity and service relationships. Physical blueprints describe actual devices, cables, rooms and security locations. Component plans isolate a function, such as security or network management, for clearer explanation. Design traceability links problem statements and requirements to architecture and detailed design decisions. This creates evidence that choices are objective and defensible. It also helps assess the effect of a changed requirement. Success metrics check whether the design achieves its goals. Capacity, delay and RMA are important, but user and device requirements may add other measures. For example, a teaching-service requirement should connect to a traffic policy, suitable devices and a defined performance test. A diagram or equipment list alone does not prove that the final service meets the requirement.

Applied example

校园可同时有三层拓扑、client-server教学、分布式研究及受控伙伴访问。按flow放置安全区、差异化服务和管理访问，再检查加密/检查时延、地址变化对规则的影响及采集对业务资源的竞争。取舍必须保留首要需求；机制达不到承诺时应修订组件方案或明确协商服务承诺并记录理由。

起句提示：A campus may use an access/distribution/core topology, client-server teaching services, distributed research computing and controlled partner access.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

A campus may use an access/distribution/core topology, client-server teaching services, distributed research computing and controlled partner access. These are compatible views, not competing names for one physical layout. Place important mechanisms where the flows require them. For instance, public services may need a separate security zone, interactive teaching may need differentiated treatment, and management needs controlled access across those boundaries. Check whether encryption or inspection adds unacceptable delay, whether address changes confuse access rules and whether management collection competes with important traffic. The chosen compromise should preserve primary requirements. If a mechanism cannot deliver the agreed service, revise the component plan or the service commitment and record the reason.

Evaluation and conclusion

结尾说明reference architecture应指导后续具体选择和测试，并把重要关系追到需求或约束。这样评审者能理解为何选择这个组合，未来变更也能保留整体目标。

起句提示：Use the reference architecture to guide detailed choices and tests.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Use the reference architecture to guide detailed choices and tests. Each important relationship should be traceable to a requirement or constraint. This allows reviewers to understand why the combination was chosen and helps future changes preserve the network’s overall purpose.

不能丢的条件

按题干保留范围与假设。

闭卷验收

先口头用中文解释，再手写或打出英文；对照上面的回答顺序，逐点检查。再做题库中的变式，确认能按新问法重组。

T130 · SDN、SD-WAN、NFV、Automation 与 ML · 1021词

题目：Evaluate how SDN, SD-WAN, NFV, automation and machine learning can support strategic network design. Explain their different roles, benefits, dependencies and limits.

评价新技术的不同职责、组合价值、条件与限制。

先记回答顺序

  1. 先按角色区分SDN、SD-WAN、NFV、automation、ML
  2. 每项写机制→收益→依赖/风险
  3. 用一个校园场景说明怎样配合
  4. 比较数据、controller、资源、兼容和运维条件
  5. 以可验证需求评价而非只追求新技术

Introduction

引言先区分五种技术：SDN改变转发控制，SD-WAN管理广域连接，NFV改变功能交付方式，automation执行生命周期任务，ML利用数据或经验支持预测和决策。它们可以配合，但不可互换，都仍需要架构和验证。

起句提示：Programmable and software-based networking can make service changes easier to coordinate.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Programmable and software-based networking can make service changes easier to coordinate. However, technology should be selected because it meets requirements, not simply because it is new. SDN changes how forwarding is controlled. SD-WAN applies software-based management to wide-area connectivity. NFV changes how network functions are delivered. Automation performs life-cycle tasks, while machine learning uses data or experience to support predictions and decisions. These ideas can work together, but they are not interchangeable and none removes the need for sound architecture and verification.

SDN 三平面

Application plane放网络应用与服务意图，control plane负责政策和规则，data plane执行转发。 一个或多个controller维护基础设施视图、作决策并给转发设备编程，data plane依据规则匹配packet。 例如应用提出教学流优先请求，控制器转为规则，交换机逐包执行；分层使职责清楚，但仍须验证通信与控制可靠性。

起句提示：SDN separates the application, control and data planes.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

SDN separates the application, control and data planes. The application plane contains network services and applications that express requirements and use a view of the network. The control plane is managed by one or more controllers. It maintains information about the infrastructure, makes or implements policy decisions and programs forwarding behaviour. The data plane contains physical or virtual forwarding devices. They process traffic according to rules and report relevant network state. The separation allows control logic and forwarding to evolve more independently. For example, an application can request a traffic policy, the controller translates it into device rules, and switches apply those rules to packets. This supports programmability and coordinated management. A logically centralized view does not require one unprotected physical controller. Controller architecture, communication and failure handling remain important design decisions.

SD-WAN

SD-WAN用软件方法管理相隔较远的WAN连接，如多校区或办公室之间。 它可改善政策一致性、应用对现有路径的使用、云连接与管理效率，也可结合安全功能。 底层链路容量、时延、丢失和可用性仍存在，安全、监测、控制器韧性与成本要检查。

起句提示：SD-WAN applies a software-based management approach to a wide area network.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

SD-WAN applies a software-based management approach to a wide area network. A WAN connects geographically separated networks, such as the LANs of different campuses or offices. SD-WAN can make policy management more consistent and improve how applications use available WAN connectivity. The course highlights better application experience, stronger managed security, optimized cloud connectivity and simpler management. These are potential benefits rather than outcomes guaranteed by the name alone. The design must still consider the capacity, delay, loss and availability of the underlying links. It also needs suitable security policies, monitoring and controller resilience. For example, centrally applying a policy for teaching traffic can simplify operation across campuses, but it cannot remove a physical outage unless an alternative path exists. Select SD-WAN when its capabilities address the organization’s flows and management needs at an acceptable cost.

SDN 与 Big Data 的双向关系

Big Data的五V是量、种类、速度、价值和可信程度，帮助说明数据特点。 SDN的广域视图、可编程控制和动态重配可帮助大量数据的运输与资源协调。 反过来，分析网络数据可帮助流量工程、跨层决策、安全检测与数据中心运行；但数据质量、控制器规模和规则更新仍是限制。

起句提示：Big data is commonly described by volume, variety, velocity, value and veracity.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Big data is commonly described by volume, variety, velocity, value and veracity. These concern how much data exists, its forms and speed, the value obtained and its trustworthiness. SDN can support big-data applications through a global network view, programmable control and dynamic reconfiguration. These help coordinate data delivery, resource use and processing across data centres. Network decisions can be adjusted as application demand changes. The relationship also works in the other direction. Analysis of network data can help SDN with traffic engineering, cross-layer decisions, security detection and data-centre operation. However, useful data does not automatically produce good decisions. The course identifies controller scalability, flow-table management and flexible programming abstractions as open issues. The designer must consider data quality, control overhead and the practical ability to apply decisions in time.

Network automation

Network automation用软件处理配置、管理、测试、部署与运行等重复任务，可结合SDN或其他工具。 自动化能减少手敲错误和不一致，让变化更快、规模更大，但也需要可靠输入和统一政策。 错误脚本也会把同一错误推广到很多设备；应控制权限、先测试、分批实施、监测并能回退。

起句提示：Network automation uses software to perform tasks in the network life cycle, including configuration, management, testing, deployment and operation.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Network automation uses software to perform tasks in the network life cycle, including configuration, management, testing, deployment and operation. The course gives SDN, Ansible, Puppet and Python scripts as examples of approaches or tools. Automation can reduce repetitive manual work, typing errors and inconsistent configuration. It can improve speed, scalability and network-wide policy consistency. It also frees engineers to focus on tasks that need judgement. However, an incorrect automated instruction can repeat the same mistake across many devices. Access control, accurate input and verification are still needed. As a practical extension, testing changes, deploying them in stages and preparing rollback help manage this risk. Automation is broader than SDN: it can be used on traditional devices as well. Its success should be measured by reliable outcomes, not only by how quickly commands are sent.

NFV 与三要素

NFV把防火墙等网络功能作为软件运行在共享通用基础设施，而非每处都要一台专用盒子。 NFVI提供计算、存储、网络和虚拟化资源；VNF实现具体功能；MANO负责部署、生命周期与资源编排。 软件化增加放置与扩缩灵活性，但共享竞争、性能、隔离和故障仍须测试。

起句提示：Network Function Virtualization, or NFV, implements network functions as software on shared, general-purpose infrastructure.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Network Function Virtualization, or NFV, implements network functions as software on shared, general-purpose infrastructure. For example, a firewall or load-balancing function can run as a software instance instead of requiring a separate dedicated appliance for every deployment. The course identifies three main elements. NFV infrastructure, or NFVI, provides the computing, storage, networking and virtualization resources. Virtual network functions, or VNFs, provide the actual network functions. Management and orchestration, or MANO, manages their deployment and life cycle and coordinates resources. Separating functions from dedicated equipment can improve flexibility and allow services to be placed or scaled when needed. However, performance, isolation, shared-resource contention and infrastructure failure still matter. Virtualization changes how functions are delivered; it does not remove the requirement to test service capacity, availability and security.

ML workflow、应用与挑战

ML先准备合适历史数据，建模、评价和优化要反复进行，之后才在新数据上预测并继续监测。 可用于流量/链路预测、入侵检测、路由辅助、QoS和资源分配，任务与数据须匹配。 算力、数据缺乏与隐私、类别不平衡及真实场景测试都会限制结果；旧数据高分不等于新网络可用。

起句提示：An ML workflow starts with suitable historical data.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

An ML workflow starts with suitable historical data. Model building, evaluation and optimization form an iterative process. After evaluation, the model can make predictions or support decisions on new data. As a practical extension, new conditions should be monitored because old patterns may no longer represent the network. Networking applications include predicting traffic volume or link quality, intrusion detection, routing support, QoS improvement and resource allocation. These applications can assist engineers but still require suitable operational controls. The course highlights computational load and the trade-off with accuracy, data availability and privacy, imbalanced datasets and the need for realistic testing. A model trained mostly on normal traffic may perform poorly on rare attacks. Evaluate results against the actual task and consider the consequences of wrong predictions. More complex models are not automatically better for every network.

Applied example

案例以跨校区服务和研究需求变化为起点。SD-WAN协调WAN政策，NFV提供软件防火墙，SDN引导流量经过它；automation一致执行已审核配置，网络数据辅助预测但须先评价再影响关键服务。控制访问、controller与共享底座故障都需考虑；自动化可能放大错误，ML也可能因数据缺失或分布改变而判断失误。

起句提示：Suppose a university needs to extend services across campuses and respond to changing research demand.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Suppose a university needs to extend services across campuses and respond to changing research demand. SD-WAN may help coordinate WAN policies. NFV can provide software firewall functions, while SDN can steer relevant traffic through them. This shows the difference between virtualizing a function and controlling connectivity. Automation can apply a reviewed configuration consistently. Network data can support traffic prediction, but predictions need evaluation before they affect critical services. Preserve secure control access and consider controller or shared-infrastructure failures. A fast automated change can spread a mistake, and a confident ML result can still be wrong when data is incomplete or unlike the training data.

Evaluation and conclusion

结尾按服务结果、运维工作、安全、恢复与成本评价组合。先确定实际任务，再确认组织能支持新增依赖；可编程提供选择，需求分析、测试和管理让选择成为可用服务。

起句提示：Assess the combined system using service outcomes, operational effort, security, resilience and cost.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Assess the combined system using service outcomes, operational effort, security, resilience and cost. Begin with a clear task and verify that the organization can support the new dependencies. Programmability creates options; disciplined requirements, testing and management turn those options into a useful network service.

不能丢的条件

按题干保留范围与假设。

闭卷验收

先口头用中文解释，再手写或打出英文；对照上面的回答顺序，逐点检查。再做题库中的变式，确认能按新问法重组。

T131 · 设计评价、产物、可追溯性与验收 · 1055词

题目：Explain how a designer should select a practical network solution and demonstrate its success. Discuss evaluation criteria, constraints, supportability, deliverables and traceability.

说明怎样选择可实施方案并证明成功。

先记回答顺序

  1. 先区分primary/secondary并列实际约束
  2. 固定评价规则和权重后比较合格候选
  3. 解释supportability与架构要素
  4. 列五类design products
  5. 用需求→架构→选择→metric/test证明成功

Introduction

引言强调设计把分析与架构变成可建、可运行、可评价的方案。它必须满足首要需求与约束，并提供选择对应组织需要的证据，因此评价、布局、文档和测试是一条相连过程。

起句提示：Network design turns analysis and architecture into a solution that can be built, operated and assessed.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Network design turns analysis and architecture into a solution that can be built, operated and assessed. A successful design is more than a shopping list and more than a high score in a comparison table. It must satisfy primary requirements, work within constraints and provide evidence linking decisions to organizational needs. Evaluation, layout, documentation and testing therefore belong to one connected process. The designer should be able to explain why a particular solution was chosen and how users will know that it works.

供应商、设备和服务评价

先从需求和reference architecture设目标、准则及比较规则，再收集候选产品、供应商和服务的证据。 使用同一评分尺度和按优先级确定权重，可比较成本、技术、性能、风险和扩展性；记录信息来源。 首选方案还须通过硬性条件并兼顾支持、长期费用和运维，最低购买价不必然最合适。

起句提示：Begin with the requirements and reference architecture.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Begin with the requirements and reference architecture. Define the evaluation goals, criteria and rules before comparing candidates. Gather and organize documentation, discuss relevant questions and collect evidence about each option. Develop a consistent rating method. Criteria may include cost, technology, performance, risk and scalability. Weights should reflect agreed priorities, and scores should be supported by comparable evidence. Ratings and ranking should be made independently of the vendors being assessed. The preferred option must meet essential requirements and provide a suitable overall balance. Purchase price alone is not enough: support, reputation, backup resources, operating costs and compatibility also matter. Keep the process transparent and document why candidates are accepted or rejected. If the evidence shows that no candidate meets a primary requirement, review the options or requirements rather than hiding that failure inside a favourable average score.

Primary/secondary criteria 与约束

Primary criteria是项目成功必须满足的条件；secondary是可权衡的加分项，冲突时可能让步。 把目标写成短而具体的容量、时延、RMA或可维护准则，便于评价。 选技术还受预算、现网与设施限制；flowspec给服务要求，大网络可分区域评价，不能只按一项速度排名。

起句提示：Primary design criteria are conditions required for project success.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Primary design criteria are conditions required for project success. Secondary criteria are desirable features that may be sacrificed when they conflict with essential goals. This distinction gives the design a clear priority structure. Translate goals into short and specific criteria. A performance goal may require particular capacity, delay or RMA levels. A supportability goal may favour technologies the staff can operate. Compare alternatives in a consistent table and explain the trade-offs. Technology selection is constrained by cost, existing networks and facilities. Flow specifications provide service and capacity requirements. A large network can be divided into manageable sections, with each section evaluated against its own needs and interfaces. The course’s utilization guidelines must be adjusted for burstiness and local conditions. Choosing the cheapest option is not successful if it fails a primary service requirement.

Architectural design 的要素

Architectural design给高层技术、协议、通信能力及网络分段关系，把组织方向连到后续详细设计。 概念方面考虑业务、扩展、可靠、安全、管理、旧系统兼容、地理、资金、风险、性能与简单性。 技术方面写node、link、topology、interface、service、protocol和traffic map；需具体到能指导选择，但不等于最终型号/线缆长度清单。

起句提示：Architectural design sets high-level technologies, protocols, communication capabilities and segment relationships.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Architectural design sets high-level technologies, protocols, communication capabilities and segment relationships. It connects organizational direction with the detailed design that follows. Concept elements include business environment, technology choices, scalability, dependability, security, management and maintainability. Compatibility with legacy systems, limits, flexibility, geography, finances, risk, performance and simplicity also matter. These define the conditions the solution must satisfy. Technology elements describe nodes, links, topology, interfaces, services, protocols and traffic mapping. They explain what kinds of components connect, how services overlay the network and where traffic requirements apply. For example, a multi-campus design needs geographical reach, growth and availability goals as well as suitable link types and protocols. Architectural design should be specific enough to guide choices without being confused with a final list of device models, cable lengths and installation details.

设计过程的五类产物

Network blueprints写设备、连接、线缆、位置及相关物理安全，供实施人员建设。 Component plan写某功能怎样由机制实现，以及内部和外部关系，如安全或管理。 供应商、设备和服务选择记录具体选项，并与准则和架构相符。 Traceability把选择连接需求和架构决策，让审核者知道为什么这样选。 Design metrics写成功如何测量，如容量、时延、可用性；五类合起来比购物清单完整。

起句提示：The design process should produce five main outputs.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

The design process should produce five main outputs. Network blueprints describe the physical arrangement, including device locations, connections, cables and relevant physical security. They help people build the proposed network. A component plan explains how a particular function or service will be implemented. It identifies mechanisms, internal relationships and interactions with other functions, for example security or management. Vendor, equipment and service-provider selections record which suppliers, products and services have been chosen. These choices should meet the design criteria and fit the architecture. Traceability links design decisions to requirements and architectural decisions. It helps reviewers understand why a choice was made and whether it has a clear basis. Finally, design metrics define how success will be measured. Measures may include capacity, delay and availability, as well as relevant user or device requirements. Together, these outputs support implementation, review and later evaluation; a device shopping list alone is not a complete design.

布局、traceability 与成功指标

Layout结合拓扑、技术、设备、关系和关键位置；逻辑图强调通信与服务关系，物理图强调实际摆放和连接。 Traceability把问题与需求连到架构和详细选择，留下客观可辩护的证据链。 Success metrics用容量、时延、RMA以及用户/设备要求检查是否达成目标；没有测量就不能宣称设计成功。

起句提示：Network layout combines topology, technology, equipment, relationships and strategic locations.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Network layout combines topology, technology, equipment, relationships and strategic locations. Logical diagrams emphasize connectivity and service relationships. Physical blueprints describe actual devices, cables, rooms and security locations. Component plans isolate a function, such as security or network management, for clearer explanation. Design traceability links problem statements and requirements to architecture and detailed design decisions. This creates evidence that choices are objective and defensible. It also helps assess the effect of a changed requirement. Success metrics check whether the design achieves its goals. Capacity, delay and RMA are important, but user and device requirements may add other measures. For example, a teaching-service requirement should connect to a traffic policy, suitable devices and a defined performance test. A diagram or equipment list alone does not prove that the final service meets the requirement.

补充性能需求

Operational suitability问客户能否操作设计好的网络，既看技术也看人员技能。 Supportability问交付后能否长期维护，需RMA、培训、程序文档、工具及备件。 Confidence在本课指按需速率、无不可接受错误或丢失地传数据的可信能力。高规格设备若团队不会维护仍可能不合适。

起句提示：Operational suitability asks whether the customer can operate the planned network.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Operational suitability asks whether the customer can operate the planned network. It depends on both the design and the skills of the people who will use and run it. Supportability asks what is needed to maintain effective operation after delivery. The course identifies RMA, workforce and training, procedures and documentation, tools, and spare or repair parts. A system that performs well on installation day may still be hard to support later. Confidence, in this course, concerns the network’s ability to deliver data at the required rate without unacceptable error or loss. The acceptable level depends on the application. These requirements extend the design beyond nominal link speed. For example, a complex device may offer high performance but require training, maintenance procedures and spare parts that the organization cannot currently provide.

Applied example

案例比较采购价较低与支持/恢复更强的方案。先同意必要目标，再设权重；违反强制要求的方案不能靠无关优点抵消。比较部署与持续成本、兼容、负载表现、恢复、技能和增长。蓝图说明物理安排，逻辑图说明服务关系，追溯表连接需求、架构、具体选择和测试；验收使用代表性高峰及相关故障场景。

起句提示：Consider two candidate solutions for a campus upgrade.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

Consider two candidate solutions for a campus upgrade. One has a lower purchase price, while the other provides stronger support and recovery features. Agree the essential service targets before assigning weights. Reject an option that cannot meet a mandatory requirement instead of allowing unrelated strengths to hide the failure. Compare deployment and recurring costs, compatibility, performance under load, recovery, staff skills and expected growth. Then show the selected devices and links in a physical blueprint and explain logical service relationships separately. A traceability table can connect each important requirement to an architecture decision, a product or placement choice and a test. Use representative peak traffic and relevant failure scenarios during acceptance.

Evaluation and conclusion

交付后保留证据和操作流程以支持维护。测量不达标时找出差距，修订设计或经明确协商修改要求。清楚的产物和可重复验证使结果可审查，也帮助后续人员理解原选择理由。

起句提示：After delivery, retain the evidence and procedures needed for continued support.

展开完整英文块，核对漏点

After delivery, retain the evidence and procedures needed for continued support. If measurements do not meet the agreed targets, identify the gap and revise the design or an explicitly negotiated requirement. Clear deliverables and repeatable checks make the outcome reviewable and help future staff maintain the reasons behind the original choices.

不能丢的条件

按题干保留范围与假设。
